Repurposing 3D Generative Model for Autoregressive Layout Generation
Authors: Haoran Feng, Yifan Niu, Zehuan Huang, Yang-Tian Sun, Chunchao Guo, Yuxin Peng, Lu Sheng
First: 2026-04-17T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: https://fenghora.github.io/LaviGen-Page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce LaviGen, a framework that repurposes 3D generative models for 3D layout generation. Unlike previous methods that infer object layouts from textual descriptions, LaviGen operates directly in the native 3D space, formulating layout generation as an autoregressive process that explicitly models geometric relations and physical constraints among objects, producing coherent and physically plausible 3D scenes. To further enhance this process, we propose an adapted 3D diffusion model that integrates scene, object, and instruction information and employs a dual-guidance self-rollout distillation mechanism to improve efficiency and spatial accuracy. Extensive experiments on the LayoutVLM benchmark show LaviGen achieves superior 3D layout generation performance, with 19% higher physical plausibility than the state of the art and 65% faster computation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fenghora/LaviGen.
中文标题/摘要
标题：复用3D生成模型进行自回归布局生成
我们提出了LaviGen框架，该框架复用3D生成模型进行3D布局生成。与以往从文本描述推断物体布局的方法不同，LaviGen直接在原生3D空间中操作，将布局生成建模为自回归过程，显式建模物体间的几何关系和物理约束，生成连贯且物理上合理的3D场景。为进一步提升该过程，我们提出了一种改进的3D扩散模型，整合场景、物体和指令信息，并采用双引导自滚动蒸馏机制以提高效率和空间精度。在LayoutVLM基准上的大量实验表明，LaviGen在3D布局生成方面表现优越，物理合理性比当前最佳方法高19%，计算速度提升65%。代码已公开于https://github.com/fenghora/LaviGen。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically plausible 3D layout generation without relying on textual descriptions, LaviGen repurposes 3D generative models to operate directly in 3D space, treating layout generation as an autoregressive process that explicitly encodes geometric and physical constraints. The method introduces a modified 3D diffusion model that fuses scene, object, and instruction inputs, enhanced by a dual-guidance self-rollout distillation mechanism to boost efficiency and spatial accuracy. Experimental results on LayoutVLM demonstrate LaviGen outperforms existing methods with 19% higher physical plausibility and 65% faster inference speed.
为实现无需文本描述的物理合理3D布局生成，LaviGen将3D生成模型重新应用于3D空间，将布局生成建模为自回归过程，显式建模物体间的几何与物理约束。该方法提出改进的3D扩散模型，融合场景、物体与指令信息，并引入双引导自滚动蒸馏机制以提升空间精度与效率。在LayoutVLM基准测试中，LaviGen表现优于现有方法，物理合理性提升19%，推理速度加快65%。
FineCog-Nav: Integrating Fine-grained Cognitive Modules for Zero-shot Multimodal UAV Navigation
Authors: Dian Shao, Zhengzheng Xu, Peiyang Wang, Like Liu, Yule Wang, Jieqi Shi, Jing Huo
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-17T17:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:59:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
UAV vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate complex 3D environments from an egocentric perspective while following ambiguous multi-step instructions over long horizons. Existing zero-shot methods remain limited, as they often rely on large base models, generic prompts, and loosely coordinated modules. In this work, we propose FineCog-Nav, a top-down framework inspired by human cognition that organizes navigation into fine-grained modules for language processing, perception, attention, memory, imagination, reasoning, and decision-making. Each module is driven by a moderate-sized foundation model with role-specific prompts and structured input-output protocols, enabling effective collaboration and improved interpretability. To support fine-grained evaluation, we construct AerialVLN-Fine, a curated benchmark of 300 trajectories derived from AerialVLN, with sentence-level instruction-trajectory alignment and refined instructions containing explicit visual endpoints and landmark references. Experiments show that FineCog-Nav consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines in instruction adherence, long-horizon planning, and generalization to unseen environments. These results suggest the effectiveness of fine-grained cognitive modularization for zero-shot aerial navigation. Project page: https://smartdianlab.github.io/projects-FineCogNav.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FineCog-Nav：集成细粒度认知模块实现零样本多模态无人机导航
无人机视觉-语言导航（VLN）要求代理从第一人称视角在复杂3D环境中导航，同时遵循长期、模糊的多步指令。现有零样本方法仍受限，常依赖大型基础模型、通用提示和松散协调的模块。本文提出FineCog-Nav，一种受人类认知启发的自上而下框架，将导航细分为语言处理、感知、注意力、记忆、想象、推理和决策等模块。每个模块由中等规模基础模型驱动，结合角色特定提示与结构化输入输出协议，实现高效协作与可解释性提升。为支持细粒度评估，我们构建了AerialVLN-Fine基准，从AerialVLN中精选300条轨迹，实现句子级指令-轨迹对齐，并优化指令以包含明确视觉终点与地标参照。实验表明，FineCog-Nav在指令遵循、长时规划及对未见环境泛化方面持续优于零样本基线。结果表明，细粒度认知模块化对零样本空中导航有效。项目页面：https://smartdianlab.github.io/projects-FineCogNav。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing zero-shot UAV navigation methods that rely on generic prompts and loosely coordinated modules, FineCog-Nav introduces a top-down cognitive framework decomposing navigation into fine-grained modules—language, perception, attention, memory, imagination, reasoning, and decision-making—each powered by a moderate-sized foundation model with role-specific prompts and structured protocols. Evaluated on AerialVLN-Fine, a new benchmark of 300 trajectory-aligned instructions with explicit visual endpoints, the system demonstrates superior performance over baselines in instruction adherence, long-horizon planning, and generalization to unseen environments, highlighting the efficacy of modular cognitive design for zero-shot aerial navigation.
为克服现有零样本无人机导航方法依赖通用提示和松散协作模块的局限性，FineCog-Nav提出一种自上而下的认知框架，将导航细分为语言、感知、注意力、记忆、想象、推理和决策等细粒度模块，每个模块由适配角色提示的中等规模基础模型驱动，并通过结构化输入输出协议协同工作。在新构建的AerialVLN-Fine基准（含300条细粒度轨迹、句级指令-轨迹对齐及明确视觉终点）上，该系统在指令遵循、长时规划和未见环境泛化能力上显著优于基线方法，验证了细粒度认知模块化在零样本空中导航中的有效性。
Differential privacy representation geometry for medical image analysis
Authors: Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh, Marziyeh Mohammadi, Sven Nebelung, Daniel Truhn
First: 2026-03-01T13:30:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:58:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Differential privacy (DP)'s effect in medical imaging is typically evaluated only through end-to-end performance, leaving the mechanism of privacy-induced utility loss unclear. We introduce Differential Privacy Representation Geometry for Medical Imaging (DP-RGMI), a framework that interprets DP as a structured transformation of representation space and decomposes performance degradation into encoder geometry and task-head utilization. Geometry is quantified by representation displacement from initialization and spectral effective dimension, while utilization is measured as the gap between linear-probe and end-to-end utility. Across over 594,000 images from four chest X-ray datasets and multiple pretrained initializations, we show that DP is consistently associated with a utilization gap even when linear separability is largely preserved. At the same time, displacement and spectral dimension exhibit non-monotonic, initialization- and dataset-dependent reshaping, indicating that DP alters representation anisotropy rather than uniformly collapsing features. Correlation analysis reveals that the association between end-to-end performance and utilization is robust across datasets but can vary by initialization, while geometric quantities capture additional prior- and dataset-conditioned variation. These findings position DP-RGMI as a reproducible framework for diagnosing privacy-induced failure modes and informing privacy model selection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于医学图像分析的差分隐私表示几何
差分隐私（DP）在医学成像中的效果通常仅通过端到端性能评估，其隐私导致的效用损失机制不明确。我们提出了医学成像差分隐私表示几何（DP-RGMI）框架，将DP解释为表示空间的结构化变换，并将性能退化分解为编码器几何和任务头利用两部分。几何量通过初始化后的表示位移和谱有效维度量化，利用量则通过线性探针与端到端效用的差距衡量。在四个胸部X光数据集的59万4千多张图像及多种预训练初始化条件下，我们发现即使线性可分性基本保持，DP仍始终与利用差距相关。同时，位移和谱维度表现出非单调、依赖初始化和数据集的重塑，表明DP改变了表示的各向异性而非均匀压缩特征。相关性分析显示，端到端性能与利用之间的关联在不同数据集间稳健，但可能随初始化变化，而几何量捕捉了更多先验和数据集条件下的变异。这些发现使DP-RGMI成为诊断隐私诱导失效模式和指导隐私模型选择的可复现框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how differential privacy (DP) causes utility loss in medical imaging, this study introduces DP-RGMI, a framework that models DP as a geometric transformation in representation space. It decomposes performance degradation into two components: encoder geometry (measured via representation displacement and spectral effective dimension) and task-head utilization (measured as the gap between linear-probe and end-to-end performance). Across 594,000 chest X-ray images and multiple pretrained models, DP consistently introduced a utilization gap even when linear separability was preserved, while geometric metrics showed non-monotonic, initialization- and dataset-dependent reshaping, indicating DP alters feature anisotropy rather than uniform collapse. Correlation analysis confirmed that utilization strongly predicts end-to-end performance across datasets, while geometric measures capture additional dataset- and initialization-specific variations, establishing DP-RGMI as a diagnostic tool for privacy-induced failure modes.
为理解差分隐私（DP）在医学影像中导致性能下降的机制，本研究提出DP-RGMI框架，将DP建模为表示空间中的几何变换，并将其性能损失分解为编码器几何（通过表示位移与谱有效维度衡量）和任务头利用率（通过线性探针与端到端性能差距衡量）。在59.4万张胸部X光图像及多个预训练模型上实验表明，DP即使在保持线性可分性时仍持续引入利用率差距，而几何指标显示表示各向异性在初始化和数据集间呈现非单调重塑。相关性分析表明，利用率在跨数据集上稳健解释端到端性能，但几何特征捕捉了由初始化和数据集条件决定的额外变化。
Modeling Parkinson's Disease Progression Using Longitudinal Voice Biomarkers: A Comparative Study of Statistical and Neural Mixed-Effects Models
Authors: Ran Tong, Lanruo Wang, Tong Wang, Wei Yan
First: 2025-07-26T20:56:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:57:59+00:00
Comments: 20pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting Parkinson's Disease (PD) progression is crucial for personalized treatment, and voice biomarkers offer a promising non-invasive method for tracking symptom severity through telemonitoring. However, analyzing this longitudinal data is challenging due to inherent within-subject correlations, the small sample sizes typical of clinical trials, and complex patient-specific progression patterns. While deep learning offers high theoretical flexibility, its application to small-cohort longitudinal studies remains under-explored compared to traditional statistical methods. This study presents an application of the Neural Mixed Effects (NME) framework to Parkinson's telemonitoring, benchmarking it against Generalized Neural Network Mixed Models (GNMM) and semi-parametric statistical baseline of Generalized Additive Mixed Models (GAMMs). Using the Oxford Parkinson's telemonitoring voice dataset (), we demonstrate that while neural architectures offer flexibility, they are prone to significant overfitting in small-sample regimes. Our results indicate that GAMMs provide the optimal balance, achieving superior predictive accuracy (MSE 6.56) compared to neural baselines (MSE > 90) while maintaining clinical interpretability. We discuss the critical implications of these findings for developing robust, deployable telemonitoring systems where data scarcity is a constraint, highlighting the necessity for larger, diverse datasets for neural model validation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用纵向语音生物标志物建模帕金森病进展：统计与神经混合效应模型的比较研究
预测帕金森病（PD）进展对个性化治疗至关重要，语音生物标志物提供了一种有前景的非侵入性方法，可通过远程监测跟踪症状严重程度。然而，由于受试者内部相关性、临床试验中典型的样本量较小以及患者特异性进展模式复杂，分析此类纵向数据具有挑战性。尽管深度学习具有高度理论灵活性，但其在小样本纵向研究中的应用相比传统统计方法仍处于探索阶段。本研究将神经混合效应（NME）框架应用于帕金森病远程监测，并与广义神经网络混合模型（GNMM）及半参数统计基线广义加性混合模型（GAMMs）进行对比。基于牛津帕金森病远程监测语音数据集，我们表明：虽然神经架构具有灵活性，但在小样本情况下易出现显著过拟合。结果表明，GAMMs在预测精度（MSE 6.56）上优于神经基线模型（MSE > 90），同时保持临床可解释性。我们讨论了这些发现对开发在数据稀缺约束下稳健、可部署的远程监测系统的重要意义，强调了神经模型验证需依赖更大、更多样化的数据集。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of predicting Parkinson's disease progression using limited longitudinal voice data, this study compares statistical and neural mixed-effects models, focusing on their performance in small-sample clinical settings. It evaluates Generalized Additive Mixed Models (GAMMs), Generalized Neural Network Mixed Models (GNMM), and Neural Mixed Effects (NME) models using the Oxford Parkinson's telemonitoring dataset. Results show that while neural models offer theoretical flexibility, they suffer from severe overfitting in small cohorts, yielding much higher mean squared error (MSE > 90) than GAMMs (MSE 6.56). The study concludes that GAMMs provide the best trade-off between predictive accuracy and clinical interpretability, suggesting that neural models require larger, more diverse datasets to be reliably deployed in real-world telemonitoring systems.
为实现帕金森病进展的非侵入式监测，本研究比较了神经混合效应模型与统计混合模型在纵向语音数据上的表现。在牛津帕金森病远程监测语音数据集上，神经模型虽具灵活性，但在小样本下易过拟合，预测误差（MSE > 90）远高于广义加性混合模型（GAMMs，MSE 6.56）。GAMMs在预测精度与临床可解释性之间取得最佳平衡，更适合数据稀缺的临床部署场景。
VeRVE: Versatile Retrieval for Videos via Unified Embeddings
Authors: Shaunak Halbe, Bhagyashree Puranik, Jayakrishnan Unnikrishnan, Kushan Thakkar, Vimal Bhat, Toufiq Parag
First: 2026-01-17T23:13:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:57:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern video retrieval systems are expected to handle diverse tasks ranging from corpus-level retrieval, fine-grained moment localization to flexible multimodal querying. Specialized architectures achieve strong retrieval performance by training modality-specific encoders on massive datasets, but they lack the ability to process composed multimodal queries. In contrast, multimodal LLM (MLLM)-based methods support rich multimodal search but their retrieval performance remains well below that of specialized systems. We present VeRVE, an MLLM-based versatile video retrieval framework that integrates corpus and moment-level retrieval capabilities while accommodating composed multimodal queries within a single architecture. We use contrastive alignment of visual and textual embeddings generated using a shared MLLM backbone to facilitate efficient embedding-based candidate search. Our embedding model, trained efficiently using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) on 700K paired visual-text data samples, surpasses other MLLM-based methods on zero-shot video retrieval tasks. Additionally, we demonstrate that the same model can be adapted without further training to achieve competitive results on zero-shot moment retrieval, and state of the art results for zero-shot composed video retrieval. With additional training for reranking candidates identified in the embedding-based search, our model substantially outperforms existing MLLM-based retrieval systems and achieves retrieval performance comparable to state of the art specialized models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VeRVE：通过统一嵌入实现视频的多功能检索
现代视频检索系统需处理从语料级检索、细粒度时刻定位到灵活多模态查询等多种任务。专用架构通过在大规模数据集上训练模态特定编码器实现强检索性能，但无法处理组合式多模态查询。相比之下，基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的方法支持丰富的多模态搜索，但其检索性能仍远低于专用系统。我们提出VeRVE，一种基于MLLM的多功能视频检索框架，可在单一架构内集成语料级与时刻级检索能力，并支持组合式多模态查询。我们利用共享MLLM主干生成的视觉与文本嵌入进行对比对齐，以实现高效的基于嵌入的候选搜索。该嵌入模型通过低秩适配（LoRA）在70万对视觉-文本数据样本上高效训练，优于其他基于MLLM的方法在零样本视频检索任务中的表现。此外，我们证明同一模型无需额外训练即可在零样本时刻检索中取得竞争力结果，并在零样本组合视频检索中达到当前最优水平。通过在嵌入搜索识别的候选结果上进行重排序训练，我们的模型显著超越现有基于MLLM的检索系统，并实现与当前最优专用模型相当的检索性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, multimodal video retrieval across diverse tasks, VeRVE introduces a unified MLLM-based framework that supports corpus-level retrieval, fine-grained moment localization, and composed multimodal queries within a single architecture. It leverages contrastive alignment of visual and textual embeddings from a shared MLLM backbone to enable efficient candidate search, and employs low-rank adaptation (LoRA) for efficient training on 700K paired samples. Experimental results show VeRVE outperforms other MLLM-based methods on zero-shot video retrieval, achieves competitive results on zero-shot moment retrieval without retraining, and sets state-of-the-art performance on zero-shot composed video retrieval; with reranking, it matches or exceeds specialized retrieval models.
为支持多样化的视频检索任务（如语料级检索与细粒度时刻定位），VeRVE提出一种统一的多模态大语言模型框架，可处理复合多模态查询。该框架通过共享MLLM主干生成视觉与文本嵌入，并利用LoRA高效训练700K对齐样本，实现对比对齐以支持快速候选检索。实验表明，VeRVE在零样本视频检索中超越其他MLLM方法，在零样本复合查询中达到最先进水平，经重排序后性能可媲美专用模型。
StreamCacheVGGT: Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers with Robust Scoring and Hybrid Cache Compression
Authors: Xuanyi Liu, Chunan Yu, Deyi Ji, Qi Zhu, Lingyun Sun, Xuanfu Li, Jin Ma, Tianrun Chen, Lanyun Zhu
First: 2026-04-16T17:12:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:56:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing dense 3D geometry from continuous video streams requires stable inference under a constant memory budget. Existing $O(1)$ frameworks primarily rely on a ``pure eviction'' paradigm, which suffers from significant information destruction due to binary token deletion and evaluation noise from localized, single-layer scoring. To address these bottlenecks, we propose StreamCacheVGGT, a training-free framework that reimagines cache management through two synergistic modules: Cross-Layer Consistency-Enhanced Scoring (CLCES) and Hybrid Cache Compression (HCC). CLCES mitigates activation noise by tracking token importance trajectories across the Transformer hierarchy, employing order-statistical analysis to identify sustained geometric salience. Leveraging these robust scores, HCC transcends simple eviction by introducing a three-tier triage strategy that merges moderately important tokens into retained anchors via nearest-neighbor assignment on the key-vector manifold. This approach preserves essential geometric context that would otherwise be lost. Extensive evaluations on five benchmarks (7-Scenes, NRGBD, ETH3D, Bonn, and KITTI) demonstrate that StreamCacheVGGT sets a new state-of-the-art, delivering superior reconstruction accuracy and long-term stability while strictly adhering to constant-cost constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StreamCacheVGGT：具备鲁棒评分与混合缓存压缩的流式视觉几何变换器
从连续视频流中重建密集3D几何需要在恒定内存预算下保持稳定推理。现有O(1)框架主要依赖“纯驱逐”范式，因二进制令牌删除导致信息大量丢失，且局部单层评分引入噪声。为解决这些瓶颈，我们提出StreamCacheVGGT，一种无需训练的框架，通过两个协同模块重新设计缓存管理：跨层一致性增强评分（CLCES）和混合缓存压缩（HCC）。CLCES通过追踪Transformer层级中令牌重要性轨迹，利用序统计分析识别持续的几何显著性，从而缓解激活噪声。基于这些鲁棒评分，HCC超越简单驱逐，引入三级分诊策略，通过键向量流形上的最近邻分配，将中等重要令牌合并至保留锚点。该方法保留了否则将丢失的关键几何上下文。在五个基准（7-Scenes、NRGBD、ETH3D、Bonn、KITTI）的广泛评估中，StreamCacheVGGT创下新SOTA，实现更优重建精度与长期稳定性，同时严格遵守恒定成本约束。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable 3D geometry reconstruction from video streams under fixed memory budgets, this work introduces StreamCacheVGGT, a training-free framework that improves cache management via two modules: Cross-Layer Consistency-Enhanced Scoring (CLCES) and Hybrid Cache Compression (HCC). CLCES reduces activation noise by tracking token importance across Transformer layers using order-statistical analysis to identify persistent geometric salience, while HCC employs a three-tier triage system that merges moderately important tokens into retained anchors via nearest-neighbor assignment on the key-vector manifold, preserving geometric context. Experiments across five benchmarks show StreamCacheVGGT achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy and long-term stability without exceeding memory constraints.
为在固定内存预算下实现视频流的稳定三维几何重建，本文提出StreamCacheVGGT框架，通过两个模块提升缓存管理：跨层一致性增强评分（CLCES）利用层间顺序统计分析识别持续几何显著性以降低激活噪声，混合缓存压缩（HCC）则通过关键向量流形上的最近邻分配将中等重要性令牌合并至保留锚点，保留关键几何上下文。在五个基准测试中，该方法在不超内存限制的前提下，实现了重建精度和长期稳定性的最先进表现。
Structural Evaluation Metrics for SVG Generation via Leave-One-Out Analysis
Authors: Haonan Zhu, Adrienne Deganutti, Elad Hirsch, Purvanshi Mehta
First: 2026-04-09T22:50:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:54:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
SVG generation is typically evaluated by comparing rendered outputs to reference images, which captures visual similarity but not the structural properties that make SVG editable, decomposable, and reusable. Inspired by the classical jackknife, we introduce element-level leave-one-out (LOO) analysis. The procedure renders the SVG with and without each element, which yields element-level signals for quality assessment and structural analysis. From this single mechanism, we derive (i) per-element quality scores that enable zero-shot artifact detection; (ii) element-concept attribution via LOO footprints crossed with VLM-grounded concept heatmaps; and (iii) four structural metrics: purity, coverage, compactness, and locality, which quantify SVG modularity from complementary angles. These metrics extend SVG evaluation from image similarity to code structure, enabling element-level diagnosis and comparison of how visual concepts are represented, partitioned, and organized within SVG code. Their practical relevance is validated on over 19,000 edits (5 types) across 5 generation systems and 3 complexity tiers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过留一法分析评估SVG生成的结构度量
SVG生成通常通过比较渲染输出与参考图像来评估，这捕捉了视觉相似性，但未涵盖使SVG可编辑、可分解和可重用的结构特性。受经典jackknife方法启发，我们引入元素级留一法（LOO）分析。该方法分别渲染包含和不包含每个元素的SVG，从而获得元素级质量评估与结构分析信号。由此我们推导出：(i) 元素级质量评分，支持零样本伪影检测；(ii) 通过LOO足迹与VLM引导的概念热图交叉，实现元素-概念归因；(iii) 四项结构度量：纯度、覆盖率、紧凑性与局部性，从互补角度量化SVG模块化程度。这些度量将SVG评估从图像相似性拓展至代码结构，支持元素级诊断与比较视觉概念在SVG代码中的表示、划分与组织方式。其实际价值已在5个生成系统、3个复杂度层级的19,000余次编辑（5类）中得到验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of image-based SVG evaluation in capturing structural code properties, this work introduces element-level leave-one-out analysis inspired by the jackknife method. By rendering SVGs with and without each individual element, the approach generates per-element signals to assess quality, detect artifacts, attribute visual concepts, and derive four structural metrics—purity, coverage, compactness, and locality—that quantify modularity. Evaluated across 19,000 edits from five generation systems at three complexity levels, the metrics successfully enable element-level diagnosis and comparative analysis of how visual concepts are encoded, partitioned, and organized in SVG code, shifting evaluation from visual similarity to structural integrity.
为弥补图像相似性评估无法捕捉SVG可编辑性和可复用性所需结构特性的问题，本文提出受jackknife方法启发的元素级留一分析。通过分别渲染包含与不包含每个元素的SVG，该方法生成元素级质量评分以实现零样本异常检测，并结合VLM概念热图实现元素-概念归因。进一步推导出四个结构度量指标——纯度、覆盖率、紧凑性与局部性，从不同角度量化SVG模块化程度。在五个生成系统、三个复杂度层级、19,000次编辑的实验中，这些指标有效诊断并比较了视觉概念在SVG代码中的编码、划分与组织方式，将评估从图像相似性拓展至结构完整性。
When Cultures Meet: Multicultural Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Parth Bhalerao, Mounika Yalamarty, Brian Trinh, Oana Ignat
First: 2025-02-21T22:19:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:51:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-image generation models have achieved strong performance in culturally homogeneous settings, yet their ability to generate multicultural scenes, where people and landmarks originate from different cultures, remains largely unexplored. We introduce multicultural text-to-image generation as a new task and present the first benchmark designed to study this setting. Our dataset contains 9,000 images spanning five countries, three age groups, two genders, 25 historical landmarks, and five languages. Using this benchmark, we analyze the behavior of state-of-the-art text-to-image models across multiple dimensions, including alignment, image quality, aesthetics, knowledge, and fairness. As one strategy for composing cultural and demographic information, we explore MosAIG, a Multi-Agent framework that enhances multicultural Image Generation by leveraging LLMs with distinct cultural personas. Our analysis shows that richer prompt composition can improve image quality and cultural grounding compared to simple prompts, while revealing substantial disparities across languages and demographic groups. We release our dataset and code at https://github.com/AIM-SCU/MosAIG.
中文标题/摘要
标题：文化相遇：多文化文本到图像生成
文本到图像生成模型在文化同质化环境中已取得良好性能，但其生成多文化场景的能力（人物与地标来自不同文化）仍鲜有研究。我们提出多文化文本到图像生成作为新任务，并构建首个针对该场景的基准数据集。该数据集包含来自五个国家、三个年龄段、两种性别、二十五处历史地标及五种语言的九千张图像。基于此基准，我们从对齐度、图像质量、美学、知识与公平性等多个维度分析当前最先进的文本到图像模型表现。为整合文化与人口信息，我们探索了MosAIG多智能体框架，该框架通过赋予LLM不同文化人格以增强多文化图像生成能力。分析表明，相较于简单提示，更丰富的提示组合可提升图像质量与文化契合度，同时揭示语言与人口群体间显著差异。我们的数据集与代码已开源：https://github.com/AIM-SCU/MosAIG。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored capability of text-to-image models to generate multicultural scenes, this study introduces a new benchmark with 9,000 images across diverse cultures, demographics, and languages. It evaluates state-of-the-art models across alignment, quality, aesthetics, knowledge, and fairness dimensions. The authors propose MosAIG, a multi-agent framework using culturally distinct LLMs to compose prompts, which improves image quality and cultural grounding compared to simple prompts, yet reveals persistent disparities across languages and demographic groups.
为填补文本到图像模型在生成多元文化场景方面的能力空白，本研究提出“多元文化图文生成”这一新任务，并构建首个包含五国、三年龄段、两性别、二十五地标及五种语言的9000张图像基准数据集。作者从对齐度、图像质量、美学、知识与公平性等维度评估当前主流模型表现，并提出MosAIG多智能体框架，利用具有不同文化人格的LLM组合提示词，相较简单提示词显著提升图像质量和文化契合度，但也揭示了语言与群体间存在明显差异。
Benchmarking Optimizers for MLPs in Tabular Deep Learning
Authors: Yury Gorishniy, Ivan Rubachev, Dmitrii Feoktistov, Artem Babenko
First: 2026-04-16T17:57:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:48:55+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/yandex-research/tabular-dl-optimizers
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
MLP is a heavily used backbone in modern deep learning (DL) architectures for supervised learning on tabular data, and AdamW is the go-to optimizer used to train tabular DL models. Unlike architecture design, however, the choice of optimizer for tabular DL has not been examined systematically, despite new optimizers showing promise in other domains. To fill this gap, we benchmark 15 optimizers on 17 tabular datasets for training MLP-based models in the standard supervised learning setting under a shared experiment protocol. Our main finding is that the Muon optimizer consistently outperforms AdamW, and thus should be considered a strong and practical choice for practitioners and researchers, if the associated training efficiency overhead is affordable. Additionally, we find exponential moving average of model weights to be a simple yet effective technique that improves AdamW on vanilla MLPs, though its effect is less consistent across model variants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：表格式深度学习中MLP优化器的基准测试
MLP是现代深度学习架构中用于表格式数据监督学习的常用骨干网络，AdamW是训练表格式深度学习模型的首选优化器。然而，与架构设计不同，表格式深度学习中优化器的选择尚未被系统性研究，尽管其他领域的新优化器展现出潜力。为填补这一空白，我们在17个表格式数据集上，采用统一实验协议对15种优化器进行基准测试，用于训练标准监督学习设置下的MLP模型。主要发现是，Muon优化器始终优于AdamW，因此如果可接受其训练效率开销，应被视为从业者和研究者的一个强大且实用的选择。此外，我们发现模型权重的指数移动平均是一种简单而有效的技术，可提升AdamW在基础MLP模型上的表现，但其效果在不同模型变体间一致性较低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic evaluation of optimizers for tabular deep learning despite their widespread use of MLPs, this study benchmarks 15 optimizers across 17 tabular datasets under a standardized protocol. The method involves training MLP models with each optimizer in a supervised setting and comparing performance metrics. The main result shows that Muon consistently outperforms AdamW, suggesting it as a practical alternative when training overhead is manageable. Additionally, exponential moving average of weights improves AdamW’s performance on vanilla MLPs, though its benefits vary across model variants.
鉴于MLP在表格数据深度学习中的广泛应用，但优化器选择尚未系统评估，本研究在17个表格数据集上对15种优化器进行标准化对比实验。结果表明，Muon优化器持续优于当前主流AdamW，适合作为训练效率可接受时的实用选择；同时，模型权重的指数移动平均能有效提升AdamW在基础MLP上的表现，但其效果在不同模型结构中并不稳定。
People readily follow personal advice from AI but it does not improve their well-being
Authors: Lennart Luettgau, Vanessa Cheung, Magda Dubois, Keno Juechems, Jessica Bergs, Luke Symes, Henry Davidson, Bessie O'Dell, Hannah Rose Kirk, Max Rollwage, Christopher Summerfield
First: 2025-11-19T11:22:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:47:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
People increasingly seek personal advice from large language models (LLMs), yet whether humans follow their advice, and its consequences for their well-being, remains unknown. In a longitudinal randomised controlled trial with a representative UK sample (N = 6,474), we found that up to 79% of participants who had a 20-minute discussion with one of three AI chatbots (GPT-4o, LLama-3.3-70B, Gemini 3 Pro) about health, careers or relationships subsequently reported following its advice. Advice-following remained above 60% even for high-stakes recommendations, suggesting that users only weakly calibrate their reliance on AI advice to potential consequences. Based on autograder evaluations of chat transcripts, LLM advice rarely violated safety best practice. However, when queried 2-3 weeks later, participants receiving personal advice from AI showed no sustained well-being benefits compared to a control group who discussed hobbies and interests with the same chatbots. These findings reveal that consumer LLMs exert substantial influence over real-world personal decisions without delivering measurable psychological benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人们乐于遵循AI的个人建议，但这并未改善其福祉
人们越来越多地向大型语言模型（LLMs）寻求个人建议，但人类是否遵循其建议及其对福祉的影响尚不明确。在一项针对代表性英国样本（N=6,474）的纵向随机对照试验中，我们发现，与GPT-4o、LLama-3.3-70B或Gemini 3 Pro三个AI聊天机器人进行20分钟关于健康、职业或人际关系讨论的参与者中，高达79%随后报告遵循了其建议。即使对于高风险建议，遵循率仍保持在60%以上，表明用户仅弱度调整对AI建议的依赖程度以匹配潜在后果。根据自动评分器对聊天记录的评估，LLM建议极少违反安全最佳实践。然而，2-3周后回访时，接受AI个人建议的参与者与对照组（讨论爱好和兴趣）相比，未显示出持续的福祉改善。这些发现表明，消费级LLMs对现实世界个人决策具有显著影响，但并未带来可衡量的心理益处。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of AI chatbots for personal advice, this study investigated whether users follow AI recommendations and whether such adherence improves well-being. Using a longitudinal randomized controlled trial with 6,474 UK participants, researchers found that 79% of users followed advice from AI chatbots (GPT-4o, LLama-3.3-70B, Gemini 3 Pro) after a 20-minute discussion on health, careers, or relationships, with adherence remaining above 60% even for high-stakes topics. Although AI advice rarely violated safety guidelines, participants who received personal advice showed no sustained well-being benefits compared to a control group discussing hobbies, indicating that AI influence on personal decisions does not translate into measurable psychological gains.
鉴于人们日益依赖AI聊天机器人获取个人建议，本研究旨在考察用户是否遵循此类建议及其对幸福感的影响。通过对6474名英国代表性样本进行纵向随机对照试验，研究发现高达79%的参与者在与GPT-4o、LLama-3.3-70B或Gemini 3 Pro等AI聊天机器人进行20分钟对话后采纳了其关于健康、职业或关系的建议，即使涉及高风险议题，采纳率仍保持在60%以上，表明用户对建议风险的评估较弱。尽管AI建议极少违反安全准则，但两周后接受个人建议的参与者在幸福感方面并未优于仅讨论兴趣爱好的对照组，说明AI虽能显著影响现实决策，却无法带来可测量的心理福祉提升。
ASMR-Bench: Auditing for Sabotage in ML Research
Authors: Eric Gan, Aryan Bhatt, Buck Shlegeris, Julian Stastny, Vivek Hebbar
First: 2026-04-17T17:47:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:47:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI systems are increasingly used to conduct research autonomously, misaligned systems could introduce subtle flaws that produce misleading results while evading detection. We introduce ASMR-Bench (Auditing for Sabotage in ML Research), a benchmark for evaluating the ability of auditors to detect sabotage in ML research codebases. ASMR-Bench consists of 9 ML research codebases with sabotaged variants that produce qualitatively different experimental results. Each sabotage modifies implementation details, such as hyperparameters, training data, or evaluation code, while preserving the high-level methodology described in the paper. We evaluated frontier LLMs and LLM-assisted human auditors on ASMR-Bench and found that both struggled to reliably detect sabotage: the best performance was an AUROC of 0.77 and a top-1 fix rate of 42%, achieved by Gemini 3.1 Pro. We also tested LLMs as red teamers and found that LLM-generated sabotages were weaker than human-generated ones but still sometimes evaded same-capability LLM auditors. We release ASMR-Bench to support research on monitoring and auditing techniques for AI-conducted research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ASMR-Bench：用于审计机器学习研究中破坏行为的基准
随着人工智能系统越来越多地自主开展研究，对齐不当的系统可能引入细微缺陷，产生误导性结果并逃避检测。我们提出了ASMR-Bench（用于审计机器学习研究中的破坏行为），一个用于评估审计人员检测机器学习研究代码库中破坏行为能力的基准。ASMR-Bench包含9个机器学习研究代码库，每个代码库均包含经过破坏的变体，其实验结果在质上有所不同。每项破坏修改了实现细节，如超参数、训练数据或评估代码，同时保留论文中描述的高层方法。我们在ASMR-Bench上评估了前沿大语言模型和大语言模型辅助的人类审计员，发现两者均难以可靠地检测破坏行为：最佳表现是AUROC为0.77，Top-1修复率为42%，由Gemini 3.1 Pro实现。我们还测试了大语言模型作为红队成员，发现大语言模型生成的破坏行为弱于人类生成的破坏行为，但仍有时能逃避同等能力的大语言模型审计员。我们发布ASMR-Bench，以支持对AI自主研究监控与审计技术的研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that autonomous AI systems may introduce undetectable flaws in ML research, the authors created ASMR-Bench, a benchmark of nine ML research codebases each containing subtle sabotages that alter experimental outcomes without changing the paper’s methodology. They evaluated both frontier LLMs and human auditors assisted by LLMs, finding that even the best model, Gemini 3.1 Pro, achieved only an AUROC of 0.77 and a 42% top-1 fix rate. LLMs used as red teamers generated weaker sabotages than humans but still occasionally evaded detection by same-capability LLM auditors.
为应对自主AI系统在ML研究中可能引入难以检测的隐蔽缺陷，作者构建了ASMR-Bench基准，包含九个ML研究代码库，每个库均含修改实现细节（如超参数或评估代码）但保留论文方法论的破坏性变体。评估前沿LLM与LLM辅助人工审计者发现，表现最佳的Gemini 3.1 Pro仅达到0.77的AUROC和42%的Top-1修复率。作为红队者时，LLM生成的破坏性变体弱于人工生成者，但仍有时能逃过同等能力LLM审计者的检测。
Author-in-the-Loop Response Generation and Evaluation: Integrating Author Expertise and Intent in Responses to Peer Review
Authors: Qian Ruan, Iryna Gurevych
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-19T14:07:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:47:21+00:00
Comments: accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Author response (rebuttal) writing is a critical stage of scientific peer review that demands substantial author effort. In practice, authors possess domain expertise, author-only information, and response strategies - concrete forms of author expertise and intent - and seek NLP assistance that integrates these signals into author response generation (ARG). Yet this author-in-the-loop paradigm lacks formal NLP formulation and systematic study: no dataset provides fine-grained author signals, existing ARG work lacks author inputs and controls, and no evaluation measures response reflection of author signals and effectiveness in addressing reviewer concerns. To fill these gaps, we introduce (i) Re3Align, the first large-scale dataset of aligned review-response-revision triplets, where revisions proxy author signals; (ii) REspGen, an author-in-the-loop ARG framework supporting flexible author input, multi-attribute control, and evaluation-guided refinement; and (iii) REspEval, a comprehensive evaluation suite with 20+ metrics spanning input utilization, controllability, response quality, and discourse. Experiments with SOTA LLMs demonstrate the benefits of author input and evaluation-guided refinement, the impact of input specificity on response quality, and controllability-quality trade-offs. We release our dataset, generation and evaluation tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：作者参与的响应生成与评估：在同行评审响应中整合作者专业知识与意图
作者回应（反驳）写作是科学同行评审的关键阶段，需要作者投入大量精力。实践中，作者拥有领域专业知识、仅作者知晓的信息和回应策略——这些是作者专业知识和意图的具体体现——并寻求自然语言处理（NLP）协助，将这些信号整合进作者回应生成（ARG）。然而，这一作者参与范式缺乏形式化的NLP表述和系统性研究：没有数据集提供细粒度的作者信号，现有ARG工作缺乏作者输入和控制机制，且没有评估指标能反映响应对作者信号的体现及其解决审稿人关切的有效性。为填补这些空白，我们提出：(i) Re3Align，首个大规模对齐的审稿-回应-修订三元组数据集，其中修订作为作者信号的代理；(ii) REspGen，一个支持灵活作者输入、多属性控制和评估引导优化的作者参与ARG框架；(iii) REspEval，一套涵盖输入利用、可控性、响应质量及话语层面的20多项评估指标的综合评估工具集。在SOTA大语言模型上的实验表明，作者输入和评估引导优化的优势、输入特异性对响应质量的影响，以及可控性与质量之间的权衡。我们公开了数据集、生成与评估工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate author expertise and intent into peer review rebuttals, this work introduces Re3Align, the first large-scale dataset of review-response-revision triplets that captures author signals via revisions, and REspGen, an author-in-the-loop framework enabling flexible author input and multi-attribute control. It also presents REspEval, a comprehensive evaluation suite with over 20 metrics assessing input utilization, controllability, quality, and discourse. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs show that author input significantly improves response quality, input specificity enhances effectiveness, and there is a trade-off between controllability and response quality.
为提升同行评审 rebuttal 生成中作者专业知识与意图的融入，该研究构建了首个包含审稿-回复-修订三元组的大规模数据集 Re3Align，用修订内容代理作者信号；提出了支持作者灵活输入与多属性控制的作者参与式生成框架 REspGen，以及包含20+指标的综合评估体系 REspEval。实验表明，引入作者输入可显著提升回复质量与可控性，输入粒度越高回复效果越佳，但可控性与质量间存在权衡。
Enhancing Hazy Wildlife Imagery: AnimalHaze3k and IncepDehazeGan
Authors: Shivarth Rai, Tejeswar Pokuri
Venue: CVPR 2025
First: 2026-04-17T17:46:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:46:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CV4Animals Workshop, CVPR 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Atmospheric haze significantly degrades wildlife imagery, impeding computer vision applications critical for conservation, such as animal detection, tracking, and behavior analysis. To address this challenge, we introduce AnimalHaze3k a synthetic dataset comprising of 3,477 hazy images generated from 1,159 clear wildlife photographs through a physics-based pipeline. Our novel IncepDehazeGan architecture combines inception blocks with residual skip connections in a GAN framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance (SSIM: 0.8914, PSNR: 20.54, and LPIPS: 0.1104), delivering 6.27% higher SSIM and 10.2% better PSNR than competing approaches. When applied to downstream detection tasks, dehazed images improved YOLOv11 detection mAP by 112% and IoU by 67%. These advances can provide ecologists with reliable tools for population monitoring and surveillance in challenging environmental conditions, demonstrating significant potential for enhancing wildlife conservation efforts through robust visual analytics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：增强雾霾野生动物图像：AnimalHaze3k 与 IncepDehazeGan
大气雾霾显著降低野生动物图像质量，阻碍了对动物检测、跟踪和行为分析等关键计算机视觉应用，这些应用对保护至关重要。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了 AnimalHaze3k 数据集，该数据集包含 3,477 张通过基于物理的流程从 1,159 张清晰野生动物照片生成的雾霾图像。我们的新型 IncepDehazeGan 架构在 GAN 框架中结合了 Inception 模块与残差跳跃连接，实现了最先进性能（SSIM: 0.8914，PSNR: 20.54，LPIPS: 0.1104），在 SSIM 上比现有方法高 6.27%，PSNR 高 10.2%。当应用于下游检测任务时，去雾图像使 YOLOv11 检测的 mAP 提升 112%，IoU 提升 67%。这些进展可为生态学家在恶劣环境条件下提供可靠的种群监测与监控工具，显著提升野生动物保护工作的视觉分析能力。
Summary / 总结
To combat the degradation of wildlife imagery caused by atmospheric haze, which hampers critical conservation tasks like detection and tracking, the authors introduce AnimalHaze3k, a synthetic dataset of 3,477 hazy images derived from 1,159 clear wildlife photos via a physics-based simulation. They propose IncepDehazeGan, a GAN-based architecture integrating inception modules and residual skip connections, achieving superior dehazing performance with SSIM 0.8914, PSNR 20.54, and LPIPS 0.1104, outperforming existing methods by 6.27% in SSIM and 10.2% in PSNR. When applied to object detection, the dehazed images boosted YOLOv11’s mAP by 112% and IoU by 67%, demonstrating practical utility for ecological monitoring under hazy conditions.
为提升雾霾环境下野生动物图像的计算机视觉应用，作者构建了包含3477张合成雾霾图像的AnimalHaze3k数据集，通过物理模型从1159张清晰野生动物照片生成。他们提出的IncepDehazeGan架构结合Inception模块与残差跳跃连接，在GAN框架下实现最优去雾效果，SSIM达0.8914，PSNR达20.54，LPIPS为0.1104，较现有方法分别提升6.27%和10.2%。应用于目标检测时，去雾图像使YOLOv11的mAP提升112%，IoU提升67%，为生态监测在恶劣环境下的应用提供了可靠工具。
Geometric regularization of autoencoders via observed stochastic dynamics
Authors: Sean Hill, Felix X. -F. Ye
First: 2026-04-17T17:44:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:44:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic dynamical systems with slow or metastable behavior evolve, on long time scales, on an unknown low-dimensional manifold in high-dimensional ambient space. Building a reduced simulator from short-burst ambient ensembles is a long-standing problem: local-chart methods like ATLAS suffer from exponential landmark scaling and per-step reprojection, while autoencoder alternatives leave tangent-bundle geometry poorly constrained, and the errors propagate into the learned drift and diffusion. We observe that the ambient covariance~$Λ$ already encodes coordinate-invariant tangent-space information, its range spanning the tangent bundle. Using this, we construct a tangent-bundle penalty and an inverse-consistency penalty for a three-stage pipeline (chart learning, latent drift, latent diffusion) that learns a single nonlinear chart and the latent SDE. The penalties induce a function-space metric, the $ρ$-metric, strictly weaker than the Sobolev $H^1$ norm yet achieving the same chart-quality generalization rate up to logarithmic factors. For the drift, we derive an encoder-pullback target via Itô's formula on the learned encoder and prove a bias decomposition showing the standard decoder-side formula carries systematic error for any imperfect chart. Under a $W^{2,\infty}$ chart-convergence assumption, chart-level error propagates controllably to weak convergence of the ambient dynamics and to convergence of radial mean first-passage times. Experiments on four surfaces embedded in up to $201$ ambient dimensions reduce radial MFPT error by $50$--$70\%$ under rotation dynamics and achieve the lowest inter-well MFPT error on most surface--transition pairs under metastable Müller--Brown Langevin dynamics, while reducing end-to-end ambient coefficient errors by up to an order of magnitude relative to an unregularized autoencoder.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过观测随机动力学对自编码器进行几何正则化
具有缓慢或亚稳态行为的随机动力系统在长时间尺度上，会在高维空间中的未知低维流形上演化。从短时环境集合构建降维模拟器是一个长期存在的问题：局部图方法如ATLAS存在指数级地标扩展和每步重投影问题，而自编码器替代方案则对切丛几何约束不足，误差会传播至学习到的漂移和扩散项。我们观察到，环境协方差Λ已编码坐标不变的切空间信息，其值域覆盖整个切丛。基于此，我们为三阶段流程（图学习、潜在漂移、潜在扩散）构建了切丛惩罚和逆一致性惩罚，以学习单一非线性图和潜在SDE。这些惩罚诱导出一个函数空间度量ρ-度量，其严格弱于Sobolev H¹范数，但仍能达到相同图质量的泛化率（至对数因子）。对于漂移项，我们通过学习到的编码器的Itô公式推导出编码器拉回目标，并证明了偏差分解，表明标准解码器侧公式对任何不完美图均存在系统性误差。在W^{2,∞}图收敛假设下，图级误差可可控传播至环境动力学的弱收敛及径向首次通过时间的收敛。在四个嵌入至最多201维环境空间的曲面上的实验表明，在旋转动力学下径向MFPT误差降低50-70%，在亚稳态Müller-Brown Langevin动力学下，多数曲面间过渡对的间井MFPT误差达到最低，同时相对于无正则化自编码器，环境系数端到端误差降低达一个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning low-dimensional manifolds from high-dimensional stochastic dynamics, this work proposes a geometrically regularized autoencoder framework that leverages ambient covariance to constrain tangent-space geometry. The method constructs a three-stage pipeline—chart learning, latent drift, and latent diffusion—using a tangent-bundle penalty and inverse-consistency penalty to enforce geometric fidelity. Experimental results across four high-dimensional surfaces show the approach reduces radial mean first-passage time errors by 50–70% under rotation dynamics and achieves the lowest inter-well MFPT errors under metastable Langevin dynamics, while cutting ambient coefficient errors by up to an order of magnitude compared to unregularized autoencoders.
为了解决从高维噪声数据中学习低维动力学的难题，本文提出一种基于几何正则化的自编码器框架，利用环境协方差约束切空间几何。该方法构建了一个三阶段流程——图表学习、潜在漂移和潜在扩散——通过切丛惩罚和逆一致性惩罚确保几何保真度。在最多201维嵌入的四个曲面上的实验表明，该方法在旋转动力学下将径向平均首次通过时间误差降低50–70%，在亚稳态Langevin动力学下对多数曲面间过渡对实现最低的间井MFPT误差，同时将环境系数误差降低一个数量级，优于未正则化的自编码器。
COVER:COverage-VErified Roadmaps for Fixed-time Motion Planning in Continuous Semi-Static Environments
Authors: Niranjan Kumar Ilampooranan, Constantinos Chamzas
First: 2025-10-04T16:58:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:41:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ability to solve motion-planning queries within a fixed time budget is critical for deploying robotic systems in time-sensitive applications. Semi-static environments, where most of the workspace remains fixed while a subset of obstacles varies between tasks, exhibit structured variability that can be exploited to provide stronger guarantees than general-purpose planners. However, existing approaches either lack formal coverage guarantees or rely on discretizations of obstacle configurations that restrict applicability to realistic domains. This paper introduces COVER, a framework that incrementally constructs coverage-verified roadmaps for semi-static environments. COVER decomposes the arrangement space by independently partitioning the configuration space of each movable obstacle and verifies roadmap feasibility within each partition, enabling fixed-time query resolution for verified regions.We evaluate COVER on a 7-DoF manipulator performing object-picking in tabletop and shelf environments, demonstrating broader problem-space coverage and higher query success rates than prior work, particularly with obstacles of different sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：COVER：面向连续半静态环境的固定时间运动规划覆盖验证路线图
在时间敏感的应用中，能够在固定时间内解决运动规划查询的能力对机器人系统的部署至关重要。半静态环境（大部分工作空间保持固定，而部分障碍物在任务间变化）表现出可被利用的结构化变异性，从而能提供比通用规划器更强的保证。然而，现有方法要么缺乏形式化的覆盖保证，要么依赖障碍物配置的离散化，限制了其在现实领域中的适用性。 本文提出了COVER框架，该框架可增量构建半静态环境的覆盖验证路线图。COVER通过独立划分每个可移动障碍物的构型空间来分解排列空间，并在每个划分内验证路线图的可行性，从而实现对验证区域的固定时间查询响应。我们在7自由度机械臂进行桌面和货架环境中物体抓取的任务上评估了COVER，结果表明其覆盖问题空间更广，查询成功率高于现有方法，尤其在处理不同尺寸障碍物时表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, fixed-time motion planning in semi-static environments where obstacles vary but most of the workspace remains fixed, this paper proposes COVER, a framework that incrementally builds coverage-verified roadmaps by independently partitioning the configuration space of each movable obstacle. It verifies roadmap feasibility within each partition to ensure fixed-time query resolution for verified regions. Experimental results on a 7-DoF manipulator show COVER achieves broader coverage and higher success rates than prior methods, especially when handling obstacles of varying sizes.
为在障碍物部分变化但大部分工作空间保持固定的半静态环境中实现可靠的固定时间运动规划，本文提出COVER框架，通过独立划分可移动障碍物的构型空间并逐分区验证路网可行性，实现覆盖验证的增量式路网构建。实验表明，该方法在7自由度机械臂执行桌面与货架取物任务时，相比现有方法具有更广的覆盖范围和更高的查询成功率，尤其在处理不同尺寸障碍物时表现更优。
Using Large Language Models and Knowledge Graphs to Improve the Interpretability of Machine Learning Models in Manufacturing
Authors: Thomas Bayer, Alexander Lohr, Sarah Weiß, Bernd Michelberger, Wolfram Höpken
First: 2026-04-17T17:41:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:41:17+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 8 figures, Submittet to conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explaining Machine Learning (ML) results in a transparent and user-friendly manner remains a challenging task of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). In this paper, we present a method to enhance the interpretability of ML models by using a Knowledge Graph (KG). We store domain-specific data along with ML results and their corresponding explanations, establishing a structured connection between domain knowledge and ML insights. To make these insights accessible to users, we designed a selective retrieval method in which relevant triplets are extracted from the KG and processed by a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate user-friendly explanations of ML results. We evaluated our method in a manufacturing environment using the XAI Question Bank. Beyond standard questions, we introduce more complex, tailored questions that highlight the strengths of our approach. We evaluated 33 questions, analyzing responses using quantitative metrics such as accuracy and consistency, as well as qualitative ones such as clarity and usefulness. Our contribution is both theoretical and practical: from a theoretical perspective, we present a novel approach for effectively enabling LLMs to dynamically access a KG in order to improve the explainability of ML results. From a practical perspective, we provide empirical evidence showing that such explanations can be successfully applied in real-world manufacturing environments, supporting better decision-making in manufacturing processes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型与知识图谱提升制造领域机器学习模型的可解释性
以透明且用户友好的方式解释机器学习（ML）结果仍是可解释人工智能（XAI）的一项挑战。本文提出一种方法，通过知识图谱（KG）增强ML模型的可解释性。我们存储领域特定数据、ML结果及其对应解释，建立领域知识与ML洞察之间的结构化关联。为使这些洞察易于用户理解，我们设计了一种选择性检索方法：从KG中提取相关三元组，并由大语言模型（LLM）处理生成用户友好的ML结果解释。我们在制造环境中使用XAI问题库对方法进行评估，除标准问题外，还引入更复杂、定制化的问题以凸显本方法的优势。我们评估了33个问题，结合定量指标（如准确率和一致性）与定性指标（如清晰度和实用性）分析结果。本研究兼具理论与实践贡献：理论上，我们提出一种新颖方法，使LLM能动态访问KG以提升ML结果的可解释性；实践上，我们提供实证证据表明此类解释可成功应用于真实制造环境，支持制造流程中的更好决策。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of making machine learning results interpretable in manufacturing, this study proposes integrating knowledge graphs with large language models to generate user-friendly explanations. The method stores domain-specific data and ML outputs within a structured knowledge graph, then retrieves relevant triplets and uses an LLM to synthesize explanations tailored to user queries. Evaluated using 33 questions from the XAI Question Bank—including complex, real-world manufacturing scenarios—the approach demonstrated high accuracy and consistency, along with strong qualitative feedback on clarity and usefulness, proving its practical value in industrial settings.
为提升制造业中机器学习结果的可解释性，该研究提出结合知识图谱与大语言模型生成用户友好的解释。方法将领域数据与模型结果结构化存储于知识图谱中，通过检索相关三元组输入大语言模型，生成清晰、上下文相关的解释。在包含33个问题（含复杂定制化问题）的XAI问题库评估中，该方法在准确率、一致性等定量指标及清晰度、实用性等定性指标上表现优异，验证了其在真实制造场景中的实用价值。
Evaluating the Progression of Large Language Model Capabilities for Small-Molecule Drug Design
Authors: Shriram Chennakesavalu, Kirill Shmilovich, Hayley Weir, Colin Grambow, John Bradshaw, Patricia Suriana, Chen Cheng, Kangway Chuang
First: 2026-04-17T17:40:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:40:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have the potential to accelerate small molecule drug design due to their ability to reason about information from diverse sources and formats. However, their practical utility remains unclear due to the lack of benchmarks that reflect real-world scenarios. In this work, we introduce a suite of chemically-grounded tasks spanning molecular property prediction, molecular representation transformations, and molecular design. Importantly, we formulate these tasks as reinforcement learning (RL) environments, enabling a unified approach for evaluation and post-training. Across three model families, we find that frontier models are increasingly proficient at chemical tasks, but that there is significant room for improvement, especially in experimental settings with low data. Critically, we show that RL-based post-training can substantially improve performance. A smaller model post-trained on our environments becomes competitive with state-of-the-art frontier models, despite a significantly weaker base model. This suggests a practical route toward employing LLMs in drug discovery; by combining carefully-designed evaluation tasks with targeted post-training, we can both elucidate and close critical capability gaps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估大型语言模型在小分子药物设计中的能力进展
大型语言模型（LLMs）因其能从多样化的信息源和格式中进行推理，有望加速小分子药物设计。然而，由于缺乏反映真实场景的基准测试，其实际效用尚不明确。本研究引入了一系列基于化学原理的任务，涵盖分子性质预测、分子表示转换和分子设计，并将其形式化为强化学习（RL）环境，从而实现统一的评估与后训练方法。在三个模型家族中，我们发现前沿模型在化学任务上的能力日益提升，但在数据稀缺的实验环境中仍有显著改进空间。关键的是，我们证明基于RL的后训练可显著提升性能：即使基础模型较弱，经过我们环境后训练的小型模型也能与前沿模型相媲美。这表明，通过结合精心设计的评估任务与针对性后训练，可为LLMs在药物发现中的应用提供切实可行的路径，同时揭示并弥合关键能力差距。

Crucially, RL-based post-training significantly boosts performance, enabling a smaller base model to match state-of-the-art models, demonstrating a viable path to enhance LLM utility in drug discovery through targeted training and evaluation.
为评估大语言模型在小分子药物设计中的实际应用潜力，本研究构建了基于化学原理的强化学习评估环境，涵盖分子性质预测、表示转换与设计任务。实验表明，前沿模型在化学任务上能力持续提升，但在数据稀缺的实验场景中仍有明显不足。关键发现是，基于强化学习的后训练可显著提升性能，使基础模型能力接近前沿模型，证明通过精心设计的评估任务与针对性后训练，可有效弥合药物发现中的关键能力差距。
Learning to Reason with Insight for Informal Theorem Proving
Authors: Yunhe Li, Hao Shi, Bowen Deng, Wei Wang, Mengzhe Ruan, Hanxu Hou, Zhongxiang Dai, Siyang Gao, Chao Wang, Shuang Qiu, Linqi Song
First: 2026-04-17T17:36:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:36:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although most of the automated theorem-proving approaches depend on formal proof systems, informal theorem proving can align better with large language models' (LLMs) strength in natural language processing. In this work, we identify a primary bottleneck in informal theorem proving as a lack of insight, namely the difficulty of recognizing the core techniques required to solve complex problems. To address this, we propose a novel framework designed to cultivate this essential reasoning skill and enable LLMs to perform insightful reasoning. We propose $\mathtt{DeepInsightTheorem}$, a hierarchical dataset that structures informal proofs by explicitly extracting core techniques and proof sketches alongside the final proof. To fully exploit this dataset, we design a Progressive Multi-Stage SFT strategy that mimics the human learning process, guiding the model from basic proof writing to insightful thinking. Our experiments on challenging mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that this insight-aware generation strategy significantly outperforms baselines. These results demonstrate that teaching models to identify and apply core techniques can substantially improve their mathematical reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过洞察力学习进行非形式定理证明
尽管大多数自动定理证明方法依赖于形式证明系统，但非形式定理证明更能契合大型语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言处理方面的优势。本研究将非形式定理证明的主要瓶颈识别为缺乏洞察力，即难以识别解决复杂问题所需的核心技巧。为此，我们提出了一种新颖框架，旨在培养这一关键推理能力，使LLMs能够进行有洞察力的推理。我们提出了$\mathtt{DeepInsightTheorem}$，一个分层数据集，通过显式提取核心技巧和证明草图，与最终证明一同结构化非形式证明。为充分利用该数据集，我们设计了一种渐进式多阶段监督微调策略，模拟人类学习过程，引导模型从基础证明写作逐步过渡到有洞察力的思考。在具有挑战性的数学基准测试中的实验表明，该洞察力感知生成策略显著优于基线方法。这些结果表明，教会模型识别和应用核心技巧可显著提升其数学推理能力。

Motivated by the observation that informal theorem proving better aligns with LLMs' natural language strengths than formal systems, this work targets the bottleneck of lacking insight—difficulty in identifying core proof techniques.
