No Universal Courtesy: A Cross-Linguistic, Multi-Model Study of Politeness Effects on LLMs Using the PLUM Corpus
Authors: Hitesh Mehta, Arjit Saxena, Garima Chhikara, Rohit Kumar
First: 2026-04-17T17:33:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:33:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper explores the response of Large Language Models (LLMs) to user prompts with different degrees of politeness and impoliteness. The Politeness Theory by Brown and Levinson and the Impoliteness Framework by Culpeper form the basis of experiments conducted across three languages (English, Hindi, Spanish), five models (Gemini-Pro, GPT-4o Mini, Claude 3.7 Sonnet, DeepSeek-Chat, and Llama 3), and three interaction histories between users (raw, polite, and impolite). Our sample consists of 22,500 pairs of prompts and responses of various types, evaluated across five levels of politeness using an eight-factor assessment framework: coherence, clarity, depth, responsiveness, context retention, toxicity, conciseness, and readability. The findings show that model performance is highly influenced by tone, dialogue history, and language. While polite prompts enhance the average response quality by up to ~11% and impolite tones worsen it, these effects are neither consistent nor universal across languages and models. English is best served by courteous or direct tones, Hindi by deferential and indirect tones, and Spanish by assertive tones. Among the models, Llama is the most tone-sensitive (11.5% range), whereas GPT is more robust to adversarial tone. These results indicate that politeness is a quantifiable computational variable that affects LLM behaviour, though its impact is language- and model-dependent rather than universal. To support reproducibility and future work, we additionally release PLUM (Politeness Levels in Utterances, Multilingual), a publicly available corpus of 1,500 human-validated prompts across three languages and five politeness categories, and provide a formal supplementary analysis of six falsifiable hypotheses derived from politeness theory, empirically assessed against the dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无普适礼貌：基于PLUM语料库的跨语言多模型礼貌对大语言模型影响研究
本文探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）对不同礼貌程度用户提示的响应。实验基于布朗与莱文森的礼貌理论和库珀的不礼貌框架，覆盖三种语言（英语、印地语、西班牙语）、五个模型（Gemini-Pro、GPT-4o Mini、Claude 3.7 Sonnet、DeepSeek-Chat、Llama 3）及三种用户对话历史（原始、礼貌、不礼貌）。样本包含22,500组各类提示与响应对，通过八项评估维度（连贯性、清晰度、深度、响应性、上下文保留、毒性、简洁性、可读性）进行五级礼貌评估。结果表明，模型表现受语气、对话历史和语言影响显著：礼貌提示可提升平均响应质量约11%，不礼貌语气则降低质量，但此效应非跨语言或跨模型一致。英语偏好礼貌或直接语气，印地语偏好谦逊间接语气，西班牙语偏好坚定语气。模型中Llama对语气最敏感（11.5%波动），GPT则对对抗性语气更鲁棒。结果表明礼貌是可量化计算变量，影响LLM行为，但其影响具语言与模型特异性，非普适。为支持可复现性与后续研究，我们发布PLUM语料库（多语言语句礼貌等级），含1,500组人工验证提示（三语言、五类礼貌），并提供六项可证伪的礼貌理论假设的正式补充分析。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how politeness in user prompts affects Large Language Models across multiple languages and architectures, motivated by the need to understand tone-sensitive behavior in LLMs. Using the PLUM corpus, researchers evaluated 22,500 prompt-response pairs across three languages and five models, assessing responses on eight linguistic dimensions. Results show polite prompts generally improve response quality by up to 11%, while impolite tones degrade it, but these effects vary significantly by language and model. English models respond best to courteous or direct tones, Hindi to deferential and indirect ones, and Spanish to assertive tones. Llama models are most sensitive to tone (11.5% performance variance), while GPT models are more resilient to adversarial tone. The findings confirm politeness as a measurable factor influencing LLM behavior, yet its impact is not universal, being strongly tied to linguistic and architectural context.
本研究旨在探究用户提示语中的礼貌程度如何影响不同语言和架构的大语言模型，动机在于理解LLM对语气敏感的行为。研究基于PLUM语料库，评估了22500组跨三种语言和五种模型的提示-响应对，从八个语言维度进行评分。结果显示，礼貌提示可使平均响应质量提升最高11%，而粗鲁语气则降低质量，但效果因语言和模型而异：英语偏好礼貌或直接语气，印地语偏好谦逊间接语气，西班牙语偏好坚定语气。Llama模型对语气最敏感（性能波动达11.5%），而GPT模型更具鲁棒性。研究证实礼貌是影响LLM行为的可量化变量，但其作用并非普适，而是依赖语言和模型架构。
VEFX-Bench: A Holistic Benchmark for Generic Video Editing and Visual Effects
Authors: Xiangbo Gao, Sicong Jiang, Bangya Liu, Xinghao Chen, Minglai Yang, Siyuan Yang, Mingyang Wu, Jiongze Yu, Qi Zheng, Haozhi Wang, Jiayi Zhang, Jared Yang, Jie Yang, Zihan Wang, Qing Yin, Zhengzhong Tu
First: 2026-04-17T17:28:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:28:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI-assisted video creation becomes increasingly practical, instruction-guided video editing has become essential for refining generated or captured footage to meet professional requirements. Yet the field still lacks both a large-scale human-annotated dataset with complete editing examples and a standardized evaluator for comparing editing systems. Existing resources are limited by small scale, missing edited outputs, or the absence of human quality labels, while current evaluation often relies on expensive manual inspection or generic vision-language model judges that are not specialized for editing quality. We introduce VEFX-Dataset, a human-annotated dataset containing 5,049 video editing examples across 9 major editing categories and 32 subcategories, each labeled along three decoupled dimensions: Instruction Following, Rendering Quality, and Edit Exclusivity. Building on VEFX-Dataset, we propose VEFX-Reward, a reward model designed specifically for video editing quality assessment. VEFX-Reward jointly processes the source video, the editing instruction, and the edited video, and predicts per-dimension quality scores via ordinal regression. We further release VEFX-Bench, a benchmark of 300 curated video-prompt pairs for standardized comparison of editing systems. Experiments show that VEFX-Reward aligns more strongly with human judgments than generic VLM judges and prior reward models on both standard IQA/VQA metrics and group-wise preference evaluation. Using VEFX-Reward as an evaluator, we benchmark representative commercial and open-source video editing systems, revealing a persistent gap between visual plausibility, instruction following, and edit locality in current models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VEFX-Bench：通用视频编辑与视觉特效的综合基准
随着AI辅助视频创作日益实用，指令引导的视频编辑已成为精修生成或拍摄素材以满足专业需求的关键环节。然而，该领域仍缺乏大规模人工标注且包含完整编辑示例的数据集，以及用于比较编辑系统的标准化评估器。现有资源受限于规模小、缺少编辑输出或缺乏人工质量标签，而当前评估常依赖昂贵的人工检查或通用视觉-语言模型裁判，后者并不专精于编辑质量评估。我们引入VEFX-Dataset，该数据集包含5049个视频编辑示例，覆盖9大编辑类别和32个子类别，每个示例沿三个解耦维度标注：指令遵循度、渲染质量与编辑独占性。基于该数据集，我们提出VEFX-Reward，专为视频编辑质量评估设计的奖励模型。VEFX-Reward联合处理源视频、编辑指令与编辑后视频，并通过序数回归预测各维度质量得分。我们进一步发布VEFX-Bench，包含300对精选视频-提示对，用于标准化编辑系统比较。实验表明，VEFX-Reward在标准IQA/VQA指标及组内偏好评估中，比通用VLM裁判和先前奖励模型更贴近人类判断。以VEFX-Reward为评估器，我们对代表性商业及开源视频编辑系统进行基准测试，揭示当前模型在视觉合理性、指令遵循与编辑局部性之间仍存在持续性差距。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for standardized evaluation and large-scale human-annotated data in AI video editing, the paper introduces VEFX-Dataset—a collection of 5,049 annotated video editing examples across 9 categories—each scored on three dimensions: instruction following, rendering quality, and edit exclusivity. Building on this dataset, VEFX-Reward, a specialized reward model, jointly processes source video, instruction, and edited output to predict per-dimension scores via ordinal regression. Evaluated against human judgments and compared to generic vision-language models, VEFX-Reward demonstrates superior alignment with human preferences. Using it to benchmark 300 video-prompt pairs in VEFX-Bench, the study reveals that current video editing systems consistently underperform in balancing visual plausibility, instruction adherence, and edit locality.
为解决AI视频编辑系统缺乏统一评估标准的问题，该研究构建了包含5049个视频编辑样例的VEFX-Dataset，覆盖9大类32子类，每例由人类标注三个维度：指令遵循、渲染质量与编辑独占性。基于该数据集，提出VEFX-Reward奖励模型，联合处理原始视频、编辑指令与编辑后视频，通过序数回归预测各维度得分。实验表明，该模型在人类评分与通用视觉语言模型对比中表现更优。在VEFX-Bench基准的300组视频-提示对上评测发现，当前主流编辑系统在视觉合理性、指令遵循与编辑局部性三方面仍存在显著性能差距。
From Benchmarking to Reasoning: A Dual-Aspect, Large-Scale Evaluation of LLMs on Vietnamese Legal Text
Authors: Van-Truong Le
First: 2026-04-17T17:28:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:28:23+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 2 figures. Accepted at the FISU Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence (FJCAI 2026), Vietnam
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The complexity of Vietnam's legal texts presents a significant barrier to public access to justice. While Large Language Models offer a promising solution for legal text simplification, evaluating their true capabilities requires a multifaceted approach that goes beyond surface-level metrics. This paper introduces a comprehensive dual-aspect evaluation framework to address this need. First, we establish a performance benchmark for four state-of-the-art large language models (GPT-4o, Claude 3 Opus, Gemini 1.5 Pro, and Grok-1) across three key dimensions: Accuracy, Readability, and Consistency. Second, to understand the "why" behind these performance scores, we conduct a large-scale error analysis on a curated dataset of 60 complex Vietnamese legal articles, using a novel, expert-validated error typology. Our results reveal a crucial trade-off: models like Grok-1 excel in Readability and Consistency but compromise on fine-grained legal Accuracy, while models like Claude 3 Opus achieve high Accuracy scores that mask a significant number of subtle but critical reasoning errors. The error analysis pinpoints \textit{Incorrect Example} and \textit{Misinterpretation} as the most prevalent failures, confirming that the primary challenge for current LLMs is not summarization but controlled, accurate legal reasoning. By integrating a quantitative benchmark with a qualitative deep dive, our work provides a holistic and actionable assessment of LLMs for legal applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从基准测试到推理：面向越南法律文本的大型语言模型双维度大规模评估
越南法律文本的复杂性构成了公众获取司法救济的重要障碍。尽管大型语言模型为法律文本简化提供了有前景的解决方案，但评估其真实能力需要超越表面指标的多维度方法。本文提出了一种全面的双维度评估框架以满足这一需求。首先，我们在准确性、可读性和一致性三个关键维度上，为四款前沿大型语言模型（GPT-4o、Claude 3 Opus、Gemini 1.5 Pro 和 Grok-1）建立了性能基准。其次，为理解这些性能得分背后的“原因”，我们对60篇复杂越南法律文章的精选数据集进行了大规模错误分析，采用一种新颖且经专家验证的错误类型学。结果揭示了一个关键权衡：如Grok-1等模型在可读性和一致性上表现优异，但牺牲了细粒度的法律准确性；而如Claude 3 Opus等模型虽取得高准确性分数，却掩盖了大量细微但关键的推理错误。错误分析指出“错误示例”和“误读”是最常见的失败类型，证实当前大型语言模型的主要挑战并非摘要生成，而是受控、准确的法律推理。通过结合定量基准与定性深度分析，本文为法律应用中的大型语言模型提供了全面且可操作的评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty Vietnamese citizens face accessing legal texts, this study evaluates large language models (LLMs) through a dual-aspect framework combining quantitative benchmarking and qualitative error analysis. It tests four leading models—GPT-4o, Claude 3 Opus, Gemini 1.5 Pro, and Grok-1—across Accuracy, Readability, and Consistency using a curated dataset of 60 legal articles. Results show a trade-off: Grok-1 performs best in Readability and Consistency but sacrifices legal Accuracy, while Claude 3 Opus scores high on Accuracy yet hides critical reasoning flaws. Error analysis identifies "Incorrect Example" and "Misinterpretation" as dominant failure modes, revealing that LLMs struggle with controlled legal reasoning rather than summarization.
本文针对越南法律文本复杂性导致公众司法获取困难的问题，提出一套双维度评估框架，结合量化基准测试与质性错误分析，评估GPT-4o、Claude 3 Opus、Gemini 1.5 Pro和Grok-1四款主流大语言模型。评估维度包括准确性、可读性和一致性，结果显示Grok-1在可读性与一致性上表现突出但牺牲法律准确性，而Claude 3 Opus虽准确性高，却隐藏大量细微但关键的推理错误。错误分析指出‘错误示例’与‘误读’是最常见失败类型，表明当前大模型的核心挑战在于可控的法律推理能力，而非表面摘要能力。
CRoCoDiL: Continuous and Robust Conditioned Diffusion for Language
Authors: Roy Uziel, Omer Belhasin, Itay Levy, Akhiad Bercovich, Ran El-Yaniv, Ran Zilberstein, Michael Elad
First: 2026-03-02T12:50:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:27:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) provide an efficient non-causal alternative to autoregressive generation but often struggle with token dependencies and semantic incoherence due to their reliance on discrete marginal distributions. We address these limitations by shifting the diffusion process into a continuous sentence-level semantic space. We propose CRoCoDiL (Continuous and Robust Conditioned Diffusion for Language), a unified fine-tuning approach that jointly trains an encoder-demasker architecture, grounding the MDM demasking in continuous latent representations. This leads to the formation of a novel autoencoder in which decoding is obtained by an MDM algorithm. Relying on the same framework, we introduce two unconditional text synthesis algorithms: Continuous-Then-Discrete (ConThenDisc), a hybrid-diffusion approach that first generates latent representations in continuous space and then decodes these to tokens via an MDM, and Continuous-Within-Discrete (ConWithinDisc), a multi-diffusion strategy that refines latent representations throughout the discrete sampling process. Experiments using LLaDA show that our methods achieve superior generation quality and more than 10x faster sampling speeds in an unconditional setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRoCoDiL：面向语言的连续且鲁棒的条件扩散模型
掩码扩散模型（MDMs）提供了一种高效的非因果替代自回归生成方法，但因其依赖离散边缘分布，常在处理词元依赖和语义不一致方面表现不佳。我们通过将扩散过程迁移至连续的句子级语义空间来解决这些局限。我们提出CRoCoDiL（面向语言的连续且鲁棒的条件扩散模型），这是一种统一的微调方法，联合训练编码器-解码器架构，使MDM的解码过程基于连续潜在表示。这形成了一个新颖的自编码器，其中解码由MDM算法实现。基于同一框架，我们引入两种无条件文本合成算法：连续后离散（ConThenDisc），一种混合扩散方法，先在连续空间生成潜在表示，再通过MDM解码为词元；以及连续内离散（ConWithinDisc），一种多扩散策略，在离散采样过程中持续优化潜在表示。实验表明，LLaDA上的测试结果证明，我们的方法在无条件生成中质量更优，采样速度提升超过10倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Masked Diffusion Models in handling token dependencies and semantic coherence, this work introduces CRoCoDiL, a framework that embeds the diffusion process into a continuous sentence-level semantic space. It jointly trains an encoder-demasker architecture to ground demasking in continuous latent representations, forming a novel autoencoder where decoding is performed via MDM. Two unconditional synthesis methods—ConThenDisc and ConWithinDisc—are proposed: the former generates continuous latents first then decodes to tokens, while the latter refines latents during discrete sampling. Experiments on LLaDA demonstrate superior generation quality and over 10x faster sampling speeds compared to baseline methods.
为解决掩码扩散模型在处理词元依赖和语义连贯性方面的不足，该研究提出CRoCoDiL框架，将扩散过程嵌入连续语义空间以提升生成质量。该框架联合训练编码器-解码器架构，使解码基于连续潜在表示，形成新型自编码器，其中解码通过扩散模型实现。提出两种无条件文本生成方法：ConThenDisc先生成连续潜在表示再解码，ConWithinDisc则在离散采样过程中持续优化潜在表示。在LLaDA上的实验表明，CRoCoDiL在生成质量上优于基线方法，且采样速度提升超过10倍。
Hero-Mamba: Mamba-based Dual Domain Learning for Underwater Image Enhancement
Authors: Tejeswar Pokuri, Shivarth Rai
Venue: AAAI 2026
First: 2026-04-17T17:24:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:24:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted at AI4ES Workshop AAAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Underwater images often suffer from severe degradation, such as color distortion, low contrast, and blurred details, due to light absorption and scattering in water. While learning-based methods like CNNs and Transformers have shown promise, they face critical limitations: CNNs struggle to model the long-range dependencies needed for non-uniform degradation, and Transformers incur quadratic computational complexity, making them inefficient for high-resolution images. To address these challenges, we propose Hero-Mamba, a novel Mamba-based network that achieves efficient dual-domain learning for underwater image enhancement. Our approach uniquely processes information from both the spatial domain (RGB image) and the spectral domain (FFT components) in parallel. This dual-domain input allows the network to decouple degradation factors, separating color/brightness information from texture/noise. The core of our network utilizes Mamba-based SS2D blocks to capture global receptive fields and long-range dependencies with linear complexity, overcoming the limitations of both CNNs and Transformers. Furthermore, we introduce a ColorFusion block, guided by a background light prior, to restore color information with high fidelity. Extensive experiments on the LSUI and UIEB benchmark datasets demonstrate that Hero-Mamba outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Notably, our model achieves a PSNR of 25.802 and an SSIM of 0.913 on LSUI, validating its superior performance and generalization capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hero-Mamba：基于Mamba的双域学习用于水下图像增强
水下图像常因水中光吸收与散射而严重退化，表现为色彩失真、对比度低和细节模糊。尽管卷积神经网络（CNN）和Transformer等学习方法已取得一定成效，但仍存在关键局限：CNN难以建模非均匀退化所需的长程依赖关系，而Transformer则因二次计算复杂度，在高分辨率图像处理中效率低下。为解决这些问题，我们提出Hero-Mamba，一种基于Mamba架构的新型网络，实现水下图像增强的高效双域学习。该方法并行处理空间域（RGB图像）与频谱域（FFT分量）信息，使网络能解耦退化因素，将色彩/亮度信息与纹理/噪声分离。网络核心采用基于Mamba的SS2D模块，以线性复杂度捕捉全局感受野与长程依赖，克服CNN与Transformer的局限。此外，我们引入受背景光先验引导的ColorFusion模块，以高保真度恢复色彩信息。在LSUI与UIEB基准数据集上的大量实验表明，Hero-Mamba优于现有最先进方法。值得注意的是，其在LSUI数据集上达到PSNR 25.802、SSIM 0.913，验证了其卓越性能与泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of CNNs and Transformers in handling non-uniform underwater image degradation, Hero-Mamba introduces a Mamba-based architecture that enables efficient dual-domain learning by processing spatial (RGB) and spectral (FFT) inputs in parallel. This design decouples degradation factors, allowing the model to separately restore color/brightness and texture/noise. The core SS2D blocks leverage linear complexity to capture long-range dependencies, overcoming CNNs' local modeling and Transformers' quadratic cost. A ColorFusion module, guided by background light priors, enhances color fidelity. Experiments on LSUI and UIEB show Hero-Mamba achieves PSNR of 25.802 and SSIM of 0.913 on LSUI, outperforming existing methods in both quality and generalization.
为解决卷积神经网络建模能力不足和Transformer计算复杂度过高的问题，Hero-Mamba提出一种基于Mamba架构的双域学习方法，同时处理空间域（RGB图像）和频谱域（FFT分量）信息，实现色彩/亮度与纹理/噪声的解耦。其核心SS2D模块以线性复杂度捕捉长程依赖，克服传统方法局限。结合基于背景光先验的ColorFusion模块，模型能高保真恢复色彩。在LSUI和UIEB数据集上，其PSNR达25.802，SSIM达0.913，显著优于现有方法。
EnvScaler: Scaling Tool-Interactive Environments for LLM Agent via Programmatic Synthesis
Authors: Xiaoshuai Song, Haofei Chang, Guanting Dong, Yutao Zhu, Ji-Rong Wen, Zhicheng Dou
First: 2026-01-09T14:32:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:24:06+00:00
Comments: Add some experiments
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are expected to be trained to act as agents in various real-world environments, but this process relies on rich and varied tool-interaction sandboxes. However, access to real systems is often restricted; LLM-simulated environments are prone to hallucinations and inconsistencies; and manually built sandboxes are hard to scale. In this paper, we propose EnvScaler, an automated framework for scalable tool-interaction environments via programmatic synthesis. EnvScaler comprises two components. First, SkelBuilder constructs diverse environment skeletons through topic mining, logic modeling, and quality evaluation. Then, ScenGenerator generates multiple task scenarios and rule-based trajectory validation functions for each environment. With EnvScaler, we synthesize 191 environments and about 7K scenarios, and apply them to Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) for Qwen3 series models. Results on three benchmarks show that EnvScaler significantly improves LLMs' ability to solve tasks in complex environments involving multi-turn, multi-tool interactions. We release our code and data at https://github.com/RUC-NLPIR/EnvScaler.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EnvScaler：通过程序化合成实现大语言模型代理的可扩展工具交互环境
大型语言模型（LLMs）有望被训练为在各种现实环境中执行任务的代理，但这一过程依赖于丰富且多样的工具交互沙盒。然而，访问真实系统往往受限；LLM模拟环境易产生幻觉和不一致性；而手动构建的沙盒难以扩展。本文提出EnvScaler，一种通过程序化合成实现可扩展工具交互环境的自动化框架。EnvScaler包含两个组件：首先，SkelBuilder通过主题挖掘、逻辑建模和质量评估构建多样化的环境骨架；其次，ScenGenerator为每个环境生成多个任务场景及基于规则的轨迹验证函数。借助EnvScaler，我们合成了191个环境和约7000个场景，并将其应用于Qwen3系列模型的监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）。在三个基准测试中的结果表明，EnvScaler显著提升了LLM在涉及多轮、多工具交互的复杂环境中解决问题的能力。我们的代码和数据已发布于https://github.com/RUC-NLPIR/EnvScaler。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, realistic tool-interaction environments to train LLM agents, the authors introduce EnvScaler, an automated framework that synthesizes diverse environments via programmatic synthesis. It consists of SkelBuilder, which generates environment skeletons through topic mining and logic modeling, and ScenGenerator, which produces task scenarios and validation functions. Experimental results show that EnvScaler enables the creation of 191 environments and 7K scenarios, which when used in SFT and RL training for Qwen3 models, significantly enhance performance on three benchmarks involving multi-turn, multi-tool interactions.
为解决训练LLM代理所需大规模、真实工具交互环境的难题，EnvScaler通过程序化合成自动化构建环境：SkelBuilder通过主题挖掘、逻辑建模与质量评估生成多样化环境骨架，ScenGenerator则生成任务场景与轨迹验证函数。该框架合成191个环境与7000余个场景，应用于Qwen3系列模型的监督微调与强化学习，在三个基准测试中显著提升模型在多轮、多工具交互任务中的表现。
FL-MHSM: Spatially-adaptive Fusion and Ensemble Learning for Flood-Landslide Multi-Hazard Susceptibility Mapping at Regional Scale
Authors: Aswathi Mundayatt, Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair
First: 2026-04-17T17:23:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:23:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing multi-hazard susceptibility mapping (MHSM) studies often rely on spatially uniform models, treat hazards independently, and provide limited representation of cross-hazard dependence and uncertainty. To address these limitations, this study proposes a deep learning (DL) workflow for joint flood-landslide multi-hazard susceptibility mapping (FL-MHSM) that combines two-level spatial partitioning, probabilistic Early Fusion (EF), a tree-based Late Fusion (LF) baseline, and a soft-gating Mixture of Experts (MoE) model, with MoE serving as final predictive model. The proposed design preserves spatial heterogeneity through zonal partitions and enables data-parallel large-area prediction using overlapping lattice grids. In Kerala, EF remained competitive with LF, improving flood recall from 0.816 to 0.840 and reducing Brier score from 0.092 to 0.086, while MoE provided strongest performance for flood susceptibility, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.905, recall of 0.930, and F1-score of 0.722. In Nepal, EF similarly improved flood recall from 0.820 to 0.858 and reduced Brier score from 0.057 to 0.049 relative to LF, while MoE outperformed both EF and LF for landslide susceptibility, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.914, recall of 0.901, and F1-score of 0.559. GeoDetector analysis of MoE outputs further showed that dominant factors varied more across zones in Kerala, where susceptibility was shaped by different combinations of topographic, land-cover, and drainage-related controls, while Nepal showed a more consistent influence of topographic and glacier-related factors across zones. These findings show that EF and LF provide complementary predictive behavior, and that their spatially adaptive integration through MoE yields robust overall predictive performance for FL-MHSM while supporting interpretable characterization of multi-hazard susceptibility in spatially heterogeneous landscapes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FL-MHSM：面向区域尺度的洪水-滑坡多灾种易发性空间自适应融合与集成学习
现有多灾种易发性制图（MHSM）研究常依赖空间均匀模型，独立处理灾种，且对灾种间依赖关系和不确定性表征有限。为克服这些局限，本研究提出一种深度学习（DL）工作流，用于联合洪水-滑坡多灾种易发性制图（FL-MHSM），结合两级空间分区、概率早期融合（EF）、基于树的晚期融合（LF）基线模型，以及软门控专家混合（MoE）模型，MoE作为最终预测模型。该设计通过分区保留空间异质性，并利用重叠格网实现数据并行的大区域预测。在喀拉拉邦，EF与LF表现相当，将洪水召回率从0.816提升至0.840，Brier分数从0.092降至0.086；MoE在洪水易发性方面表现最优，AUC-ROC达0.905，召回率0.930，F1分数0.722。在尼泊尔，EF同样将洪水召回率从0.820提升至0.858，Brier分数从0.057降至0.049；MoE在滑坡易发性方面优于EF和LF，AUC-ROC达0.914，召回率0.901，F1分数0.559。MoE输出的地理探测器分析进一步表明，喀拉拉邦各分区主导因素变化较大，易发性受地形、土地覆盖及排水相关因子不同组合影响；而尼泊尔各分区则更一致受地形和冰川相关因子影响。研究结果表明，EF与LF具有互补预测行为，其通过MoE的空间自适应集成可实现FL-MHSM稳健的整体预测性能，并支持对空间异质性景观中多灾种易发性的可解释表征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of spatially uniform and hazard-independent models in multi-hazard susceptibility mapping, this study introduces FL-MHSM, a deep learning framework combining spatially adaptive two-level partitioning, probabilistic early fusion, tree-based late fusion, and a soft-gating mixture of experts model. The method enables large-area prediction while preserving spatial heterogeneity through overlapping lattice grids. Experiments in Kerala and Nepal show that early fusion improved flood recall and reduced Brier score compared to late fusion, while the mixture of experts model delivered the best performance overall: AUC-ROC of 0.905 and recall of 0.930 for flood susceptibility in Kerala, and AUC-ROC of 0.914 and recall of 0.901 for landslide susceptibility in Nepal. GeoDetector analysis further revealed that dominant susceptibility drivers varied spatially in Kerala but were more consistent in Nepal, highlighting the value of spatially adaptive fusion for interpretable multi-hazard mapping.
为解决现有多灾种易发性模型在空间上同质化、灾害独立处理及缺乏跨灾种依赖与不确定性表达的问题，本研究提出FL-MHSM深度学习框架，结合两级空间分区、概率早期融合、树基晚期融合基线及软门控专家混合模型作为最终预测器。在喀拉拉邦和尼泊尔的应用中，早期融合较晚期融合提升了洪灾召回率并降低Brier分数，而专家混合模型在洪灾与滑坡易发性预测中表现最优，AUC-ROC值高、召回率与F1分数优异。地理探测器分析显示，喀拉拉邦各区域主导因子随空间变化显著（地形、地被与水文因子组合不同），而尼泊尔则以地形与冰川因子为主导且空间一致性高，表明空间自适应融合可提升预测稳健性并支持异质性景观中的多灾种易发性可解释建模。
Information Router for Mitigating Modality Dominance in Vision-Language Models
Authors: Seulgi Kim, Mohit Prabhushankar, Ghassan AlRegib
First: 2026-04-17T17:20:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:20:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of benchmarks, yet they often suffer from modality dominance, where predictions rely disproportionately on a single modality. Prior approaches primarily address this issue by steering model's attention allocation, implicitly assuming that all modalities provide sufficient information. However, attention only determines where the model focuses, and cannot enrich information that is missing or ambiguous. In the real world, input modalities often differ in information density and their signal-to-noise ratios. In such cases, simply adjusting model's attention does not resolve the underlying lack of information. In this paper, we propose \textsc{MoIR}: \textit{Multi-modal Information Router}, an information-level fusion method that explicitly reduces information disparity prior to fusion. \textsc{MoIR} identifies less informative tokens and routes complementary information from a stronger modality, constructing information-dense token representations before they are processed by a large language model. By modifying information availability, \textsc{MoIR} enables reliable shifts in modality dominance, even when one modality is degraded. We evaluate \textsc{MoIR} on three widely used multi-modal benchmarks across multiple model backbones. Experimental results show that \textsc{MoIR} consistently demonstrates more balanced modality contribution, and improves robustness and downstream performance, particularly even under modality degradation. These findings demonstrate that explicitly modifying cross-modal information is an effective and complementary strategy for mitigating modality dominance in multi-modal reasoning models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于缓解视觉-语言模型中模态主导性的信息路由器
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多种基准测试中表现出色，但常受模态主导性影响，即预测过度依赖单一模态。此前方法主要通过引导模型注意力分配来解决，隐含假设所有模态均提供充足信息。然而，注意力仅决定模型关注位置，无法弥补缺失或模糊的信息。现实中，输入模态在信息密度和信噪比上常有差异。此时，仅调整注意力无法解决信息不足的根本问题。本文提出MoIR：多模态信息路由器，一种在融合前显式减少信息差异的信息级融合方法。MoIR识别信息较弱的token，并路由来自较强模态的互补信息，构建信息密集的token表示后再输入大语言模型。通过调整信息可用性，MoIR即使在某一模态退化时，也能可靠地改变模态主导性。我们在多个模型骨干结构上，于三个广泛使用的多模态基准测试中评估MoIR。实验表明，MoIR持续实现更均衡的模态贡献，提升鲁棒性与下游性能，尤其在模态退化时表现突出。这些发现表明，显式修改跨模态信息是缓解多模态推理模型中模态主导性的有效且互补策略。
Summary / 总结
To address modality dominance in vision-language models—where predictions overly rely on one modality—this paper introduces MoIR, a multi-modal information router that explicitly reduces information disparity before fusion. MoIR identifies underinformative tokens and routes complementary data from more informative modalities to construct denser representations prior to language model processing. Evaluated across three benchmarks with multiple backbones, MoIR consistently achieves more balanced modality contributions, enhances robustness under modality degradation, and improves downstream performance, demonstrating that information-level modification is a key strategy for mitigating dominance.
为缓解视觉-语言模型中模态主导问题（即预测过度依赖单一模态），本文提出MoIR（多模态信息路由器），在融合前显式减少模态间信息差异。该方法识别信息不足的token，并从信息更丰富的模态路由互补信息，构建信息密集的表示。在三个主流多模态基准上，MoIR显著提升模态贡献平衡性、增强模态退化下的鲁棒性，并提高下游性能，表明在信息层面进行调整是缓解模态主导的有效策略。
Semantic Area Graph Reasoning for Multi-Robot Language-Guided Search
Authors: Ruiyang Wang, Hao-Lun Hsu, Jiwoo Kim, Miroslav Pajic
First: 2026-04-17T17:19:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:19:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coordinating multi-robot systems (MRS) to search in unknown environments is particularly challenging for tasks that require semantic reasoning beyond geometric exploration. Classical coordination strategies rely on frontier coverage or information gain and cannot incorporate high-level task intent, such as searching for objects associated with specific room types. We propose \textit{Semantic Area Graph Reasoning} (SAGR), a hierarchical framework that enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-robot exploration and semantic search through a structured semantic-topological abstraction of the environment. SAGR incrementally constructs a semantic area graph from a semantic occupancy map, encoding room instances, connectivity, frontier availability, and robot states into a compact task-relevant representation for LLM reasoning. The LLM performs high-level semantic room assignment based on spatial structure and task context, while deterministic frontier planning and local navigation handle geometric execution within assigned rooms. Experiments on the Habitat-Matterport3D dataset across 100 scenarios show that SAGR remains competitive with state-of-the-art exploration methods while consistently improving semantic target search efficiency, with up to 18.8\% in large environments. These results highlight the value of structured semantic abstractions as an effective interface between LLM-based reasoning and multi-robot coordination in complex indoor environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多机器人语言引导搜索的语义区域图推理
在未知环境中协调多机器人系统（MRS）进行搜索，尤其在需要超越几何探索的语义推理任务中极具挑战性。传统协调策略依赖前沿覆盖或信息增益，无法融入高层任务意图，如搜索特定房间类型关联的对象。我们提出语义区域图推理（SAGR），一种分层框架，通过环境的结构化语义-拓扑抽象，使大语言模型（LLMs）协调多机器人探索与语义搜索。SAGR 从语义占据图增量构建语义区域图，将房间实例、连通性、前沿可用性及机器人状态编码为紧凑的任务相关表示，供 LLM 推理。LLM 根据空间结构与任务上下文执行高层语义房间分配，而确定性前沿规划与局部导航则在分配房间内处理几何执行。在 Habitat-Matterport3D 数据集的 100 个场景实验中，SAGR 在保持与最先进探索方法竞争力的同时，持续提升语义目标搜索效率，大型环境中最高提升 18.8%。结果凸显了结构化语义抽象作为 LLM 推理与复杂室内多机器人协调之间有效接口的价值。
Summary / 总结
To enable multi-robot systems to perform semantic search in unknown indoor environments, this paper introduces Semantic Area Graph Reasoning (SAGR), a hierarchical framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to coordinate exploration and target search through a structured semantic-topological abstraction. SAGR incrementally builds a semantic area graph from a semantic occupancy map, encoding room connectivity, frontier availability, and robot states into a compact representation for LLM reasoning. The LLM assigns semantic tasks to robots based on spatial structure and task context, while deterministic planners handle local navigation. Experiments on 100 Habitat-Matterport3D scenarios demonstrate that SAGR matches or exceeds state-of-the-art exploration methods while improving semantic search efficiency by up to 18.8% in large environments.
为解决多机器人系统在未知环境中执行超越几何探索的语义搜索任务的挑战，本文提出语义区域图推理（SAGR）框架，利用大语言模型通过结构化的语义拓扑抽象协调探索。SAGR从语义占据图中逐步构建语义区域图，将房间连接性、前沿可用性及机器人状态编码为紧凑的推理表示。大语言模型根据空间结构与任务上下文分配语义任务，而确定性规划器负责局部导航。在100个Habitat-Matterport3D场景实验中，SAGR在保持与最先进探索方法相当性能的同时，大幅提升了语义目标搜索效率，大型环境中的效率提升最高达18.8%。
Scaling Behaviors of LLM Reinforcement Learning Post-Training: An Empirical Study in Mathematical Reasoning
Authors: Zelin Tan, Hejia Geng, Xiaohang Yu, Mulei Zhang, Guancheng Wan, Yifan Zhou, Qiang He, Xiangyuan Xue, Heng Zhou, Yutao Fan, Zhongzhi Li, Zaibin Zhang, Guibin Zhang, Chen Zhang, Zhenfei Yin, Philip Torr, Lei Bai
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-09-29T17:10:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:19:41+00:00
Comments: V4 version:This Paper has been accepted by ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While scaling laws for large language models (LLMs) during pre-training have been extensively studied, their behavior under reinforcement learning (RL) post-training remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic empirical investigation of scaling behaviors in RL-based post-training, with a particular focus on mathematical reasoning. Based on a set of experiments across the full Qwen2.5 dense model series (0.5B to 72B), we characterize how model scale, data volume, and computational budget interact to shape performance. Our analysis leads to four key findings: 1. Larger models consistently exhibit superior learning efficiency on both compute and data metrics. 2. The relationship between test loss, compute, and data can be modeled by a predictive power-law which is robust across both base and instruction-tuned models. 3. Although larger models exhibit higher learning efficiency, the analytical learning efficiency term k(N) in the power-law reveals a latent saturation trend in learning efficiency as model size continues to increase. 4. In data-constrained regimes, repeated reuse of high-quality data proves highly effective, as final performance is primarily governed by the total number of optimization steps rather than the uniqueness of samples. Collectively, these results provide a principled foundation and practical guidelines for efficiently scaling the reasoning capabilities of LLMs through RL post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型强化学习后训练的缩放行为：数学推理中的实证研究
尽管大语言模型（LLM）在预训练阶段的缩放规律已得到广泛研究，其在强化学习（RL）后训练中的行为仍鲜有探索。本文系统实证研究了基于RL的后训练缩放行为，特别聚焦于数学推理。基于在完整Qwen2.5稠密模型系列（0.5B至72B）上的多组实验，我们刻画了模型规模、数据量与计算预算如何交互影响性能。分析得出四项关键发现：1. 更大模型在计算与数据指标上均表现出更优的学习效率。2. 测试损失、计算量与数据量之间的关系可由稳健的幂律模型预测，适用于基础模型与指令调优模型。3. 尽管更大模型学习效率更高，幂律中的分析学习效率项k(N)揭示了随着模型规模持续增大，学习效率存在潜在饱和趋势。4. 在数据受限场景下，高质量数据的重复利用效果显著，最终性能主要由优化步数总量决定，而非样本独特性。综合而言，这些结果为通过RL后训练高效扩展LLM推理能力提供了理论基础与实践指导。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how scaling laws behave in reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs), particularly in mathematical reasoning, where prior research focused on pre-training. Using the Qwen2.5 dense model series from 0.5B to 72B parameters, the authors empirically analyze the interplay between model size, data volume, and compute budget. They find that larger models consistently learn more efficiently in both compute and data terms, and that test loss, compute, and data follow a robust power-law relationship across model variants. However, the learning efficiency parameter k(N) in this power-law shows a saturation trend as model size grows, suggesting diminishing returns. Additionally, in data-limited settings, performance is dominated by the total number of optimization steps rather than data diversity, highlighting the value of reusing high-quality data.
该研究探讨了大语言模型在强化学习后训练阶段的缩放规律，尤其聚焦于数学推理任务，此前研究主要集中在预训练阶段。通过对Qwen2.5系列模型（0.5B至72B参数）的实证分析，作者考察了模型规模、数据量与计算预算之间的相互作用。结果表明，更大模型在计算与数据效率上表现更优，测试损失、计算量与数据量之间呈现稳健的幂律关系，适用于基础模型与指令微调模型。然而，学习效率参数k(N)随模型增大趋于饱和，暗示规模增长存在边际收益递减。在数据受限场景下，性能主要由优化步数决定，而非样本多样性，重用高质量数据效果显著。这些发现为通过强化学习后训练高效提升模型推理能力提供了理论基础与实践指导。
SwanNLP at SemEval-2026 Task 5: An LLM-based Framework for Plausibility Scoring in Narrative Word Sense Disambiguation
Authors: Deshan Sumanathilaka, Nicholas Micallef, Julian Hough, Saman Jayasinghe
First: 2026-04-17T17:18:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:18:51+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 5 Tables, 1 figure, Accepted to SemEval 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in language models have substantially improved Natural Language Understanding (NLU). Although widely used benchmarks suggest that Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively disambiguate, their practical applicability in real-world narrative contexts remains underexplored. SemEval-2026 Task 5 addresses this gap by introducing a task that predicts the human-perceived plausibility of a word sense within a short story. In this work, we propose an LLM-based framework for plausibility scoring of homonymous word senses in narrative texts using a structured reasoning mechanism. We examine the impact of fine-tuning low-parameter LLMs with diverse reasoning strategies, alongside dynamic few-shot prompting for large-parameter models, on accurate sense identification and plausibility estimation. Our results show that commercial large-parameter LLMs with dynamic few-shot prompting closely replicate human-like plausibility judgments. Furthermore, model ensembling slightly improves performance, better simulating the agreement patterns of five human annotators compared to single-model predictions
中文标题/摘要
标题：SwanNLP参加SemEval-2026任务5：基于大语言模型的叙事词义消歧中合理性评分框架
近年来语言模型的进展显著提升了自然语言理解（NLU）能力。尽管广泛使用的基准测试表明大语言模型（LLMs）能有效进行词义消歧，但其在真实叙事语境中的实际应用仍待探索。SemEval-2026任务5填补了这一空白，通过预测短篇故事中词义的人类感知合理性来开展研究。本文提出一种基于LLM的框架，利用结构化推理机制对叙事文本中的同义词义进行合理性评分。我们研究了低参数LLM通过多样化推理策略微调，以及大参数模型动态少样本提示对准确词义识别和合理性估计的影响。结果表明，采用动态少样本提示的商用大参数LLM能近似人类的合理性判断。此外，模型集成略微提升性能，比单模型预测更贴近五名人类标注者的一致性模式。

为评估大语言模型在叙事语境中判断词义合理性的能力，本文提出一种基于LLM的框架，通过结构化推理对短篇故事中的同义词词义合理性进行评分。方法上，研究对比了低参数模型的微调策略与大参数模型的动态少样本提示技术，以提升词义识别与合理性评估效果。实验表明，采用动态少样本提示的大参数模型能较好模拟人类对词义合理性的判断，且模型集成策略进一步提升了性能，更贴近五位人类标注者之间的共识模式。
Beyond Distribution Sharpening: The Importance of Task Rewards
Authors: Sarthak Mittal, Leo Gagnon, Guillaume Lajoie
First: 2026-04-17T17:17:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:17:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier models have demonstrated exceptional capabilities following the integration of task-reward-based reinforcement learning (RL) into their training pipelines, enabling systems to evolve from pure reasoning models into sophisticated agents. However, debate persists regarding whether RL genuinely instills new skills within a base model or merely sharpens its existing distribution to elicit latent capabilities. To address this dichotomy, we present an explicit comparison between distribution sharpening and task-reward-based learning, utilizing RL as a tool to implement both paradigms. Our analysis reveals the inherent limitations of distribution sharpening, demonstrating from first principles how and why the optima can be unfavorable and the approach fundamentally unstable. Furthermore, our experiments using Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on math datasets confirm that sharpening yields limited gains, whereas incorporating task-based reward signal can greatly help achieve robust performance improvements and stable learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越分布锐化：任务奖励的重要性
前沿模型在训练流程中整合基于任务奖励的强化学习（RL）后，展现出卓越能力，使系统从纯推理模型演变为复杂智能体。然而，关于RL是否真正赋予基础模型新技能，还是仅锐化其现有分布以激发潜在能力，仍存争议。为解决这一二分法，我们明确对比分布锐化与基于任务奖励的学习，利用RL工具实现两种范式。分析表明，分布锐化存在固有局限，从第一性原理阐明其最优解为何不利且方法本质不稳定。此外，我们在数学数据集上使用Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507的实验表明，锐化收益有限，而引入基于任务的奖励信号可显著提升性能并实现稳定学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the debate over whether reinforcement learning (RL) genuinely enhances model capabilities or merely sharpens existing distributions, this study explicitly contrasts distribution sharpening with task-reward-based RL using RL as a controlled implementation tool. Theoretical analysis reveals that distribution sharpening suffers from inherent instability and suboptimal convergence due to its lack of task alignment, while task-reward-based learning provides a principled mechanism for guiding model evolution. Experimental results on math datasets using Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 show that sharpening yields minimal performance gains, whereas task-reward signals consistently drive significant and stable improvements.
为厘清强化学习是否真正赋予模型新能力或仅是强化其已有分布，本研究通过RL工具显式对比分布锐化与任务奖励驱动学习。理论分析表明，分布锐化存在固有不稳定性，易收敛至不利最优解，而任务奖励学习则更稳定有效。在数学数据集上对Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct及Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507的实验显示，锐化效果有限，而引入任务奖励显著提升性能与鲁棒性。
TokenLight: Precise Lighting Control in Images using Attribute Tokens
Authors: Sumit Chaturvedi, Yannick Hold-Geoffroy, Mengwei Ren, Jingyuan Liu, He Zhang, Yiqun Mei, Julie Dorsey, Zhixin Shu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:17:33+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, CVPR 2026, Project Page: https://vrroom.github.io/tokenlight/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper presents a method for image relighting that enables precise and continuous control over multiple illumination attributes in a photograph. We formulate relighting as a conditional image generation task and introduce attribute tokens to encode distinct lighting factors such as intensity, color, ambient illumination, diffuse level, and 3D light positions. The model is trained on a large-scale synthetic dataset with ground-truth lighting annotations, supplemented by a small set of real captures to enhance realism and generalization. We validate our approach across a variety of relighting tasks, including controlling in-scene lighting fixtures and editing environment illumination using virtual light sources, on synthetic and real images. Our method achieves state-of-the-art quantitative and qualitative performance compared to prior work. Remarkably, without explicit inverse rendering supervision, the model exhibits an inherent understanding of how light interacts with scene geometry, occlusion, and materials, yielding convincing lighting effects even in traditionally challenging scenarios such as placing lights within objects or relighting transparent materials plausibly. Project page: vrroom.github.io/tokenlight/
中文标题/摘要
标题：TokenLight：使用属性令牌实现图像中的精确光照控制
本文提出了一种图像重光照方法，可对照片中的多个光照属性进行精确且连续的控制。我们将重光照建模为条件图像生成任务，并引入属性令牌以编码不同光照因素，如强度、颜色、环境光照、漫反射程度和3D光源位置。模型在带有真实光照标注的大规模合成数据集上训练，并辅以少量真实拍摄数据以增强真实感和泛化能力。我们在合成和真实图像上验证了该方法在多种重光照任务中的表现，包括控制场景内灯光设备和使用虚拟光源编辑环境光照。与先前工作相比，我们的方法在定量和定性指标上均达到最先进水平。值得注意的是，无需显式的逆渲染监督，模型天然理解了光与场景几何、遮挡和材质的相互作用，即使在传统上具有挑战性的场景（如将光源置于物体内部或合理重光照透明材质）中也能生成令人信服的光照效果。项目页面：vrroom.github.io/tokenlight/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise and continuous control over lighting attributes in images, this work introduces TokenLight, a method that treats relighting as a conditional image generation task using attribute tokens to encode lighting factors like intensity, color, and 3D light positions. Trained on a large synthetic dataset with ground-truth lighting annotations and augmented with real images, the model learns to generate realistic relit scenes without explicit inverse rendering supervision. Experimental results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across synthetic and real-world relighting tasks, including editing in-scene lights and handling challenging cases like lighting within objects or transparent materials, showing strong qualitative and quantitative results.
为实现对图像中多种光照属性的精确连续控制，该研究提出TokenLight方法，将重光照建模为条件图像生成任务，通过属性标记编码光照强度、颜色及3D光源位置等要素。模型在带真实光照标注的合成数据集上训练，并辅以少量真实图像提升真实感与泛化能力，无需显式逆渲染监督即可学习光照与场景几何、遮挡及材质的交互关系。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实图像上的定量与定性表现均优于现有方法，尤其在复杂场景如光源置于物体内部或透明材质重光照中仍能生成可信的光照效果。
OXtal: An All-Atom Diffusion Model for Organic Crystal Structure Prediction
Authors: Emily Jin, Andrei Cristian Nica, Mikhail Galkin, Jarrid Rector-Brooks, Kin Long Kelvin Lee, Santiago Miret, Frances H. Arnold, Michael Bronstein, Avishek Joey Bose, Alexander Tong, Cheng-Hao Liu
First: 2025-12-07T20:46:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:16:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurately predicting experimentally realizable 3D molecular crystal structures from their 2D chemical graphs is a long-standing open challenge in computational chemistry called crystal structure prediction (CSP). Efficiently solving this problem has implications ranging from pharmaceuticals to organic semiconductors, as crystal packing directly governs the physical and chemical properties of organic solids. In this paper, we introduce OXtal, a large-scale 100M parameter all-atom diffusion model that directly learns the conditional joint distribution over intramolecular conformations and periodic packing. To efficiently scale OXtal, we abandon explicit equivariant architectures imposing inductive bias arising from crystal symmetries in favor of data augmentation strategies. We further propose a novel crystallization-inspired lattice-free training scheme, Stoichiometric Stochastic Shell Sampling ($S^4$), that efficiently captures long-range interactions while sidestepping explicit lattice parametrization -- thus enabling more scalable architectural choices at all-atom resolution. By leveraging a large dataset of 600K experimentally validated crystal structures (including rigid and flexible molecules, co-crystals, and solvates), OXtal achieves orders-of-magnitude improvements over prior ab initio machine learning CSP methods, while remaining orders of magnitude cheaper than traditional quantum-chemical approaches. Specifically, OXtal recovers experimental structures with conformer $\text{RMSD}_1<0.5$ Å and attains over 80\% packing similarity rate, demonstrating its ability to model both thermodynamic and kinetic regularities of molecular crystallization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OXtal：用于有机晶体结构预测的全原子扩散模型
从其二维化学图准确预测可实验实现的三维分子晶体结构，是计算化学中长期存在的开放挑战，称为晶体结构预测（CSP）。高效解决该问题对药物和有机半导体等领域具有重要意义，因为晶体堆积直接决定有机固体的物理和化学性质。本文提出OXtal，这是一个拥有1亿参数的全原子扩散模型，直接学习分子内构象与周期性堆积的条件联合分布。为高效扩展OXtal，我们摒弃了显式等变架构（其引入了源于晶体对称性的归纳偏置），转而采用数据增强策略。我们进一步提出了一种受结晶启发的无晶格训练方案——化学计量随机壳采样（$S^4$），该方案高效捕捉长程相互作用，同时避免显式晶格参数化，从而在全原子分辨率下支持更可扩展的架构选择。通过利用包含刚性和柔性分子、共晶和溶剂化物在内的60万组实验验证晶体结构的大数据集，OXtal在性能上比先前的从头算机器学习CSP方法提升数个数量级，同时比传统量子化学方法便宜数个数量级。具体而言，OXtal恢复的实验结构构象$\text{RMSD}_1<0.5$ Å，堆积相似率超过80%，展示了其对分子结晶热力学与动力学规律的建模能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to predict experimentally realizable 3D crystal structures from 2D chemical graphs for applications in pharmaceuticals and organic semiconductors, OXtal introduces a large-scale all-atom diffusion model with 100M parameters that jointly models intramolecular conformations and periodic packing. To scale efficiently, it avoids equivariant architectures relying on crystal symmetries and instead uses data augmentation, along with a novel lattice-free training method called Stoichiometric Stochastic Shell Sampling ($S^4$) to capture long-range interactions without explicit lattice parametrization. Evaluated on a 600K dataset of experimentally validated crystals, OXtal outperforms prior machine learning CSP methods by orders of magnitude in accuracy and is far more computationally efficient than quantum-chemical methods, achieving conformer RMSD<0.5 Å and over 80% packing similarity rate.
为解决从二维化学图预测可实验实现的三维分子晶体结构这一长期挑战（对药物和有机半导体至关重要），本文提出OXtal，一个拥有1亿参数的全原子扩散模型，直接联合建模分子构象与周期性堆积。为实现高效扩展，该模型摒弃了依赖晶体对称性的等变架构，转而采用数据增强策略，并引入一种新颖的无晶格训练方法——化学计量随机壳采样（$S^4$），以捕捉长程相互作用而无需显式晶格参数化。在包含60万实验验证晶体结构的数据集上测试，OXtal在性能上比先前的机器学习CSP方法提升数个数量级，且成本远低于传统量子化学方法，能恢复实验结构的构象RMSD1<0.5Å，堆积相似率超过80%。
Unsupervised domain adaptation for radioisotope identification in gamma spectroscopy
Authors: Peter Lalor, Ayush Panigrahy, Alex Hagen
First: 2026-03-05T22:19:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:16:41+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 5 figures, and 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training machine learning models for radioisotope identification using gamma spectroscopy remains an elusive challenge for many practical applications, largely stemming from the difficulty of acquiring and labeling large, diverse experimental datasets. Simulations can mitigate this challenge, but the accuracy of models trained on simulated data can deteriorate substantially when deployed to an out-of-distribution operational environment. In this study, we demonstrate that unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) can improve the ability of a model trained on synthetic data to generalize to a new testing domain, provided unlabeled data from the target domain is available. Conventional supervised techniques are unable to utilize this data because the absence of isotope labels precludes defining a supervised classification loss. We compare a range of different UDA techniques, finding that feature alignment strategies, particularly via maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) minimization or domain-adversarial training, yield the most consistent improvement to testing scores. For instance, using a custom transformer-based neural network, we achieve a testing accuracy of $0.904 \pm 0.022$ on an experimental LaBr$_3$ test set after performing unsupervised feature alignment via MMD minimization, compared to $0.754 \pm 0.014$ before alignment. Overall, our results highlight the potential of using UDA to adapt a radioisotope classifier trained on synthetic data for real-world deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于伽马谱学中放射性同位素识别的无监督域自适应
利用伽马谱学训练放射性同位素识别的机器学习模型在许多实际应用中仍具挑战性，主要源于获取和标注大规模、多样化的实验数据集困难。仿真可缓解此问题，但基于仿真数据训练的模型在部署到分布外的实际环境中时，其准确性可能大幅下降。本研究证明，若目标域存在未标注数据，无监督域自适应（UDA）可提升在合成数据上训练的模型对新测试域的泛化能力。传统监督方法无法利用此类数据，因缺乏同位素标签而无法定义监督分类损失。我们比较了多种UDA技术，发现特征对齐策略（特别是通过最大均值差异MMD最小化或域对抗训练）能最稳定地提升测试得分。例如，使用自定义的基于Transformer的神经网络，经MMD最小化无监督特征对齐后，在实验LaBr₃测试集上测试准确率达0.904±0.022，而对齐前仅为0.754±0.014。总体而言，我们的结果凸显了UDA在将基于合成数据训练的放射性同位素分类器适配于实际部署中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of training radioisotope identification models using gamma spectroscopy due to scarce labeled real-world data, this study explores unsupervised domain adaptation to bridge the gap between synthetic training data and real operational environments. The method leverages unlabeled target-domain data to align feature distributions via techniques like maximum mean discrepancy minimization or domain-adversarial training, enabling generalization without supervision. Experimental results show that applying MMD-based alignment to a transformer model boosts testing accuracy from 0.754 ± 0.014 to 0.904 ± 0.022 on a LaBr₃ dataset, demonstrating the effectiveness of UDA in improving real-world performance.
为解决因真实数据标注稀缺导致的伽马谱学放射性同位素识别模型训练难题，本研究提出无监督域自适应方法，利用目标域无标签数据对合成训练数据与真实环境进行特征分布对齐，从而提升模型泛化能力。实验采用自定义Transformer网络，通过最大均值差异最小化方法实现域对齐，测试准确率从0.754±0.014显著提升至0.904±0.022，验证了无监督域自适应在实际部署中的有效性。
Characterising LLM-Generated Competency Questions: a Cross-Domain Empirical Study using Open and Closed Models
Authors: Reham Alharbi, Valentina Tamma, Terry R. Payne, Jacopo de Berardinis
First: 2026-04-17T17:16:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:16:32+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2507.02989
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competency Questions (CQs) are a cornerstone of requirement elicitation in ontology engineering. CQs represent requirements as a set of natural language questions that an ontology should satisfy; they are traditionally modelled by ontology engineers together with domain experts as part of a human-centred, manual elicitation process. The use of Generative AI automates CQ creation at scale, therefore democratising the process of generation, widening stakeholder engagement, and ultimately broadening access to ontology engineering. However, given the large and heterogeneous landscape of LLMs, varying in dimensions such as parameter scale, task and domain specialisation, and accessibility, it is crucial to characterise and understand the intrinsic, observable properties of the CQs they produce (e.g., readability, structural complexity) through a systematic, cross-domain analysis. This paper introduces a set of quantitative measures for the systematic comparison of CQs across multiple dimensions. Using CQs generated from well defined use cases and scenarios, we identify their salient properties, including readability, relevance with respect to the input text and structural complexity of the generated questions. We conduct our experiments over a set of use cases and requirements using a range of LLMs, including both open (KimiK2-1T, LLama3.1-8B, LLama3.2-3B) and closed models (Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT 4.1). Our analysis demonstrates that LLM performance reflects distinct generation profiles shaped by the use case.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用开放与闭源模型进行跨领域实证研究，表征大语言模型生成的能力问题
能力问题（CQs）是本体工程需求获取的核心。CQs将需求表示为本体应满足的一组自然语言问题，传统上由本体工程师与领域专家共同手动完成，属于以人为核心的获取过程。生成式AI的应用可规模化自动化创建CQs，从而民主化生成过程，扩大利益相关者参与，最终提升对本体工程的可及性。然而，鉴于大语言模型（LLMs）种类繁多、异质性强（如参数规模、任务与领域专精度、可访问性等不同），有必要通过系统性的跨领域分析，表征并理解其生成CQs的内在、可观测属性（如可读性、结构复杂度）。本文提出一套用于多维度系统比较CQs的量化指标。我们基于明确定义的用例与场景生成CQs，识别其显著属性，包括可读性、与输入文本的相关性及生成问题的结构复杂度。实验在多个用例与需求上进行，涵盖多种LLMs，包括开放模型（KimiK2-1T、LLama3.1-8B、LLama3.2-3B）与闭源模型（Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT 4.1）。分析表明，LLM性能反映其生成模式，且受用例影响显著。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the need to systematically evaluate competency questions (CQs) generated by large language models (LLMs) across diverse domains, as manual CQ creation is traditionally labor-intensive and limited in scale. Using a controlled set of use cases and multiple LLMs—including open models like KimiK2-1T and closed models like GPT-4.1—they analyze how different model architectures and domain specializations influence CQ quality. Results show that LLMs exhibit distinct generation profiles depending on the use case, with some models producing more readable or semantically aligned questions than others, highlighting the importance of model selection and context alignment in automated ontology engineering.
该研究旨在系统评估大语言模型（LLM）在不同领域生成的胜任力问题（CQ），以应对生成式AI在本体工程中自动化需求提取的兴起。作者提出量化指标，从可读性、与输入文本的相关性及结构复杂度等维度评估CQ。通过控制用例和多种LLM（包括开源模型如KimiK2-1T和闭源模型如GPT-4.1）进行实验，发现不同模型因架构和训练目标差异，生成的CQ具有明显不同特征，表明模型选择对本体需求提取的可靠性至关重要。
The Reasoning Trap: How Enhancing LLM Reasoning Amplifies Tool Hallucination
Authors: Chenlong Yin, Zeyang Sha, Shiwen Cui, Changhua Meng, Zechao Li
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-10-27T03:58:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:15:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) is a key strategy for building Agents that "think then act." However, recent observations, like OpenAI's o3, suggest a paradox: stronger reasoning often coincides with increased hallucination, yet no prior work has systematically examined whether reasoning enhancement itself causes tool hallucination. To address this gap, we pose the central question: Does strengthening reasoning increase tool hallucination? To answer this, we introduce SimpleToolHalluBench, a diagnostic benchmark measuring tool hallucination in two failure modes: (i) no tool available, and (ii) only distractor tools available. Through controlled experiments, we establish three key findings. First, we demonstrate a causal relationship: progressively enhancing reasoning through RL increases tool hallucination proportionally with task performance gains. Second, this effect transcends overfitting - training on non-tool tasks (e.g., mathematics) still amplifies subsequent tool hallucination. Third, the effect is method-agnostic, appearing when reasoning is instilled via supervised fine-tuning and when it is merely elicited at inference by switching from direct answers to step-by-step thinking. We also evaluate mitigation strategies including Prompt Engineering and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), revealing a fundamental reliability-capability trade-off: reducing hallucination consistently degrades utility. Mechanistically, Reasoning RL disproportionately collapses tool-reliability-related representations, and hallucinations surface as amplified divergences concentrated in late-layer residual streams. These findings reveal that current reasoning enhancement methods inherently amplify tool hallucination, highlighting the need for new training objectives that jointly optimize for capability and reliability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理陷阱：增强大语言模型推理能力会加剧工具幻觉
增强大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力是构建“先思考再行动”代理的关键策略。然而，近期观察（如OpenAI的o3）表明存在悖论：更强的推理能力往往伴随更高的幻觉率，但此前研究尚未系统探讨推理增强本身是否导致工具幻觉。为填补这一空白，我们提出核心问题：增强推理是否增加工具幻觉？为此，我们引入SimpleToolHalluBench诊断基准，测量工具幻觉在两种失败模式下的表现：（i）无可用工具，（ii）仅存在干扰工具。通过受控实验，我们得出三项关键发现：第一，因果关系成立：通过强化学习逐步增强推理能力，其工具幻觉率与任务性能提升成比例增长；第二，该效应超越过拟合——即使在非工具任务（如数学）上训练，仍会加剧后续工具幻觉；第三，该效应方法无关，无论推理是通过监督微调植入，还是仅在推理时通过从直接回答切换为逐步思考激发，均会出现。我们还评估了提示工程与直接偏好优化（DPO）等缓解策略，发现存在根本性的可靠性-能力权衡：降低幻觉会持续损害实用性。机制上，推理强化学习会不成比例地压缩与工具可靠性相关的表征，幻觉表现为集中在后期残差流中的放大偏差。这些发现揭示当前推理增强方法固有地放大工具幻觉，凸显亟需开发同时优化能力与可靠性的新训练目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that enhancing LLM reasoning often correlates with increased tool hallucination, this study investigates whether reasoning improvement itself causes this phenomenon. Using SimpleToolHalluBench, a benchmark measuring hallucination under two failure modes, the authors conduct controlled experiments showing that reasoning enhancement via RL causally increases tool hallucination in proportion to performance gains. This effect persists even when training on non-tool tasks and across different reasoning induction methods, including supervised fine-tuning and inference-time step-by-step prompting. Mitigation strategies like prompt engineering and DPO reduce hallucination but at the cost of utility, revealing a reliability-capability trade-off. Mechanistically, reasoning RL disproportionately weakens tool-reliability representations, causing hallucinations to manifest as amplified divergences in late-layer residual streams.
鉴于增强大语言模型推理能力常伴随工具幻觉增加的现象，本研究探讨推理增强本身是否导致该问题。通过SimpleToolHalluBench诊断基准（在两种故障模式下测量幻觉），实验表明：通过强化学习逐步增强推理会与性能提升成比例地增加工具幻觉，且该效应在非工具任务训练和不同推理诱导方法（如监督微调或推理时逐步思考）中均存在。缓解策略如提示工程和DPO虽可降低幻觉，但会牺牲实用性，揭示可靠性与能力间的权衡。机制上，推理强化学习会不成比例地削弱工具可靠性表征，幻觉表现为后期残差流中的放大偏差。
Do Vision-Language Models Truly Perform Vision Reasoning? A Rigorous Study of the Modality Gap
Authors: Yige Xu, Yongjie Wang, Zizhuo Wu, Kaisong Song, Jun Lin, Zhiqi Shen
First: 2026-04-17T17:15:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:15:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs) has recently attracted significant attention due to its broad applicability across diverse downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether the superior performance of VLMs stems from genuine vision-grounded reasoning or relies predominantly on the reasoning capabilities of their textual backbones. To systematically measure this, we introduce CrossMath, a novel multimodal reasoning benchmark designed for controlled cross-modal comparisons. Specifically, we construct each problem in text-only, image-only, and image+text formats guaranteeing identical task-relevant information, verified by human annotators. This rigorous alignment effectively isolates modality-specific reasoning differences while eliminating confounding factors such as information mismatch. Extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a consistent phenomenon: a substantial performance gap between textual and visual reasoning. Notably, VLMs excel with text-only inputs, whereas incorporating visual data (image+text) frequently degrades performance compared to the text-only baseline. These findings indicate that current VLMs conduct reasoning primarily in the textual space, with limited genuine reliance on visual evidence. To mitigate this limitation, we curate a CrossMath training set for VLM fine-tuning. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that fine-tuning on this training set significantly boosts reasoning performance across all individual and joint modalities, while yielding robust gains on two general visual reasoning tasks. Source code is available at https://github.com/xuyige/CrossMath.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言模型真的能进行视觉推理吗？一种模态差距的严谨研究
由于视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多种下游任务中的广泛应用，其推理能力近期备受关注。然而，尚不清楚VLMs的优越性能是源于真正的视觉基础推理，还是主要依赖其文本骨干的推理能力。为系统衡量这一问题，我们提出了CrossMath，一个专为受控跨模态比较设计的新多模态推理基准。具体而言，我们以文本仅、图像仅和图像+文本三种格式构建每个问题，确保任务相关信息完全一致，并经人工标注员验证。这种严谨对齐有效隔离了模态特异性推理差异，同时消除了信息不匹配等干扰因素。对当前最先进VLMs的广泛评估揭示了一个一致现象：文本推理与视觉推理之间存在显著性能差距。值得注意的是，VLMs在仅文本输入时表现优异，而加入视觉数据（图像+文本）时，其性能往往较仅文本基线下降。这些发现表明，当前VLMs主要在文本空间中进行推理，对视觉证据的依赖有限。为缓解这一局限，我们为VLM微调精心构建了CrossMath训练集。实证评估表明，使用该训练集微调可显著提升所有单模态和联合模态的推理性能，并在两项通用视觉推理任务上带来稳健提升。源代码见https://github.com/xuyige/CrossMath。

This study investigates whether vision-language models (VLMs) genuinely perform vision-based reasoning or merely leverage their textual components. To isolate modality-specific reasoning, the authors introduce CrossMath, a benchmark with problems presented in text-only, image-only, and image+text formats, ensuring identical task-relevant information. Evaluations across state-of-the-art VLMs reveal a consistent pattern: models perform best with text-only inputs, and adding visual data often reduces performance. This suggests VLMs primarily reason in the textual space, with minimal reliance on visual evidence. The authors then fine-tune models on a curated CrossMath training set, which significantly improves reasoning across all modalities and general visual reasoning tasks.
该研究旨在探究视觉-语言模型（VLMs）是否真正进行视觉推理，还是主要依赖其文本组件。作者设计了CrossMath基准，将问题以纯文本、纯图像和图文结合三种形式呈现，确保任务相关信息一致。评估发现，VLMs在纯文本输入下表现最佳，加入视觉信息后性能常下降，表明模型主要在文本空间进行推理，对视觉证据依赖有限。使用专门构建的CrossMath训练集微调后，模型在所有模态及通用视觉推理任务上均有显著性能提升。
Efficient Continual Learning in Language Models via Thalamically Routed Cortical Columns
Authors: Afshin Khadangi
First: 2026-02-25T23:38:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:13:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models deployed in the wild must adapt to evolving data, user behavior, and task mixtures without erasing previously acquired capabilities. In practice, this remains difficult: sequential updates induce catastrophic forgetting, while many stabilization methods rely on external procedures that are costly, brittle, or difficult to scale. We present TRC$^{2}$ (Thalamically Routed Cortical Columns), a decoder-only architecture that makes continual learning a property of the backbone itself. TRC$^{2}$ combines stacked cortical columns with a thalamic modulatory pathway for selective inter-column communication and a hippocampal pathway for event selective retrieval, delayed surprise-based writing, and replay-driven consolidation. This design localizes fast plasticity while preserving a slower stable computation pathway. We further introduce a causal memory-update scheme and an online replay controller that adjusts consolidation strength from measured forgetting. Across a task-sequential language-modeling stream over C4, WikiText-103, and GSM8K, TRC$^{2}$ consistently improves task-boundary modeling quality and substantially reduces cumulative forgetting relative to Transformer, Mamba, MoE, DeepSeek and continual learning baselines trained under the same pipeline. Ablations show that the thalamic and hippocampal components are central to the retention gains, while the full model remains competitive in throughput and training cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过丘脑路由皮层柱实现语言模型中的高效持续学习
部署在实际环境中的大型语言模型必须在不丢失先前获得能力的前提下，适应不断变化的数据、用户行为和任务混合。然而，实践中这仍很困难：顺序更新会导致灾难性遗忘，而许多稳定化方法依赖于外部程序，成本高、脆弱或难以扩展。我们提出了TRC²（丘脑路由皮层柱），一种仅解码器架构，使持续学习成为其骨干网络的固有属性。TRC²结合堆叠的皮层柱与丘脑调控通路，实现皮层柱间的有选择性通信，以及海马通路用于事件选择性检索、基于延迟惊喜的写入和回放驱动的巩固。该设计将快速可塑性局部化，同时保留较慢的稳定计算路径。我们进一步引入因果记忆更新方案和在线回放控制器，根据测量的遗忘情况调整巩固强度。在C4、WikiText-103和GSM8K上的任务序列语言建模流中，TRC²持续提升任务边界建模质量，并显著降低累积遗忘量，相较Transformer、Mamba、MoE、DeepSeek及在相同管道下训练的持续学习基线模型表现更优。消融实验表明，丘脑和海马组件对记忆保持收益至关重要，而完整模型在吞吐量和训练成本上仍保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
To address catastrophic forgetting in large language models during continual learning, TRC² introduces a decoder-only architecture inspired by cortical and thalamic neuroscience, combining stacked cortical columns with thalamic routing for selective communication and a hippocampal pathway for event-based retrieval and replay-driven consolidation. The model employs a causal memory-update scheme and an online replay controller that dynamically adjusts consolidation based on measured forgetting. Experiments across C4, WikiText-103, and GSM8K show TRC² consistently improves task-boundary modeling and reduces cumulative forgetting compared to Transformers, Mamba, MoE, DeepSeek, and other continual learning baselines, while maintaining competitive training efficiency.
为解决大语言模型在持续学习中出现的灾难性遗忘问题，TRC²提出一种受神经生物学启发的解码器架构，结合堆叠皮层列与丘脑和海马通路，实现选择性通信、事件驱动检索与回放驱动巩固。模型采用因果记忆更新机制和在线回放控制器，根据测量的遗忘动态调整巩固强度。在C4、WikiText-103和GSM8K数据集上的实验表明，TRC²在任务边界建模上表现更优，显著降低累积遗忘，相比Transformer、Mamba、MoE、DeepSeek及其他持续学习基线模型，同时保持训练效率竞争力。
Where Do Vision-Language Models Fail? World Scale Analysis for Image Geolocalization
Authors: Siddhant Bharadwaj, Ashish Vashist, Fahimul Aleem, Shruti Vyas
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-04-17T17:09:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:09:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the CVPR EarthVision 2026 Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image geolocalization has traditionally been addressed through retrieval-based place recognition or geometry-based visual localization pipelines. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong zero-shot reasoning capabilities across multimodal tasks, yet their performance in geographic inference remains underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic evaluation of multiple state-of-the-art VLMs for country-level image geolocalization using ground-view imagery only. Instead of relying on image matching, GPS metadata, or task-specific training, we evaluate prompt-based country prediction in a zero-shot setting. The selected models are tested on three geographically diverse datasets to assess their robustness and generalization ability. Our results reveal substantial variation across models, highlighting the potential of semantic reasoning for coarse geolocalization and the limitations of current VLMs in capturing fine-grained geographic cues. This study provides the first focused comparison of modern VLMs for country-level geolocalization and establishes a foundation for future research at the intersection of multimodal reasoning and geographic understanding.
