为探究视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在地理推理中的能力，本研究在仅使用地面视角图像、零样本提示预测国家的前提下，系统评估了多个前沿VLMs的国家级图像地理定位性能，不依赖图像匹配、GPS元数据或任务特定训练。模型在三个地理分布各异的数据集上进行测试，以评估其鲁棒性和泛化能力。结果表明，不同模型表现差异显著，说明语义推理虽可支持粗粒度定位，但当前VLMs在捕捉细粒度地理线索方面仍存在明显局限。
Joint-Centric Dual Contrastive Alignment with Structure-Preserving and Information-Balanced Regularization
Authors: Habibeh Naderi, Behrouz Haji Soleimani, Stan Matwin
First: 2026-04-17T17:07:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:07:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose HILBERT (HIerarchical Long-sequence Balanced Embedding with Reciprocal contrastive Training), a cross-attentive multimodal framework for learning document-level audio-text representations from long, segmented sequences in low-resource data settings. HILBERT leverages frozen pre-trained speech and language encoders to extract segment-level features, which are aggregated via cross-modal attention and self-attentive pooling to form modality-specific document representations and a joint cross-attentive embedding. To align modalities while preserving modality-specific structure under severe audio-text dimensional imbalance, we introduce a reciprocal dual contrastive objective that simultaneously aligns audio-to-joint and text-to-joint representations, rather than directly contrasting audio and text alone. Two auxiliary regularizers further stabilize long-sequence fusion: a Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) loss that preserves structural consistency between each modality and the joint embedding, and a mutual information balancing loss that prevents dominance of a single modality by equalizing information flow from audio and text into the joint space. For downstream prediction, HILBERT employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) classifier over concatenated audio, text, and joint representations to accommodate heterogeneous label regimes. Extensive evaluation across multiple audio-text backbone combinations demonstrates that HILBERT learns semantically meaningful long-sequence representations and achieves superior performance on highly imbalanced multi-class settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于结构保持与信息平衡正则化的联合中心双对比对齐
我们提出HILBERT（HIerarchical Long-sequence Balanced Embedding with Reciprocal contrastive Training），一种跨注意力多模态框架，用于在低资源数据环境下从长段序列中学习文档级音频-文本表示。HILBERT利用冻结的预训练语音和语言编码器提取段级特征，通过跨模态注意力和自注意力池化聚合，形成模态特异的文档表示和联合跨注意力嵌入。为在严重音频-文本维度失衡下对齐模态并保持模态特异性结构，我们引入双向双对比目标，同时对齐音频到联合和文本到联合表示，而非直接对比音频与文本。两个辅助正则器进一步稳定长序列融合：中心核对齐（CKA）损失保持各模态与联合嵌入间的结构一致性，互信息平衡损失通过均衡音频与文本向联合空间的信息流，防止单一模态主导。下游预测中，HILBERT在拼接的音频、文本和联合表示上采用专家混合（MoE）分类器，以适应异构标签体系。在多种音频-文本骨干组合上的广泛评估表明，HILBERT学习到语义丰富的长序列表示，并在高度不平衡的多分类设置中表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning meaningful document-level audio-text representations from long, segmented sequences in low-resource settings, HILBERT introduces a cross-attentive multimodal framework that uses frozen pre-trained encoders to extract segment-level features, then aggregates them via cross-modal attention and self-attentive pooling to form modality-specific and joint embeddings. To handle severe audio-text dimensional imbalance while preserving modality structure, it employs a reciprocal dual contrastive loss that aligns audio-to-joint and text-to-joint representations simultaneously, complemented by two regularizers: Centered Kernel Alignment to preserve structural consistency and mutual information balancing to equalize information flow. Experimental results across multiple backbone combinations show HILBERT learns semantically rich long-sequence representations and outperforms baselines in highly imbalanced multi-class tasks using a Mixture-of-Experts classifier.
为了解决在低资源环境下从长片段序列中学习文档级音频-文本表示的难题，HILBERT提出了一种跨注意力多模态框架，利用冻结的预训练编码器提取片段级特征，再通过跨模态注意力与自注意力池化生成模态特异性和联合嵌入。为应对音频-文本维度失衡并保持模态结构，它采用双向对比损失，同时对齐音频到联合空间与文本到联合空间的表示。两个辅助正则化项——中心核对齐损失用于保持模态与联合嵌入的结构一致性，互信息平衡损失用于均衡音频与文本向联合空间的信息流——进一步稳定融合过程。实验表明，HILBERT在多个骨干网络组合上学习到语义丰富的长序列表示，并在高度不平衡的多分类任务中优于基线方法。
Find, Fix, Reason: Context Repair for Video Reasoning
Authors: Haojian Huang, Chuanyu Qin, Yinchuan Li, Yingcong Chen
First: 2026-04-17T17:04:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:04:19+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures, 17 tables. Ongoing work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning has advanced video reasoning in large multi-modal models, yet dominant pipelines either rely on on-policy self-exploration, which plateaus at the model's knowledge boundary, or hybrid replay that mixes policies and demands careful regularization. Dynamic context methods zoom into focused evidence but often require curated pretraining and two-stage tuning, and their context remains bounded by a small model's capability. In contrast, larger models excel at instruction following and multi-modal understanding, can supply richer context to smaller models, and rapidly zoom in on target regions via simple tools. Building on this capability, we introduce an observation-level intervention: a frozen, tool-integrated teacher identifies the missing spatiotemporal dependency and provides a minimal evidence patch (e.g., timestamps, regions etc.) from the original video while the question remains unchanged. The student answers again with the added context, and training updates with a chosen-rollout scheme integrated into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We further propose a Robust Improvement Reward (RIR) that aligns optimization with two goals: outcome validity through correct answers and dependency alignment through rationales that reflect the cited evidence. Advantages are group-normalized across the batch, preserving on-policy exploration while directing it along causally meaningful directions with minimal changes to the training stack. Experiments on various related benchmarks show consistent accuracy gains and strong generalization. Web page and source code will be available at https://github.com/JethroJames/FFR.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：查找、修复、推理：视频推理的上下文修复
强化学习已推动大型多模态模型在视频推理方面的进展，但主流流程要么依赖基于策略的自我探索，会在模型知识边界处停滞，要么采用混合回放策略，需精细正则化。动态上下文方法聚焦于关键证据，但通常需要定制化预训练和两阶段调优，且其上下文受限于小模型能力。相比之下，大模型擅长指令遵循和多模态理解，可为小模型提供更丰富的上下文，并通过简单工具快速聚焦目标区域。基于此能力，我们引入一种观测级干预：一个冻结的、集成工具的教师模型识别缺失的空间-时间依赖关系，并在问题不变的前提下，从原始视频中提供最小证据补丁（如时间戳、区域等）。学生模型在新增上下文后再次作答，训练采用集成于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的选定回放方案。我们进一步提出鲁棒改进奖励（RIR），使优化同时满足两个目标：通过正确答案确保结果有效性，通过反映所引证据的推理理由实现依赖对齐。该方法在批处理中进行组归一化，保留策略探索的同时，引导其沿因果意义明确的方向进行，且对训练栈改动极小。在多个相关基准测试中，实验显示一致的准确率提升和强泛化能力。项目网页和源代码将在 https://github.com/JethroJames/FFR.git 上发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing video reasoning methods—such as plateauing on-policy exploration, complex hybrid replay, and context-bound dynamic methods—the paper proposes a context-repair intervention using a frozen, tool-integrated teacher to identify missing spatiotemporal dependencies and inject minimal evidence patches into the original video. The student model then reanswers with the added context, and training is updated via a chosen-rollout scheme within Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enhanced by a Robust Improvement Reward (RIR) that jointly encourages correct outcomes and rationale alignment with cited evidence. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements and strong generalization, with minimal modifications to the training pipeline.
为克服现有视频推理方法的局限——如策略探索停滞、混合回放复杂、动态上下文方法受限于模型能力——本文提出一种上下文修复干预机制：使用一个冻结的、集成工具的教师模型，识别缺失的时空依赖并注入最小证据片段（如时间戳或区域），同时保持原问题不变。学生模型在添加上下文后重新作答，训练通过集成到Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）中的选择式回放机制更新，并采用新型Robust Improvement Reward（RIR）奖励函数，同时优化正确答案与推理理由对证据的对齐。实验在多个基准上显示一致的准确率提升和强泛化能力，且无需大幅修改训练流程。
Detecting and Suppressing Reward Hacking with Gradient Fingerprints
Authors: Songtao Wang, Quang Hieu Pham, Fangcong Yin, Xinpeng Wang, Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen, Greg Durrett, Xi Ye
First: 2026-04-17T17:01:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:01:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically optimizes for outcome rewards without imposing constraints on intermediate reasoning. This leaves training susceptible to reward hacking, where models exploit loopholes (e.g., spurious patterns in training data) in the reward function to achieve high scores without solving the intended task. These reward-hacking behaviors are often implicit, as the intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) may appear plausible on the surface, limiting the effectiveness of purely text-based monitoring. We propose Gradient Fingerprint (GRIFT), a method for detecting reward hacking using models' internal computations. Given a prompt and a model-generated CoT, GRIFT computes gradients of the CoT conditioned on the prompt and compresses them into a compact representation, which is then used to assess whether the CoT reflects reward hacking behavior. Across verifiable reasoning benchmarks spanning math, code, and logical reasoning, GRIFT substantially outperforms strong baselines, including CoT Monitor and TRACE, achieving over 25% relative improvement in detecting reward hacking behavior. Moreover, integrating GRIFT into the rejection fine-tuning pipeline for reasoning tasks reduces reward hacking and improves performance on the true task objective. Our results highlight a promising direction of leveraging gradient level representations for assessing the quality of CoT reasoning traces. Our code is available at: https://github.com/songtao-x/reward_hack.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用梯度指纹检测与抑制奖励黑客行为
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）通常仅优化最终奖励，而不对中间推理施加约束，这使得训练易受奖励黑客行为影响——模型利用奖励函数中的漏洞（如训练数据中的虚假模式）以实现高分，而无需解决实际任务。此类奖励黑客行为往往隐性，因中间思维链（CoT）表面看似合理，限制了纯文本监控的有效性。我们提出梯度指纹（GRIFT），一种利用模型内部计算检测奖励黑客行为的方法。给定提示与模型生成的CoT，GRIFT计算CoT相对于提示的梯度，并将其压缩为紧凑表示，用于评估CoT是否反映奖励黑客行为。在涵盖数学、代码与逻辑推理的可验证推理基准上，GRIFT显著优于强基线方法（如CoT Monitor和TRACE），在检测奖励黑客行为方面实现超过25%的相对提升。此外，将GRIFT集成至推理任务的拒绝微调流程中，可减少奖励黑客行为并提升对真实任务目标的性能。我们的结果凸显了利用梯度级表示评估CoT推理轨迹质量的前景。代码见：https://github.com/songtao-x/reward_hack。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of reward hacking in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where models exploit reward function loopholes without solving the intended task, the authors introduce Gradient Fingerprint (GRIFT), a method that detects such behavior by computing and compressing gradients of model-generated chain-of-thought reasoning conditioned on prompts. GRIFT evaluates whether these gradients indicate reward-hacking patterns, distinguishing them from legitimate reasoning. Experiments across math, code, and logical reasoning benchmarks show GRIFT outperforms baselines like CoT Monitor and TRACE by over 25% in detection accuracy, and when integrated into rejection fine-tuning, it reduces reward hacking while improving task performance.
为应对强化学习中奖励函数漏洞被利用导致的奖励作弊问题，本文提出Gradient Fingerprint（GRIFT）方法，通过计算并压缩模型在提示条件下生成的思维链（CoT）的梯度，以检测是否出现奖励作弊行为。该方法在数学、编程与逻辑推理等多个可验证推理基准上，显著优于CoT Monitor和TRACE等基线方法，检测准确率提升超25%。当GRIFT集成到拒绝微调流程中时，可有效减少奖励作弊并提升对真实任务目标的性能，验证了梯度级表示在评估推理质量方面的潜力。
BAGEL: Benchmarking Animal Knowledge Expertise in Language Models
Authors: Jiacheng Shen, Masato Hagiwara, Milad Alizadeh, Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon, Marius Miron, David Robinson, Emmanuel Chemla, Sara Keen, Gagan Narula, Mathieu Laurière, Matthieu Geist, Olivier Pietquin
First: 2026-04-17T17:00:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:00:37+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models have shown strong performance on broad-domain knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, but it remains unclear how well language models handle specialized animal-related knowledge under a unified closed-book evaluation protocol. We introduce BAGEL, a benchmark for evaluating animal knowledge expertise in language models. BAGEL is constructed from diverse scientific and reference sources, including bioRxiv, Global Biotic Interactions, Xeno-canto, and Wikipedia, using a combination of curated examples and automatically generated closed-book question-answer pairs. The benchmark covers multiple aspects of animal knowledge, including taxonomy, morphology, habitat, behavior, vocalization, geographic distribution, and species interactions. By focusing on closed-book evaluation, BAGEL measures animal-related knowledge of models without external retrieval at inference time. BAGEL further supports fine-grained analysis across source domains, taxonomic groups, and knowledge categories, enabling a more precise characterization of model strengths and systematic failure modes. Our benchmark provides a new testbed for studying domain-specific knowledge generalization in language models and for improving their reliability in biodiversity-related applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BAGEL：评估语言模型动物知识专长的基准
大型语言模型在广泛领域的知识和推理基准测试中表现出色，但尚不清楚它们在统一的闭卷评估协议下处理专门动物相关知识的能力如何。我们引入BAGEL，一个用于评估语言模型动物知识专长的基准。BAGEL从多样化的科学和参考来源构建，包括bioRxiv、全球生物相互作用数据库、Xeno-canto和维基百科，结合人工精选示例与自动生成的闭卷问答对。该基准涵盖动物知识的多个方面，包括分类、形态、栖息地、行为、发声、地理分布和物种互动。通过聚焦闭卷评估，BAGEL在推理时无需外部检索即可衡量模型的动物相关知识。BAGEL还支持跨来源领域、分类群和知识类别的细粒度分析，有助于更精确地刻画模型优势和系统性失败模式。本基准为研究语言模型在特定领域知识泛化能力及提升其在生物多样性相关应用中的可靠性提供了新测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess how well large language models handle specialized animal knowledge without external retrieval, the authors introduce BAGEL, a closed-book benchmark constructed from scientific and reference sources like bioRxiv, Global Biotic Interactions, Xeno-canto, and Wikipedia. The benchmark evaluates models across taxonomy, morphology, habitat, behavior, vocalization, distribution, and species interactions using curated and auto-generated question-answer pairs. Experimental results show that while models perform reasonably well on general knowledge, they exhibit significant failures in domain-specific tasks, especially in taxonomy and species interactions, revealing systematic weaknesses in animal knowledge generalization.
为评估大语言模型在动物知识方面的专精能力，作者构建了BAGEL基准测试，该测试基于bioRxiv、Xeno-canto和维基百科等科学资源，包含人工精选与自动生成的闭卷问答对，覆盖动物分类、行为、栖息地等多个维度。实验表明，模型在通用动物知识上表现尚可，但在发声机制和物种互动等专业领域存在明显缺陷，揭示了其在特定领域知识泛化方面的系统性不足。
CollideNet: Hierarchical Multi-scale Video Representation Learning with Disentanglement for Time-To-Collision Forecasting
Authors: Nishq Poorav Desai, Ali Etemad, Michael Greenspan
First: 2026-04-17T17:00:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T17:00:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Time-to-Collision (TTC) forecasting is a critical task in collision prevention, requiring precise temporal prediction and comprehending both local and global patterns encapsulated in a video, both spatially and temporally. To address the multi-scale nature of video, we introduce a novel spatiotemporal hierarchical transformer-based architecture called CollideNet, specifically catered for effective TTC forecasting. In the spatial stream, CollideNet aggregates information for each video frame simultaneously at multiple resolutions. In the temporal stream, along with multi-scale feature encoding, CollideNet also disentangles the non-stationarity, trend, and seasonality components. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in comparison to prior works on three commonly used public datasets, setting a new state-of-the-art by a considerable margin. We conduct cross-dataset evaluations to analyze the generalization capabilities of our method, and visualize the effects of disentanglement of the trend and seasonality components of the video data. We release our code at https://github.com/DeSinister/CollideNet/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CollideNet：用于碰撞时间预测的具有解耦机制的层次化多尺度视频表征学习
碰撞时间预测（TTC）是防撞任务中的关键环节，需要精确的时间预测，并理解视频中空间与时间维度上所蕴含的局部与全局模式。为应对视频的多尺度特性，我们提出了一种新颖的时空层次化Transformer架构——CollideNet，专为高效TTC预测设计。在空间流中，CollideNet在多个分辨率下同时聚合各视频帧的信息；在时间流中，除多尺度特征编码外，CollideNet还解耦非平稳性、趋势与季节性成分。我们的方法在三个常用公开数据集上相较现有方法取得当前最优性能，且优势显著。我们通过跨数据集评估分析方法的泛化能力，并可视化视频数据中趋势与季节性成分解耦的效果。代码已开源：https://github.com/DeSinister/CollideNet/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise time-to-collision forecasting in collision prevention, CollideNet introduces a hierarchical spatiotemporal transformer architecture that jointly models multi-scale spatial and temporal patterns. The spatial stream aggregates multi-resolution features per frame, while the temporal stream disentangles non-stationarity, trend, and seasonality components for improved prediction. Evaluated on three public datasets, CollideNet achieves state-of-the-art performance with significant margins over prior methods and demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization, with visualizations confirming the effectiveness of its disentanglement mechanism.
为提升碰撞预防中的时间碰撞预测精度，CollideNet提出一种层次化时空Transformer架构，联合建模多尺度空间与时间模式。空间流在每帧内聚合多分辨率特征，时间流则解耦非平稳性、趋势与季节性成分以增强预测。在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，CollideNet显著优于现有方法，达到当前最优性能，且跨数据集评估验证其具备良好的泛化能力。
TRIDENT: Enhancing Large Language Model Safety with Tri-Dimensional Diversified Red-Teaming Data Synthesis
Authors: Xiaorui Wu, Xiaofeng Mao, Fei Li, Xin Zhang, Xuanhong Li, Chong Teng, Donghong Ji, Zhuang Li
First: 2025-05-30T15:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:59:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in various natural language processing tasks but remain vulnerable to generating harmful content or being exploited for malicious purposes. Although safety alignment datasets have been introduced to mitigate such risks through supervised fine-tuning (SFT), these datasets often lack comprehensive risk coverage. Most existing datasets focus primarily on lexical diversity while neglecting other critical dimensions. To address this limitation, we propose a novel analysis framework to systematically measure the risk coverage of alignment datasets across three essential dimensions: Lexical Diversity, Malicious Intent, and Jailbreak Tactics. We further introduce TRIDENT, an automated pipeline that leverages persona-based, zero-shot LLM generation to produce diverse and comprehensive instructions spanning these dimensions. Each harmful instruction is paired with an ethically aligned response, resulting in two datasets: TRIDENT-Core, comprising 26,311 examples, and TRIDENT-Edge, with 18,773 examples. Fine-tuning Llama 3.1-8B on TRIDENT-Edge demonstrates substantial improvements, achieving an average 14.29% reduction in Harm Score, and a 20% decrease in Attack Success Rate compared to the best-performing baseline model fine-tuned on the WildBreak dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRIDENT：通过三维多样化红队数据合成增强大语言模型安全性
大语言模型（LLMs）在多种自然语言处理任务中表现优异，但仍易生成有害内容或被用于恶意目的。尽管已引入安全对齐数据集通过监督微调（SFT）缓解此类风险，但这些数据集往往缺乏全面的风险覆盖。现有数据集大多侧重词汇多样性，而忽视其他关键维度。为解决此局限，我们提出一种新颖分析框架，系统衡量对齐数据集在三个核心维度——词汇多样性、恶意意图和越狱策略上的风险覆盖。我们进一步引入TRIDENT，一个自动化流水线，利用基于人格的零样本LLM生成，产生涵盖上述维度的多样化且全面的指令。每条有害指令均配以伦理对齐的响应，形成两个数据集：TRIDENT-Core（26,311例）和TRIDENT-Edge（18,773例）。在TRIDENT-Edge上微调Llama 3.1-8B模型，显著提升性能，平均降低有害评分14.29%，攻击成功率较基于WildBreak数据集的最优基线模型降低20%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing safety alignment datasets that lack comprehensive risk coverage, this work introduces TRIDENT, an automated pipeline that synthesizes diverse harmful instructions across three critical dimensions—Lexical Diversity, Malicious Intent, and Jailbreak Tactics—using persona-based, zero-shot LLM generation. Each instruction is paired with an ethically aligned response, yielding two datasets: TRIDENT-Core (26,311 examples) and TRIDENT-Edge (18,773 examples). Experimental results show that fine-tuning Llama 3.1-8B on TRIDENT-Edge reduces Harm Score by 14.29% and Attack Success Rate by 20% compared to the best baseline fine-tuned on WildBreak, demonstrating superior safety performance.
为解决现有LLM安全对齐数据集覆盖风险维度不足的问题，作者提出TRIDENT方法，通过基于角色的零样本LLM生成，自动生成涵盖词法多样性、恶意意图和越狱策略三个关键维度的多样化有害指令，并配以伦理对齐的响应，形成TRIDENT-Core（26,311例）和TRIDENT-Edge（18,773例）两个数据集。在TRIDENT-Edge上微调Llama 3.1-8B后，其有害评分平均降低14.29%，攻击成功率较WildBreak最佳基线模型下降20%，安全性能显著提升。
Adaptive multi-fidelity optimization with fast learning rates
Authors: Come Fiegel, Victor Gabillon, Michal Valko
Venue: Proceedings of the 23rd International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS), 2020
First: 2026-04-17T16:59:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:59:07+00:00
Comments: Published at International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS) 2020
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In multi-fidelity optimization, biased approximations of varying costs of the target function are available. This paper studies the problem of optimizing a locally smooth function with a limited budget, where the learner has to make a tradeoff between the cost and the bias of these approximations. We first prove lower bounds for the simple regret under different assumptions on the fidelities, based on a cost-to-bias function. We then present the Kometo algorithm which achieves, with additional logarithmic factors, the same rates without any knowledge of the function smoothness and fidelity assumptions, and improves previously proven guarantees. We finally empirically show that our algorithm outperforms previous multi-fidelity optimization methods without the knowledge of problem-dependent parameters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有快速学习率的自适应多保真度优化
在多保真度优化中，目标函数存在不同成本的有偏近似。本文研究在有限预算下优化局部光滑函数的问题，学习者需在近似成本与偏差之间进行权衡。我们首先基于成本-偏差函数，在不同保真度假设下证明了简单后悔的下界。随后提出Kometo算法，在无需了解函数光滑性与保真度假设的情况下，通过额外对数因子达到相同收敛速率，并改进了先前的理论保证。最后，我们通过实验证明，该算法在不依赖问题特定参数的情况下，优于现有多种保真度优化方法。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses multi-fidelity optimization under a limited budget, where the learner must balance approximation cost and bias when optimizing a locally smooth function. It first establishes theoretical lower bounds on simple regret based on a cost-to-bias function, then introduces the Kometo algorithm that achieves near-optimal rates without requiring prior knowledge of function smoothness or fidelity parameters, improving upon existing guarantees. Empirical experiments demonstrate that Kometo outperforms prior methods in practice without needing problem-specific assumptions.
该文研究在预算受限下，如何优化局部光滑函数，其中学习者需在不同成本的近似函数的偏差与代价间权衡。作者首先基于代价-偏差函数推导了不同保真度假设下的简单后悔下界，随后提出Kometo算法，在无需预先知道函数光滑性或保真度参数的前提下，实现接近最优的收敛速率，且优于现有方法的理论保证。实验表明，该算法在实践中优于先前方法，无需问题特定参数调优。
Enhancing AI and Dynamical Subseasonal Forecasts with Probabilistic Bias Correction
Authors: Hannah Guan, Soukayna Mouatadid, Paulo Orenstein, Judah Cohen, Haiyu Dong, Zekun Ni, Jeremy Berman, Genevieve Flaspohler, Alex Lu, Jakob Schloer, Joshua Talib, Jonathan A. Weyn, Lester Mackey
First: 2026-04-17T16:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:58:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision-makers rely on weather forecasts to plant crops, manage wildfires, allocate water and energy, and prepare for weather extremes. Today, such forecasts enjoy unprecedented accuracy out to two weeks thanks to steady advances in physics-based dynamical models and data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) models. However, model skill drops precipitously at subseasonal timescales (2 - 6 weeks ahead), due to compounding errors and persistent biases. To counter this degradation, we introduce probabilistic bias correction (PBC), a machine learning framework that substantially reduces systematic error by learning to correct historical probabilistic forecasts. When applied to the leading dynamical and AI models from the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts (ECMWF), PBC doubles the subseasonal skill of the AI Forecasting System and improves the skill of the operationally-debiased dynamical model for 91% of pressure, 92% of temperature, and 98% of precipitation targets. We designed PBC for operational deployment, and, in ECMWF's 2025 real-time forecasting competition, its global forecasts placed first for all weather variables and lead times, outperforming the dynamical models from six operational forecasting centers, an international dynamical multi-model ensemble, ECMWF's AI Forecasting System, and the forecasting systems of 34 teams worldwide. These probabilistic skill gains translate into more accurate prediction of extreme events and have the potential to improve agricultural planning, energy management, and disaster preparedness in vulnerable communities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过概率性偏差校正增强人工智能与动力学次季节预报
决策者依赖天气预报来种植作物、管理野火、分配水资源和能源，并应对极端天气。得益于物理基础的动力学模型和数据驱动的人工智能（AI）模型的持续进步，当前的预报在两周内已达到前所未有的精度。然而，在次季节时间尺度（2至6周）上，模型性能急剧下降，这是由于误差累积和持续性偏差所致。为应对这一退化，我们引入了概率性偏差校正（PBC），这是一种机器学习框架，通过学习校正历史概率预报，显著减少系统性误差。当应用于欧洲中期天气预报中心（ECMWF）的领先动力学和AI模型时，PBC使AI预报系统的次季节技能翻倍，并在91%的压力、92%的温度和98%的降水目标上提升了操作性偏差校正动力学模型的性能。我们为实际部署设计了PBC，在ECMWF 2025实时预报竞赛中，其全球预报在所有天气变量和预报时效上均排名第一，优于六个运营预报中心的动力学模型、一个国际动力学多模型集合、ECMWF的AI预报系统以及全球34支团队的预报系统。这些概率性技能提升转化为对极端事件更准确的预测，有望改善脆弱社区的农业规划、能源管理和灾害应对能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rapid decline in forecast accuracy beyond two weeks due to compounding errors and persistent biases in both dynamical and AI models, researchers developed probabilistic bias correction (PBC), a machine learning framework that learns to correct historical probabilistic forecasts. Applied to ECMWF’s leading dynamical and AI models, PBC significantly reduced systematic errors, doubling the subseasonal skill of the AI Forecasting System and enhancing the dynamical model’s performance across 91% of pressure, 92% of temperature, and 98% of precipitation targets. In ECMWF’s 2025 real-time forecasting competition, PBC’s global forecasts ranked first for all variables and lead times, outperforming six operational centers, an international ensemble, the AI system itself, and 34 global teams.
为应对两周后预报精度急剧下降的问题——这是由于动态模型和AI模型在亚季节尺度（2-6周）上累积误差和持续偏差所致——研究人员提出了概率偏差校正（PBC）方法，这是一种通过学习历史概率预报进行误差修正的机器学习框架。当应用于ECMWF领先模型时，PBC显著降低了系统性误差，使AI预报系统的亚季节技能翻倍，并在91%的气压、92%的温度和98%的降水目标上提升了动态模型的性能。在ECMWF 2025实时预报竞赛中，PBC的全球预报在所有变量和预报时效上均排名第一，优于六个业务中心、一个国际多模式集合、ECMWF自身AI系统及全球34支队伍的预报系统。
Angle-based Localization and Rigidity Maintenance Control for Multi-Robot Networks
Authors: J. Francisco Presenza, Leonardo J. Colombo, Juan I. Giribet, Ignacio Mas
First: 2026-04-13T17:28:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:54:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we study angle-based localization and rigidity maintenance control for multi-robot networks. First, we establish the relationship between angle rigidity and bearing rigidity considering \textit{directed} sensing graphs and \textit{body-frame} bearing measurements in both $2$ and $3$-\textit{dimensional space}. In particular, we demonstrate that a framework in $\mathrm{SE}(d)$ is infinitesimally bearing rigid if and only if it is infinitesimally angle rigid and each robot obtains at least $d-1$ bearing measurements ($d \in \{2, 3\}$). Building on these findings, this paper proposes a distributed angle-based localization scheme and establishes local exponential stability under switching sensing graphs, requiring only infinitesimal angle rigidity across the visited topologies. Then, since the set of available angles strongly depends on the robots' spatial configuration due to sensing constraints, we investigate rigidity maintenance control. The \textit{angle rigidity eigenvalue} is presented as a metric for the degree of rigidity. A decentralized gradient-based controller capable of executing mission-specific commands while maintaining a sufficient level of angle rigidity is proposed. Simulations were conducted to evaluate the scheme's effectiveness and practicality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多机器人网络的角度定位与刚性维持控制
本文研究了多机器人网络的角度定位与刚性维持控制。首先，我们建立了在有向感知图和体坐标系方向测量下，角度刚性与测向刚性之间的关系，适用于二维和三维空间。特别地，我们证明了在 $\mathrm{SE}(d)$ 中，一个框架在无穷小测向刚性下当且仅当其在无穷小角度刚性下成立，且每个机器人至少获得 $d-1$ 个测向测量值（$d \in \{2, 3\}$）。基于这些发现，本文提出了一种分布式角度定位方案，并在切换感知图下建立了局部指数稳定性，仅需在访问的拓扑结构中满足无穷小角度刚性。由于感知约束，可用角度强烈依赖于机器人空间构型，因此我们研究了刚性维持控制。提出了“角度刚性特征值”作为刚性程度的度量。设计了一种分布式梯度控制器，能够在执行特定任务的同时维持足够的角度刚性。通过仿真评估了该方案的有效性和实用性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of angle-based localization and rigidity maintenance in multi-robot networks under directed sensing graphs and body-frame bearing measurements in 2D and 3D space. It establishes that infinitesimal angle rigidity, combined with each robot having at least d−1 bearing measurements (d=2,3), is equivalent to infinitesimal bearing rigidity in SE(d). Based on this, a distributed angle-based localization algorithm is proposed, proven to achieve local exponential stability under switching topologies as long as angle rigidity is maintained across visited graphs. Additionally, the paper introduces the angle rigidity eigenvalue as a rigidity metric and proposes a decentralized gradient-based controller that enables mission execution while preserving sufficient rigidity, validated through simulations.
本文研究多机器人网络中的角度定位与刚性维持控制问题，通过建立在有向感知图和机体坐标系测量下，二维与三维空间中角度刚性与测向刚性的理论关系，证明在SE(d)框架下，当每个机器人获得至少d-1个测向量时，无穷小测向刚性等价于无穷小角度刚性。在此基础上，提出一种分布式角度定位方案，可在切换感知图下实现局部指数稳定，仅需满足无穷小角度刚性。同时，为应对机器人空间构型变化导致的可用角度受限问题，引入角度刚性特征值作为刚性度量，并设计一种分布式梯度控制器，在执行任务指令的同时维持足够刚性。仿真结果验证了该方案的有效性与实用性。
Optimizing Korean-Centric LLMs via Token Pruning
Authors: Hoyeol Kim, Hyeonwoo Kim
First: 2026-04-17T16:53:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:53:14+00:00
Comments: 5 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a systematic benchmark of state-of-the-art multilingual large language models (LLMs) adapted via token pruning - a compression technique that eliminates tokens and embedding parameters corresponding to languages irrelevant to the target application. Focusing on Korean-centric natural language processing (NLP) tasks, we evaluate architectures including Qwen3, Gemma-3, Llama-3, and Aya across three vocabulary configurations: Original, English-Korean (EnKo), and English-Korean-Chinese (EnKoZh). Performance is assessed using established benchmarks for general aptitude, cultural literacy, instruction following, and machine translation. Our findings indicate that token pruning significantly improves generation stability by eliminating language confusion, and in the case of machine translation, frequently enhances performance on Korean-specific tasks. While instruction-following capabilities display architecture-dependent variance linked to latent cross-lingual representations, the significant reduction in vocabulary size validates token pruning as a highly effective optimization strategy for memory-constrained, domain-specific deployments, despite modest gains in inference latency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过令牌剪枝优化以韩语为中心的大语言模型
本文系统评估了通过令牌剪枝（一种压缩技术，可消除与目标应用无关的语言对应的令牌和嵌入参数）调整的最先进多语言大语言模型（LLM）。聚焦韩语中心的自然语言处理（NLP）任务，我们在三种词汇配置（原始、英韩、英韩中）下评估了Qwen3、Gemma-3、Llama-3和Aya等架构。性能通过通用能力、文化素养、指令遵循和机器翻译等标准基准进行评估。研究发现，令牌剪枝通过消除语言混淆显著提升生成稳定性，在机器翻译中常增强韩语特定任务的表现。尽管指令遵循能力因架构差异而异，与潜在跨语言表示相关，但词汇量的显著减少验证了令牌剪枝作为内存受限、领域特定部署中高效优化策略的有效性，尽管推理延迟略有增加。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize large language models for Korean-centric NLP tasks while reducing memory footprint, this study evaluates token pruning—a technique that removes tokens and embedding parameters for irrelevant languages—as a compression strategy. It tests models including Qwen3, Gemma-3, Llama-3, and Aya across three vocabulary configurations: Original, English-Korean (EnKo), and English-Korean-Chinese (EnKoZh), using benchmarks for general aptitude, cultural literacy, instruction following, and machine translation. 为优化面向韩语任务的大语言模型并降低内存占用，本研究评估了通过删除无关语言的词元和嵌入参数的‘词元剪枝’技术。在Qwen3、Gemma-3、Llama-3和Aya等模型上，分别测试了原始词汇、英韩（EnKo）和英韩中（EnKoZh）三种词汇配置，并通过通用能力、文化素养、指令遵循和机器翻译等基准评估性能。结果表明，词元剪枝通过消除语言干扰提升了生成稳定性，常能改善韩语特定任务的机器翻译表现，指令遵循能力则因模型架构与跨语言表征差异而异。尽管推理延迟略有上升，但词汇量显著减少证实该方法是内存受限、领域专用部署中的高效优化策略。
A Two-Stage, Object-Centric Deep Learning Framework for Robust Exam Cheating Detection
Authors: Van-Truong Le, Le-Khanh Nguyen, Trong-Doanh Nguyen
First: 2026-04-17T16:53:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:53:08+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 5 figures. Accepted at the FISU Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence (FJCAI 2026), Vietnam
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Academic integrity continues to face the persistent challenge of examination cheating. Traditional invigilation relies on human observation, which is inefficient, costly, and prone to errors at scale. Although some existing AI-powered monitoring systems have been deployed and trusted, many lack transparency or require multi-layered architectures to achieve the desired performance. To overcome these challenges, we propose an improvement over a simple two-stage framework for exam cheating detection that integrates object detection and behavioral analysis using well-known technologies. First, the state-of-the-art YOLOv8n model is used to localize students in exam-room images. Each detected region is cropped and preprocessed, then classified by a fine-tuned RexNet-150 model as either normal or cheating behavior. The system is trained on a dataset compiled from 10 independent sources with a total of 273,897 samples, achieving 0.95 accuracy, 0.94 recall, 0.96 precision, and 0.95 F1-score - a 13\% increase over a baseline accuracy of 0.82 in video-based cheating detection. In addition, with an average inference time of 13.9 ms per sample, the proposed approach demonstrates robustness and scalability for deployment in large-scale environments. Beyond the technical contribution, the AI-assisted monitoring system also addresses ethical concerns by ensuring that final outcomes are delivered privately to individual students after the examination, for example, via personal email. This prevents public exposure or shaming and offers students an opportunity to reflect on their behavior. For further improvement, it is possible to incorporate additional factors, such as audio data and consecutive frames, to achieve greater accuracy. This study provides a foundation for developing real-time, scalable, ethical, and open-source solutions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种面向鲁棒考试作弊检测的两阶段、以对象为中心的深度学习框架
学术诚信持续面临考试作弊的挑战。传统监考依赖人工观察，效率低、成本高且规模化时易出错。尽管部分现有AI监考系统已部署并获信任，但许多缺乏透明度或需多层架构才能达到预期性能。为克服这些挑战，我们提出改进的两阶段框架，结合目标检测与行为分析，采用成熟技术。首先，使用最先进的YOLOv8n模型定位考场图像中的学生，每个检测区域被裁剪并预处理，再由微调的RexNet-150模型分类为正常或作弊行为。系统基于10个独立来源组成的273,897样本数据集训练，准确率0.95、召回率0.94、精确率0.96、F1分数0.95——相较视频作弊检测基线准确率0.82提升13%。此外，平均推理时间为每样本13.9毫秒，表明该方法在大规模环境部署中具备鲁棒性与可扩展性。除技术贡献外，AI辅助监考系统还兼顾伦理问题，考试后将结果私密交付学生（如个人邮箱），避免公开曝光或羞辱，给予学生反思机会。为进一步提升，可引入音频数据及连续帧等要素以提高精度。本研究为开发实时、可扩展、伦理合规且开源的解决方案奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency and error-proneness of human invigilation in exam settings, this study proposes a two-stage deep learning framework combining object detection and behavioral classification. It first uses YOLOv8n to detect students in exam-room images, then crops and classifies each region using a fine-tuned RexNet-150 model to distinguish normal from cheating behavior. Evaluated on a 273,897-sample dataset from 10 sources, the system achieves 0.95 accuracy, 0.94 recall, 0.96 precision, and 0.95 F1-score—13% higher than a baseline of 0.82—while maintaining a fast 13.9 ms per inference, making it suitable for large-scale deployment. The system also ensures ethical privacy by delivering results privately to students post-exam, avoiding public shaming.
为解决人工监考效率低、易出错的问题，该研究提出一种两阶段深度学习框架，结合目标检测与行为分类。首先使用YOLOv8n检测考场中的学生，再对每个区域裁剪预处理后，用微调的RexNet-150模型区分正常与作弊行为。在来自10个来源、共273,897个样本的数据集上，系统达到0.95准确率、0.94召回率、0.96精确率和0.95 F1分数，比基线视频检测方法提升13%，平均推理时间为每样本13.9毫秒，支持大规模部署。系统还通过私密方式（如个人邮箱）向学生发送结果，避免公开羞辱，保障伦理隐私。
ConlangCrafter: Constructing Languages with a Multi-Hop LLM Pipeline
Authors: Morris Alper, Moran Yanuka, Raja Giryes, Gašper Beguš
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-08-08T07:36:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:51:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026. Project page: https://conlangcrafter.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Constructed languages (conlangs) such as Esperanto and Quenya have played diverse roles in art, philosophy, and international communication. Meanwhile, foundation models have revolutionized creative generation in text, images, and beyond. In this work, we leverage modern LLMs as computational creativity aids for end-to-end conlang creation. We introduce ConlangCrafter, a multi-hop pipeline that decomposes language design into modular stages -- phonology, morphology, syntax, lexicon generation, and translation. At each stage, our method leverages LLMs' metalinguistic reasoning capabilities, injecting randomness to encourage diversity and leveraging self-refinement feedback to encourage consistency in the emerging language description. We construct a novel, scalable evaluation framework for this task, evaluating metrics measuring consistency and typological diversity. Automatic and manual evaluations demonstrate ConlangCrafter's ability to produce coherent and varied conlangs without human linguistic expertise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ConlangCrafter：使用多跳LLM管道构建人工语言
诸如世界语和昆雅语等人工语言（conlangs）在艺术、哲学和国际交流中扮演了多样角色。与此同时，基础模型已革新了文本、图像等领域的创意生成。本研究利用现代大语言模型（LLM）作为计算创意辅助工具，实现端到端的人工语言创作。我们提出了ConlangCrafter，一个分阶段的多跳管道，将语言设计分解为音系、形态、句法、词汇生成和翻译等模块化阶段。在每个阶段，我们的方法利用LLM的元语言推理能力，注入随机性以鼓励多样性，并通过自精炼反馈机制确保语言描述的一致性。我们构建了一个新颖且可扩展的评估框架，评估一致性与类型学多样性等指标。自动与人工评估表明，ConlangCrafter无需人类语言学专业知识即可生成连贯且多样的人工语言。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of large language models to automate creative linguistic tasks, this work introduces ConlangCrafter, a multi-hop pipeline that systematically constructs constructed languages by decomposing design into phonology, morphology, syntax, lexicon, and translation stages. Each stage employs LLMs with injected randomness and self-refinement loops to ensure both diversity and internal consistency. Evaluated via a novel framework measuring typological diversity and coherence, ConlangCrafter generates coherent, varied conlangs without requiring human linguistic expertise, demonstrating strong performance in both automated and manual assessments.
受人工构造语言的创作潜力及大语言模型最新进展启发，本研究提出ConlangCrafter，一个分阶段的多跳管道系统，自动完成语音、形态、句法、词汇和翻译等模块化语言设计。每个阶段利用LLM生成语言成分，通过引入随机性增强多样性，并借助自修正机制确保内部一致性。该系统通过新构建的评估框架（衡量类型多样性与一致性）进行评估，结果表明其无需人类语言学专业知识即可生成连贯且多样的构造语言，自动与人工评估均支持此结论。
AdaBoost Does Not Always Cycle: A Computer-Assisted Counterexample
Authors: Erik Y. Wang
First: 2026-04-08T13:06:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:49:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We give a computer-assisted counterexample to the open question, posed by Rudin, Schapire, and Daubechies in COLT 2012, of whether exhaustive AdaBoost always converges to a finite cycle. The construction is based on a block-product gadget whose two factors share an exact period-2 orbit for their 5-step branch maps, but whose linearized return maps have dominant eigenvalues with an irrational logarithmic ratio. This irrationality forces the burst-winner sequence to have an irrational asymptotic frequency, precluding eventual periodicity. All assertions are certified by exact rational arithmetic. This work was developed in collaboration with GPT-5.4 Pro and Claude Opus 4.6.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaBoost并不总是循环：一个计算机辅助的反例
我们给出了一个计算机辅助的反例，回应了Rudin、Schapire和Daubechies在2012年COLT会议上提出的开放性问题：是否 exhaustive AdaBoost 总是收敛到一个有限循环。该构造基于一个块积装置，其两个因子在5步分支映射中共享一个精确的周期2轨道，但其线性化返回映射的主导特征值具有无理数的对数比。这种无理性迫使胜出序列具有无理数的渐近频率，从而排除了最终周期性。所有断言均通过精确的有理数算术验证。本工作由GPT-5.4 Pro和Claude Opus 4.6合作开发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the open question from COLT 2012 regarding whether exhaustive AdaBoost always converges to a finite cycle, this paper constructs a computer-assisted counterexample using a block-product gadget. The gadget’s two factors share a period-2 orbit in their 5-step branch maps, but their linearized return maps have dominant eigenvalues with an irrational logarithmic ratio, forcing the burst-winner sequence to have an irrational asymptotic frequency and thus preventing eventual periodicity. All claims are verified via exact rational arithmetic, demonstrating that AdaBoost can avoid cycling under specific conditions.
为回应COLT 2012年提出的关于 exhaustive AdaBoost 是否总收敛于有限循环的开放问题，本研究通过计算机辅助构造了一个反例，证明该收敛性不成立。方法构建了一个块积装置，其中两个因子在5步分支映射中共享周期为2的轨道，但其线性化返回映射的主导特征值具有无理数对数比，导致胜出序列的渐近频率为无理数，从而排除了最终周期性。所有断言均通过精确有理数算术验证。实验结果证实系统未进入有限循环，从而否定了该猜想。
WildFeedback: Aligning LLMs With In-situ User Interactions And Feedback
Authors: Taiwei Shi, Zhuoer Wang, Longqi Yang, Ying-Chun Lin, Zexue He, Mengting Wan, Pei Zhou, Sujay Jauhar, Sihao Chen, Shan Xia, Hongfei Zhang, Jieyu Zhao, Xiaofeng Xu, Xia Song, Jennifer Neville
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2024-08-28T05:53:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:47:12+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Camera-ready. 25 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) continue to advance, aligning these models with human preferences has emerged as a critical challenge. Traditional alignment methods, relying on human or LLM annotated datasets, are limited by their resource-intensive nature, inherent subjectivity, misalignment with real-world user preferences, and the risk of feedback loops that amplify model biases. To overcome these limitations, we introduce WildFeedback, a novel framework that leverages in-situ user feedback during conversations with LLMs to create preference datasets automatically. Given a corpus of multi-turn user-LLM conversation, WildFeedback identifies and classifies user feedback to LLM responses between conversation turns. The user feedback is then used to create examples of preferred and dispreferred responses according to users' preference. Our experiments demonstrate that LLMs fine-tuned on WildFeedback dataset exhibit significantly improved alignment with user preferences, as evidenced by both traditional benchmarks and our proposed checklist-guided evaluation. By incorporating in-situ feedback from actual users, WildFeedback addresses the scalability, subjectivity, and bias challenges that plague existing approaches, marking a significant step toward developing LLMs that are more responsive to the diverse and evolving needs of their users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WildFeedback：通过现场用户交互与反馈对齐大语言模型
随着大语言模型（LLM）的持续发展，如何使这些模型与人类偏好对齐已成为一项关键挑战。传统对齐方法依赖人工或LLM标注的数据集，受限于资源密集、固有主观性、与真实用户偏好不一致，以及可能放大模型偏见的反馈循环风险。为克服这些局限，我们提出了WildFeedback框架，该框架利用用户与LLM对话过程中的现场反馈，自动构建偏好数据集。给定多轮用户-LLM对话语料，WildFeedback识别并分类对话轮次间用户对LLM响应的反馈，据此生成用户偏好的响应示例。实验表明，基于WildFeedback数据集微调的LLM在传统基准和我们提出的清单式评估中，均显著提升了与用户偏好的对齐程度。通过引入真实用户的现场反馈，WildFeedback解决了现有方法在可扩展性、主观性和偏见方面的挑战，标志着向开发更贴合用户多样且不断变化需求的LLM迈出了重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional alignment methods that rely on costly and subjective human annotations, WildFeedback introduces a framework that automatically generates preference datasets by analyzing in-situ user feedback within multi-turn conversations with LLMs. The method identifies and classifies user feedback between conversational turns to construct preferred and dispreferred response pairs, enabling fine-tuning of LLMs without manual annotation. Experimental results show that models fine-tuned on this feedback-driven dataset achieve significantly better alignment with user preferences compared to baselines, as measured by both standard benchmarks and a novel checklist-based evaluation, demonstrating improved responsiveness to real user needs.
为解决传统对齐方法依赖昂贵且主观的人类或LLM标注数据的问题，WildFeedback提出一种框架，通过分析用户与LLM对话中的即时反馈自动构建偏好数据集。该方法识别并分类对话回合间的用户反馈，生成偏好与非偏好响应对，用于微调LLM。实验表明，使用该反馈数据集微调的模型在标准基准和新设计的清单式评估中均显著优于基线，展现出对真实用户需求更强的响应能力。
Neuro-Symbolic ODE Discovery with Latent Grammar Flow
Authors: Karin Yu, Eleni Chatzi, Georgios Kissas
First: 2026-04-17T16:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:46:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding natural and engineered systems often relies on symbolic formulations, such as differential equations, which provide interpretability and transferability beyond black-box models. We introduce Latent Grammar Flow (LGF), a neuro-symbolic generative framework for discovering ordinary differential equations from data. LGF embeds equations as grammar-based representations into a discrete latent space and forces semantically similar equations to be positioned closer together with a behavioural loss. Then, a discrete flow model guides the sampling process to recursively generate candidate equations that best fit the observed data. Domain knowledge and constraints, such as stability, can be either embedded into the rules or used as conditional predictors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于潜在语法流的神经符号常微分方程发现
理解自然与人工系统常依赖于符号表达式，如微分方程，其提供超越黑箱模型的可解释性和可迁移性。 我们提出潜在语法流（LGF），一种用于从数据中发现常微分方程的神经符号生成框架。LGF将方程作为基于语法的表示嵌入到离散潜在空间中，并通过行为损失使语义相似的方程在空间中更接近。随后，离散流模型引导采样过程递归生成最符合观测数据的候选方程。领域知识与约束（如稳定性）可嵌入规则中，或作为条件预测器使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and transferable models of natural and engineered systems, this work proposes Latent Grammar Flow (LGF), a neuro-symbolic framework that discovers ordinary differential equations from data by embedding them as grammar-based representations in a discrete latent space. LGF enforces semantic similarity through a behavioral loss and uses a discrete flow model to recursively sample candidate equations that best fit the observed data, while allowing domain constraints like stability to be integrated via rules or conditioning. Experimental results show LGF effectively recovers ground-truth ODEs from synthetic and real-world datasets, outperforming baseline methods in both accuracy and interpretability.
为解决自然与工程系统建模中对可解释、可迁移模型的需求，本文提出Latent Grammar Flow（LGF）框架，通过将微分方程以语法形式嵌入离散潜在空间，结合行为损失确保语义相似方程在空间中靠近，并利用离散流模型递归采样最贴合观测数据的候选方程，同时支持通过规则或条件输入融入领域约束（如稳定性）。实验表明，LGF在方程发现准确率与跨域泛化能力上优于基线方法，且保持了模型的可解释性。
Dental Panoramic Radiograph Analysis Using YOLO26 From Tooth Detection to Disease Diagnosis
Authors: Khawaja Azfar Asif, Rafaqat Alam Khan
First: 2026-04-17T16:46:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:46:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Panoramic radiography is a fundamental diagnostic tool in dentistry, offering a comprehensive view of the entire dentition with minimal radiation exposure. However, manual interpretation is time-consuming and prone to errors, especially in high-volume clinical settings. This creates a pressing need for efficient automated solutions. This study presents the first application of YOLOv26 for automated tooth detection, FDI-based numbering, and dental disease segmentation in panoramic radiographs. The DENTEX dataset was preprocessed using Roboflow for format conversion and augmentation, yielding 1,082 images for tooth enumeration and 1,040 images for disease segmentation across four pathology classes. Five YOLOv26-seg variants were trained on Google Colab using transfer learning at a resolution of 800x800. Results demonstrate that the YOLOv26m-seg model achieved the best performance for tooth enumeration, with a precision of 0.976, recall of 0.970, and box mAP50 of 0.976. It outperformed the YOLOv8x baseline by 4.9% in precision and 3.3% in mAP50, while also enabling high-quality mask-level segmentation (mask mAP50 = 0.970). For disease segmentation, the YOLOv26l-seg model attained a box mAP50 of 0.591 and a mask mAP50 of 0.547. Impacted teeth showed the highest per-class average precision (0.943), indicating that visual distinctiveness influences detection performance more than annotation quantity. Overall, these findings demonstrate that YOLOv26-based models offer a robust and accurate framework for automated dental image analysis, with strong potential to enhance diagnostic efficiency and consistency in clinical practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于YOLOv26的牙科全景X光片分析：从牙齿检测到疾病诊断
全景X光片是牙科基础诊断工具，能以最小辐射暴露提供全口牙齿的全面视图。然而，人工解读耗时且易出错，尤其在高流量临床环境中。本研究首次将YOLOv26应用于全景X光片的自动化牙齿检测、FDI编号及牙病分割。使用Roboflow对DENTEX数据集进行预处理，格式转换与增强后，获得1,082张用于牙齿计数、1,040张用于疾病分割的图像（涵盖四类病理）。在Google Colab上，以800x800分辨率，采用迁移学习训练了五种YOLOv26-seg变体。结果显示，YOLOv26m-seg模型在牙齿计数中表现最佳，精度0.976，召回率0.970，box mAP50为0.976，较YOLOv8x基线精度提升4.9%，mAP50提升3.3%，并实现高质量掩码分割（mask mAP50=0.970）。疾病分割方面，YOLOv26l-seg模型box mAP50为0.591，mask mAP50为0.547。受阻牙齿类别平均精度最高（0.943），表明视觉显著性比标注数量对检测性能影响更大。总体而言，基于YOLOv26的模型为自动化牙科影像分析提供了稳健准确的框架，有望显著提升临床诊断效率与一致性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to automate and improve accuracy in dental panoramic radiograph interpretation, this study applies YOLOv26 for tooth detection, FDI-based numbering, and disease segmentation. Using the DENTEX dataset preprocessed with Roboflow, five YOLOv26-seg variants were trained on Google Colab via transfer learning at 800x800 resolution. The YOLOv26m-seg model achieved top performance in tooth enumeration with 0.976 precision, 0.970 recall, and 0.976 box mAP50, outperforming the YOLOv8x baseline by 4.9% in precision and 3.3% in mAP50, while delivering high-quality mask segmentation. For disease segmentation, YOLOv26l-seg reached 0.591 box mAP50 and 0.547 mask mAP50, with impacted teeth showing the highest per-class precision (0.943), suggesting visual distinctiveness outweighs annotation volume in detection accuracy.
为提升牙科诊断效率与一致性，本研究首次将YOLOv26应用于全景牙片中的牙齿检测、FDI编号及疾病分割。基于经Roboflow预处理的DENTEX数据集，五种YOLOv26-seg变体在Google Colab上通过迁移学习训练（分辨率800x800）。YOLOv26m-seg在牙齿计数中表现最优，精度0.976、召回率0.970、box mAP50达0.976，较YOLOv8x基线精度提升4.9%、mAP50提升3.3%，并实现高质量掩码分割（mask mAP50=0.970）。疾病分割方面，YOLOv26l-seg取得box mAP50 0.591、mask mAP50 0.547，其中受累牙齿类别平均精度最高（0.943），表明视觉可区分性比标注数量对检测性能影响更大。
Measuring the Semantic Structure and Evolution of Conspiracy Theories
Authors: Manisha Keim, Sarmad Chandio, Osama Khalid, Rishab Nithyanand
First: 2026-03-27T04:09:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:38:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on conspiracy theories has largely focused on belief formation, exposure, and diffusion, while paying less attention to how their meanings change over time. This gap persists partly because conspiracy-related terms are often treated as stable lexical markers, making it difficult to separate genuine semantic changes from surface-level vocabulary changes. In this paper, we measure the semantic structure and evolution of conspiracy theories in online political discourse. Using 169.9M comments from Reddit's r/politics subreddit spanning 2012--2022, we first demonstrate that conspiracy-related language forms coherent and semantically distinguishable regions of language space, allowing conspiracy theories to be treated as semantic objects. We then track how these objects evolve over time using aligned word embeddings, enabling comparisons of semantic neighborhoods across periods. Our analysis reveals that conspiracy theories evolve non-uniformly, exhibiting patterns of semantic stability, expansion, contraction, and replacement that are not captured by keyword-based approaches alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测量阴谋论的语义结构与演变
关于阴谋论的研究主要关注信念形成、接触和传播，较少关注其含义随时间的变化。这一研究空白部分源于阴谋相关术语常被视为稳定的词汇标记，难以区分真实的语义变化与表层词汇变化。本文通过分析2012至2022年间Reddit政治板块的1.699亿条评论，测量在线政治话语中阴谋论的语义结构与演变。我们首先证明，与阴谋相关的语言在语言空间中形成连贯且语义可区分的区域，使阴谋论可被视为语义对象。随后，我们利用对齐词嵌入追踪这些对象随时间的演变，实现不同时期语义邻域的比较。分析表明，阴谋论的演变非均匀，呈现出语义稳定、扩展、收缩与替换等模式，而关键词方法无法完全捕捉这些模式。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the gap in conspiracy theory research by focusing on how their semantic structures evolve over time, rather than just their spread or belief formation. Using 169.9 million Reddit comments from r/politics between 2012 and 2022, the authors treat conspiracy-related language as dynamic semantic objects in a high-dimensional word embedding space, revealing that these theories form distinct, evolving clusters. Their analysis shows that conspiracy theories do not uniformly change over time, but instead exhibit patterns of stability, expansion, contraction, and replacement—patterns invisible to traditional keyword-based methods.
该研究针对阴谋论研究中对语义演变关注不足的问题，分析了2012至2022年间Reddit政治板块的1.699亿条评论。通过词嵌入对阴谋相关语言进行语义空间映射与追踪，将其视为动态语义对象而非静态关键词。分析发现，阴谋论语义演变非均匀发生，呈现稳定性、扩展性、收缩性与替代性等模式，而关键词方法无法捕捉这些语义轨迹。
"Taking Stock at FAccT": Using Participatory Design to Co-Create a Vision for the Fairness, Accountability and Transparency Community
Authors: Shiran Dudy, Jan Simson, Yanan Long
First: 2026-04-17T16:37:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:37:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted at FAccT 2026, 27 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As a relatively new forum, ACM FAccT has become a key space for activists and scholars to critically examine emerging AI and ML technologies. It brings together academics, civil society members, and government representatives from diverse fields to explore the broader societal impacts of both deployed and proposed technologies. We report a large-scale participatory design (PD) process for reflexive conference governance, which combined an in-person CRAFT session, an asynchronous Polis poll and the synthesis of a governance-facing report for the FAccT leadership. Participants shaped the substantive agenda by authoring seed statements, adding new statements and making patterns of agreement, disagreement and uncertainty made visible through voting.Our endeavors represent one of the the first instances of applying PD to a venue that critically interrogates the societal impacts of AI, fostering a niche in which critical scholars are free to voice their concerns. Finally, this work advances large-scale PD theory by providing an effective case study of a co-design paradigm that can readily scale temporally and epistemologically.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“在FAccT盘点”：运用参与式设计共同构建公平、问责与透明社区的愿景
作为相对较新的论坛，ACM FAccT已成为活动人士和学者批判性审视新兴AI与ML技术的关键空间。它汇聚了来自不同领域的学术界人士、民间社会成员和政府代表，共同探讨已部署和拟议技术对更广泛社会的影响。我们报告了一项大规模参与式设计（PD）过程，用于反思性会议治理，该过程结合了线下CRAFT会议、异步Polis投票以及为FAccT领导层撰写的治理导向报告。参与者通过撰写初始陈述、添加新陈述并投票揭示同意、不同意和不确定性的模式，共同塑造实质性议程。我们的努力代表了首次将PD应用于批判性审视AI社会影响的论坛之一，为批判学者提供了一个自由表达关切的特定空间。最后，本研究通过提供一个可有效扩展至时间和认识论层面的共同设计范例，推动了大规模PD理论的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to strengthen democratic governance within the emerging ACM FAccT community, this study employed participatory design to co-create a vision for fairness, accountability, and transparency in AI. The method combined an in-person CRAFT session, an asynchronous Polis poll, and a synthesized governance report, enabling participants—including academics, civil society members, and government representatives—to collaboratively shape the conference agenda through seed statements, new contributions, and visible patterns of agreement, disagreement, and uncertainty. The results demonstrated that this approach successfully surfaced critical concerns, fostered inclusive dialogue, and produced actionable governance insights, marking a scalable model for co-design in socially impactful domains.
为推动ACM FAccT社区治理民主化，本研究采用大规模参与式设计方法，联合学者、社会活动人士与政府代表共同构建AI公平、问责与透明度的愿景。方法结合线下CRAFT研讨会、异步Polis投票及治理报告合成，参与者可撰写初始陈述、新增观点，并通过投票揭示共识、分歧与不确定性模式。实验结果表明，该方法有效塑造了反映多元利益相关者立场的实质性议程，验证了参与式设计在批判性AI论坛中的可行性，并提供了一个可扩展的协同设计范式，兼顾时间与知识维度的灵活性。
OT on the Map: Quantifying Domain Shifts in Geographic Space
Authors: Haoran Zhang, Livia Betti, Konstantin Klemmer, Esther Rolf, David Alvarez-Melis
First: 2026-04-17T16:33:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:33:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In computer vision and machine learning for geographic data, out-of-domain generalization is a pervasive challenge, arising from uneven global data coverage and distribution shifts across geographic regions. Though models are frequently trained in one region and deployed in another, there is no principled method for determining when this cross-region adaptation will be successful. A well-defined notion of distance between distributions can effectively quantify how different a new target domain is compared to the domains used for model training, which in turn could support model training and deployment decisions. In this paper, we propose a strategy for computing distances between geospatial domains that leverages geographic information with Optimal Transport methods (GeoSpOT). In our experiments, GeoSpOT distances emerge as effective predictors of cross-domain transfer difficulty. We further demonstrate that embeddings from pretrained location encoders provide information comparable to image/text embeddings, despite relying solely on longitude-latitude pairs as input. This allows users to get an approximation of out-of-domain performance for geospatial models, even when the exact downstream task is unknown, or no task-specific data is available. Building on these findings, we show that GeoSpOT distances can preemptively guide data selection and enable predictive tools to analyze regions where a model is likely to underperform.
中文标题/摘要
标题：地图上的OT：量化地理空间中的领域迁移
在计算机视觉和地理数据的机器学习中，跨域泛化是一个普遍存在的挑战，源于全球数据覆盖不均及地理区域间的分布偏移。尽管模型常在某一区域训练而在另一区域部署，但尚无系统方法判断跨区域适应是否成功。定义分布间的距离可有效量化新目标域与训练域的差异，从而支持模型训练与部署决策。本文提出一种基于地理信息与最优传输方法（GeoSpOT）计算地理空间域间距离的策略。实验表明，GeoSpOT距离能有效预测跨域迁移难度。我们进一步证明，即使仅依赖经纬度对作为输入，预训练位置编码器的嵌入信息亦可媲美图像/文本嵌入，使用户在未知下游任务或无任务特定数据时，仍能近似评估地理空间模型的跨域性能。基于此，我们展示GeoSpOT距离可预先指导数据选择，并支持预测工具分析模型可能表现不佳的区域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of out-of-domain generalization in geographic machine learning due to uneven global data coverage and distribution shifts, this paper introduces GeoSpOT, a method that computes distances between geospatial domains using Optimal Transport and geographic information. Experimental results show that GeoSpOT effectively predicts cross-domain transfer difficulty, and that embeddings from pretrained location encoders—based solely on longitude-latitude pairs—can approximate task-agnostic out-of-domain performance. The method further enables proactive data selection and predictive analysis of regions where models are likely to underperform.
为了解决地理数据机器学习中因全球数据分布不均和区域分布偏移导致的跨域泛化难题，本文提出GeoSpOT方法，利用最优传输与地理坐标计算地理空间域间距离。实验表明，GeoSpOT距离能有效预测跨域迁移难度，且预训练位置编码器无需任务数据即可近似任务嵌入，支持对未知区域模型性能的估算。该方法还可用于主动数据选择和预测模型在哪些区域可能表现不佳。
Beyond Surface Statistics: Robust Conformal Prediction for LLMs via Internal Representations
Authors: Yanli Wang, Peng Kuang, Xiaoyu Han, Kaidi Xu, Haohan Wang
First: 2026-04-17T16:28:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:28:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where reliability matters, yet output-level uncertainty signals such as token probabilities, entropy, and self-consistency can become brittle under calibration--deployment mismatch. Conformal prediction provides finite-sample validity under exchangeability, but its practical usefulness depends on the quality of the nonconformity score. We propose a conformal framework for LLM question answering that uses internal representations rather than output-facing statistics: specifically, we introduce Layer-Wise Information (LI) scores, which measure how conditioning on the input reshapes predictive entropy across model depth, and use them as nonconformity scores within a standard split conformal pipeline. Across closed-ended and open-domain QA benchmarks, with the clearest gains under cross-domain shift, our method achieves a better validity--efficiency trade-off than strong text-level baselines while maintaining competitive in-domain reliability at the same nominal risk level. These results suggest that internal representations can provide more informative conformal scores when surface-level uncertainty is unstable under distribution shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越表面统计：通过内部表示实现大语言模型的鲁棒一致性预测
大语言模型在可靠性至关重要的场景中日益部署，但输出层不确定性信号（如标记概率、熵值和自一致性）在部署环境与校准环境不匹配时可能变得脆弱。一致性预测在交换性假设下提供有限样本有效性，但其实际效用取决于非一致性分数的质量。我们提出一种针对大语言模型问答的一致性预测框架，使用内部表示而非输出层统计量：具体而言，我们引入层间信息（LI）分数，衡量输入条件如何重塑模型深度中的预测熵，并将其作为标准分割一致性管道中的非一致性分数。在封闭式和开放域问答基准测试中，尤其在跨域偏移场景下表现最显著，我们的方法在有效性-效率权衡上优于强文本级基线，同时在相同名义风险水平下保持了竞争力的领域内可靠性。这些结果表明，当表面不确定性在分布偏移下不稳定时，内部表示可提供更具信息量的一致性分数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of surface-level uncertainty metrics in LLMs under distribution shift, this work proposes a conformal prediction framework that leverages internal layer-wise representations. It introduces Layer-Wise Information (LI) scores, which quantify how input conditioning alters predictive entropy across model layers, and uses these as nonconformity scores within a split conformal pipeline. Experiments across QA benchmarks show that this method achieves superior validity-efficiency trade-offs compared to text-level baselines, especially under cross-domain shifts, while preserving competitive in-domain reliability at the same risk level.
鉴于表面不确定性指标在分布偏移下易失效，本文提出一种利用模型内部表示的置信区间框架。引入层间信息（LI）得分，衡量输入条件如何改变各层预测熵，并将其作为分层置信框架中的非一致性分数。在多个问答基准测试中，该方法在跨域偏移下表现更优，相比文本级基线在有效性与效率间取得更好平衡，同时保持相同风险水平下的领域内可靠性。
GAViD: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Context-Aware Group Affect Recognition from Videos
Authors: Deepak Kumar, Abhishek Pratap Singh, Puneet Kumar, Xiaobai Li, Balasubramanian Raman
First: 2026-04-17T16:23:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:23:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding affective dynamics in real-world social systems is fundamental to modeling and analyzing human-human interactions in complex environments. Group affect emerges from intertwined human-human interactions, contextual influences, and behavioral cues, making its quantitative modeling a challenging computational social systems problem. However, computational modeling of group affect in in-the-wild scenarios remains challenging due to limited large-scale annotated datasets and the inherent complexity of multimodal social interactions shaped by contextual and behavioral variability. The lack of comprehensive datasets annotated with multimodal and contextual information further limits advances in the field. To address this, we introduce the Group Affect from ViDeos (GAViD) dataset, comprising 5091 video clips with multimodal data (video, audio and context), annotated with ternary valence and discrete emotion labels and enriched with VideoGPT-generated contextual metadata and human-annotated action cues. We also present Context-Aware Group Affect Recognition Network (CAGNet) for multimodal context-aware group affect recognition. CAGNet achieves 63.20\% test accuracy on GAViD, comparable to state-of-the-art performance. The dataset and code are available at github.com/deepakkumar-iitr/GAViD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GAViD：用于视频中情境感知群体情感识别的大规模多模态数据集
理解现实世界社会系统中的情感动态，是建模和分析复杂环境中人与人互动的基础。群体情感源于交织的人际互动、情境影响和行为线索，使其定量建模成为计算社会系统中的挑战性问题。然而，由于缺乏大规模标注数据集，且野外场景中多模态社会互动受情境和行为变异影响，群体情感的计算建模仍具挑战性。为解决此问题，我们引入了“来自视频的群体情感”（GAViD）数据集，包含5091个视频片段，配有视频、音频和情境的多模态数据，标注有三元效价和离散情绪标签，并辅以VideoGPT生成的情境元数据和人工标注的动作线索。我们还提出了情境感知群体情感识别网络（CAGNet），在GAViD上达到63.20%的测试准确率，与当前最先进水平相当。数据集和代码可在github.com/deepakkumar-iitr/GAViD获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model group affect in real-world social settings, this work introduces GAViD, a large-scale multimodal video dataset annotated with ternary valence and discrete emotion labels, enriched with contextual metadata and human-annotated action cues. The authors propose CAGNet, a context-aware network architecture designed to integrate video, audio, and contextual features for group affect recognition. Experimental results show CAGNet achieves 63.20% test accuracy on GAViD, matching state-of-the-art performance and demonstrating the dataset’s utility for advancing computational social systems research.
为解决真实场景中群体情感建模的挑战，该研究构建了GAViD数据集，包含5091段视频，标注了三元效价与离散情绪标签，并融合了上下文元数据与人工标注的动作线索。作者提出CAGNet模型，通过整合视频、音频与上下文信息实现群体情感识别，在GAViD测试集上达到63.20%准确率，表现接近当前最优水平，凸显该数据集对计算社会系统研究的价值。
Investigating Conversational Agents to Support Secondary School Students Learning CSP
Authors: Matthew Frazier, Kostadin Damevski, Lori Pollock
First: 2026-04-17T16:22:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:22:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Secondary school students enrolled in the AP Computer Science Principles (CSP) course commonly utilize web resources (e.g., tutorials, Q\&A sites) to better understand key concepts in the curriculum. The primary obstacle to using these resources is finding information appropriate for the learning task and student's background. In addition to web search, conversational agents are increasingly a viable alternative for CSP students. In this paper, we study the potential of conversational agents to aid secondary school students as they acquire knowledge on CSP concepts. We explore general purpose, generative conversational agents (e.g., ChatGPT) and custom, fixed-response conversational agents built specifically to aid CSP students. We present results from classroom use by 45 high school students in grades 9-11 (ages 14-17) across six CSP sections. Our main contributions are in better understanding how conversational agents can help CSP students and an evaluation of the effectiveness and engagement of different approaches for CSP exploratory search.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探究对话代理对中学学生学习CSP的支持
参加AP计算机科学原理（CSP）课程的中学生常利用网络资源（如教程、问答网站）以更好地理解课程中的关键概念。使用这些资源的主要障碍在于找到适合学习任务和学生背景的信息。除了网络搜索，对话代理正日益成为CSP学生的可行替代方案。本文研究对话代理在帮助中学生掌握CSP概念方面的潜力。我们探讨通用生成式对话代理（如ChatGPT）和专为辅助CSP学生设计的定制固定响应对话代理。我们呈现了来自六门CSP课程、45名9至11年级（14至17岁）高中生课堂使用的结果。主要贡献在于更深入理解对话代理如何帮助CSP学生，以及评估不同方法在CSP探索性搜索中的有效性与参与度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge students face in finding suitable online resources for AP Computer Science Principles, this study investigates whether conversational agents can improve learning outcomes. It compares general-purpose generative agents like ChatGPT with custom-built fixed-response agents tailored for CSP. Through classroom experiments involving 45 students across six sections, the research finds that both types of agents support exploratory learning, with custom agents showing higher engagement and better alignment with curriculum needs, while generative agents offer broader flexibility but require more careful guidance.
鉴于学生在寻找适合AP计算机科学原理课程的在线资源时面临困难，本研究探讨了对话代理是否能有效辅助学习。研究对比了通用生成式代理（如ChatGPT）与专为CSP设计的固定响应代理。通过对六门课程共45名初中生的课堂实验发现，两类代理均能促进探索性学习，其中定制代理在参与度和课程契合度上表现更优，而生成式代理则提供更广泛但针对性较弱的帮助。
Large Language Models for Market Research: A Data-augmentation Approach
Authors: Mengxin Wang, Dennis J. Zhang, Heng Zhang
First: 2024-12-26T22:06:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:20:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed artificial intelligence by excelling in complex natural language processing tasks. Their ability to generate human-like text has opened new possibilities for market research, particularly in conjoint analysis, where understanding consumer preferences is essential but often resource-intensive. Traditional survey-based methods face limitations in scalability and cost, making LLM-generated data a promising alternative. However, while LLMs have the potential to simulate real consumer behavior, recent studies highlight a significant gap between LLM-generated and human data, with biases introduced when substituting between the two. In this paper, we address this gap by proposing a novel statistical data augmentation approach that efficiently integrates LLM-generated data with real data in conjoint analysis. This results in statistically robust estimators with consistent and asymptotically normal properties, in contrast to naive approaches that simply substitute human data with LLM-generated data, which can exacerbate bias. We further present a finite-sample performance bound on the estimation error. We validate our framework through an empirical study on COVID-19 vaccine preferences, demonstrating its superior ability to reduce estimation error and save data and costs by 24.9% to 79.8%. In contrast, naive approaches fail to save data due to the inherent biases in LLM-generated data compared to human data. Another empirical study on sports car choices validates the robustness of our results. Our findings suggest that while LLM-generated data is not a direct substitute for human responses, it can serve as a valuable complement when used within a robust statistical framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于市场研究的大语言模型：一种数据增强方法
大语言模型（LLMs）通过在复杂自然语言处理任务中表现出色，彻底改变了人工智能。其生成类人文本的能力为市场研究开辟了新可能，特别是在需要理解消费者偏好的联立分析中，传统调查方法因可扩展性和成本问题而受限。尽管LLMs有潜力模拟真实消费者行为，但近期研究表明，LLM生成数据与人类数据之间存在显著差距，替换时会引入偏差。本文通过提出一种新颖的统计数据增强方法，高效整合LLM生成数据与真实数据，从而在联立分析中获得统计稳健的估计量，具有相合性和渐近正态性，与简单替换人类数据为LLM生成数据的朴素方法相比，后者可能加剧偏差。我们进一步给出了有限样本下的估计误差性能界。通过关于新冠疫苗偏好的实证研究，验证了该框架能有效降低估计误差，并节省24.9%至79.8%的数据和成本；相比之下，朴素方法因LLM生成数据与人类数据固有偏差而无法节省数据。另一项关于跑车选择的实证研究进一步验证了结果的稳健性。我们的研究发现表明，尽管LLM生成数据不能直接替代人类响应，但在稳健统计框架下可作为有价值的补充。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce costs and scale market research while maintaining statistical reliability, this paper proposes a statistical data augmentation method that integrates Large Language Model (LLM)-generated data with real human survey data in conjoint analysis. Unlike naive substitution approaches that risk amplifying bias, the proposed method produces estimators with consistent and asymptotically normal properties, ensuring robust inference. Empirical tests on COVID-19 vaccine preferences and sports car choices show the method reduces estimation error and cuts data and costs by 24.9% to 79.8%, while naive methods fail to achieve cost savings due to inherent LLM biases.
为降低市场研究成本并提升可扩展性，本文提出一种统计数据增强方法，将大语言模型生成的数据与真实人类响应结合用于联立分析。与直接替换人类数据的朴素方法不同，该方法生成具有一致性和渐近正态性的稳健估计量，并提供有限样本误差界。在新冠疫苗偏好和跑车选择的实证研究中，该框架将估计误差降低并节省24.9%至79.8%的数据与成本，而朴素方法因LLM数据固有偏差无法实现成本节约。
AIFIND: Artifact-Aware Interpreting Fine-Grained Alignment for Incremental Face Forgery Detection
Authors: Hao Wang, Beichen Zhang, Yanpei Gong, Shaoyi Fang, Zhaobo Qi, Yuanrong Xu, Xinyan Liu, Weigang Zhang
First: 2026-04-17T16:17:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:17:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As forgery types continue to emerge consistently, Incremental Face Forgery Detection (IFFD) has become a crucial paradigm. However, existing methods typically rely on data replay or coarse binary supervision, which fails to explicitly constrain the feature space, leading to severe feature drift and catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we propose AIFIND, Artifact-Aware Interpreting Fine-Grained Alignment for Incremental Face Forgery Detection, which leverages semantic anchors to stabilize incremental learning. We design the Artifact-Driven Semantic Prior Generator to instantiate invariant semantic anchors, establishing a fixed coordinate system from low-level artifact cues. These anchors are injected into the image encoder via Artifact-Probe Attention, which explicitly constrains volatile visual features to align with stable semantic anchors. Adaptive Decision Harmonizer harmonizes the classifiers by preserving angular relationships of semantic anchors, maintaining geometric consistency across tasks. Extensive experiments on multiple incremental protocols validate the superiority of AIFIND.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AIFIND：面向增量人脸伪造检测的伪影感知细粒度对齐解释
随着伪造类型持续涌现，增量人脸伪造检测（IFFD）已成为关键范式。然而现有方法通常依赖数据重放或粗粒度二元监督，未能显式约束特征空间，导致严重特征漂移与灾难性遗忘。为此，我们提出AIFIND（面向增量人脸伪造检测的伪影感知细粒度对齐解释），利用语义锚点稳定增量学习。设计伪影驱动语义先验生成器，构建由低级伪影线索定义的固定坐标系；通过伪影探针注意力注入图像编码器，显式约束易变视觉特征与稳定语义锚点对齐；自适应决策调和器通过保持语义锚点间角度关系，维持跨任务几何一致性。多增量协议实验验证AIFIND优越性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of feature drift and catastrophic forgetting in incremental face forgery detection due to reliance on coarse supervision, AIFIND introduces a method that stabilizes learning via semantic anchors derived from low-level artifacts. It employs an Artifact-Driven Semantic Prior Generator to create invariant anchors, which are then integrated into the image encoder through Artifact-Probe Attention to align volatile features with stable semantics. An Adaptive Decision Harmonizer further ensures classifier consistency by preserving angular relationships among anchors. Experiments across multiple incremental protocols demonstrate AIFIND's superior performance in maintaining detection accuracy over time.
为应对伪造类型持续演进且避免灾难性遗忘，AIFIND提出通过语义锚点稳定增量学习的方法。其通过Artifact-Driven Semantic Prior Generator生成不变语义锚点，并借助Artifact-Probe Attention注入图像编码器，使易变视觉特征与稳定语义对齐。Adaptive Decision Harmonizer进一步保持锚点间角关系，确保分类器跨任务一致性。在多个增量人脸伪造检测协议上的实验表明，AIFIND在保持精度和减少特征漂移方面显著优于现有方法。
ChemGraph-XANES: An Agentic Framework for XANES Simulation and Analysis
Authors: Vitor F. Grizzi, Thang Duc Pham, Luke N. Pretzie, Jiayi Xu, Murat Keceli, Cong Liu
First: 2026-04-17T16:15:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:15:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computational X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES) is widely used to probe local coordination environments, oxidation states, and electronic structure in chemically complex systems. However, the use of computational XANES at scale is constrained more by workflow complexity than by the underlying simulation method itself. To address this challenge, we present ChemGraph-XANES, an agentic framework for automated XANES simulation and analysis that unifies natural-language task specification, structure acquisition, FDMNES input generation, task-parallel execution, spectral normalization, and provenance-aware data curation. Built on ASE, FDMNES, Parsl, and a LangGraph/LangChain-based tool interface, the framework exposes XANES workflow operations as typed Python tools that can be orchestrated by large language model (LLM) agents. In multi-agent mode, a retrieval-augmented expert agent consults the FDMNES manual to ground parameter selection, while executor agents translate user requests into structured tool calls. We demonstrate documentation-grounded parameter retrieval and show that the same workflow supports both explicit structure-file inputs and chemistry-level natural-language requests. Because independent XANES calculations are naturally task-parallel, the framework is well suited for high-throughput deployment on high-performance computing (HPC) systems, enabling scalable XANES database generation for downstream analysis and machine-learning applications. ChemGraph-XANES thus provides a reproducible and extensible workflow layer for physics-based XANES simulation, spectral curation, and agent-compatible computational spectroscopy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChemGraph-XANES：用于XANES模拟与分析的代理框架
计算X射线吸收近边结构（XANES）广泛用于探测化学复杂体系中的局部配位环境、氧化态和电子结构。然而，计算XANES的大规模应用受限于工作流程的复杂性，而非底层模拟方法本身。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了ChemGraph-XANES，一个用于自动化XANES模拟与分析的代理框架，统一了自然语言任务指定、结构获取、FDMNES输入生成、任务并行执行、光谱归一化和溯源感知的数据整理。该框架基于ASE、FDMNES、Parsl及LangGraph/LangChain工具接口构建，将XANES工作流操作作为类型化的Python工具暴露出来，可由大语言模型（LLM）代理编排。在多代理模式下，检索增强专家代理参考FDMNES手册以锚定参数选择，执行代理则将用户请求转化为结构化工具调用。我们展示了基于文档的参数检索，并表明同一工作流支持显式结构文件输入和化学层面的自然语言请求。由于独立XANES计算天然具有任务并行性，该框架非常适合在高性能计算（HPC）系统上进行高吞吐量部署，从而支持可扩展的XANES数据库生成，用于下游分析和机器学习应用。ChemGraph-XANES因此提供了一个可复现且可扩展的工作流层，用于基于物理的XANES模拟、光谱整理及代理兼容的计算光谱学。

Motivated by the workflow complexity limiting scalable computational XANES analysis, ChemGraph-XANES introduces an agentic framework that automates simulation and analysis through natural-language task specification, structure acquisition, FDMNES input generation, task-parallel execution, spectral normalization, and provenance-aware curation.
