Hybrid Latents: Geometry-Appearance-Aware Surfel Splatting
Authors: Neel Kelkar, Simon Niedermayr, Klaus Engel, Rüdiger Westermann
First: 2026-04-16T12:13:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:12:24+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a hybrid Gaussian-hash-grid radiance representation for reconstructing 2D Gaussian scene models from multi-view images. Similar to NeST splatting, our approach reduces the entanglement between geometry and appearance common in NeRF-based models, but adds per-Gaussian latent features alongside hash-grid features to bias the optimizer toward a separation of low- and high-frequency scene components. This explicit frequency-based decomposition reduces the tendency of high-frequency texture to compensate for geometric errors. Encouraging Gaussians with hard opacity falloffs further strengthens the separation between geometry and appearance, improving both geometry reconstruction and rendering efficiency. Finally, probabilistic pruning combined with a sparsity-inducing BCE opacity loss allows redundant Gaussians to be turned off, yielding a minimal set of Gaussians sufficient to represent the scene. Using both synthetic and real-world datasets, we compare against the state of the art in Gaussian-based novel-view synthesis and demonstrate superior reconstruction fidelity with an order of magnitude fewer primitives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：混合隐变量：几何-外观感知的Surfel光栅化
我们提出了一种混合高斯-哈希网格辐射表示方法，用于从多视角图像重建2D高斯场景模型。与NeST光栅化类似，我们的方法减少了NeRF模型中几何与外观常见的耦合问题，同时在每个高斯上添加隐变量特征，与哈希网格特征结合，引导优化器将低频与高频场景成分分离。这种显式的频域分解降低了高频纹理补偿几何误差的趋势。鼓励具有硬遮挡衰减的高斯进一步强化几何与外观的分离，从而提升几何重建质量与渲染效率。最后，结合概率剪枝与稀疏诱导的BCE遮挡损失，可关闭冗余高斯，获得足以表示场景的最小高斯集合。我们在合成与真实数据集上对比当前最先进的高斯新视角合成方法，证明在使用数量级更少的基元情况下，重建保真度更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the entanglement of geometry and appearance in NeRF-based models, this work proposes a hybrid Gaussian-hash-grid radiance representation that decouples low- and high-frequency scene components via per-Gaussian latent features. By encouraging hard opacity falloffs and using a sparsity-inducing BCE opacity loss with probabilistic pruning, the method reduces redundant Gaussians while improving geometry reconstruction and rendering efficiency. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show it achieves superior reconstruction fidelity with an order of magnitude fewer Gaussians than state-of-the-art Gaussian-based methods.
为了解决NeRF类模型中几何与外观信息耦合的问题，该研究提出一种混合高斯-哈希网格辐射表示方法，通过为每个高斯添加隐式特征，显式分离低频几何与高频外观成分。方法通过频率分解引导优化，减少高频纹理对几何误差的补偿，并利用硬遮挡衰减增强分离效果。实验表明，在合成与真实数据集上，该方法在显著减少高斯数量（达一个数量级）的同时，实现了优于当前最优方法的重建精度与渲染效率。
DENALI: A Dataset Enabling Non-Line-of-Sight Spatial Reasoning with Low-Cost LiDARs
Authors: Nikhil Behari, Diego Rivero, Luke Apostolides, Suman Ghosh, Paul Pu Liang, Ramesh Raskar
First: 2026-04-17T16:12:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:12:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Consumer LiDARs in mobile devices and robots typically output a single depth value per pixel. Yet internally, they record full time-resolved histograms containing direct and multi-bounce light returns; these multi-bounce returns encode rich non-line-of-sight (NLOS) cues that can enable perception of hidden objects in a scene. However, severe hardware limitations of consumer LiDARs make NLOS reconstruction with conventional methods difficult. In this work, we motivate a complementary direction: enabling NLOS perception with low-cost LiDARs through data-driven inference. We present DENALI, the first large-scale real-world dataset of space-time histograms from low-cost LiDARs capturing hidden objects. We capture time-resolved LiDAR histograms for 72,000 hidden-object scenes across diverse object shapes, positions, lighting conditions, and spatial resolutions. Using our dataset, we show that consumer LiDARs can enable accurate, data-driven NLOS perception. We further identify key scene and modeling factors that limit performance, as well as simulation-fidelity gaps that hinder current sim-to-real transfer, motivating future work toward scalable NLOS vision with consumer LiDARs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DENALI：支持低成本激光雷达非视线空间推理的数据集
移动设备和机器人中的消费级激光雷达通常每像素输出单个深度值。然而，其内部记录的是包含直接和多反射光返回的完整时间分辨直方图；这些多反射返回编码了丰富的非视线（NLOS）线索，可用于感知场景中的隐藏物体。但消费级激光雷达的硬件限制使得传统方法难以实现NLOS重建。本文提出互补方向：通过数据驱动推理，利用低成本激光雷达实现NLOS感知。我们提出了DENALI，这是首个由低成本激光雷达采集的、包含隐藏物体的空间-时间直方图的大规模真实世界数据集。我们为72,000个隐藏物体场景采集了时间分辨激光雷达直方图，涵盖多样化的物体形状、位置、光照条件和空间分辨率。利用该数据集，我们证明消费级激光雷达可实现准确的数据驱动NLOS感知。我们进一步识别了限制性能的关键场景与建模因素，以及阻碍当前仿真到现实迁移的仿真保真度差距，为未来实现基于消费级激光雷达的可扩展NLOS视觉研究提供方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the untapped potential of consumer LiDARs to perceive hidden objects via multi-bounce light returns, this work introduces DENALI, the first large-scale real-world dataset capturing space-time histograms from low-cost LiDARs across 72,000 hidden-object scenes under varied conditions. The method leverages these time-resolved histograms to enable data-driven non-line-of-sight (NLOS) perception without requiring expensive hardware or complex physical models. Experimental results demonstrate that consumer LiDARs can achieve accurate NLOS reconstruction using this dataset, while also revealing critical performance bottlenecks such as scene geometry, lighting, and simulation fidelity gaps that impede sim-to-real transfer.
为挖掘消费级激光雷达通过多反射光返回实现非视线感知的潜力，本研究提出DENALI数据集——首个大规模真实场景下低成本激光雷达空间-时间直方图数据集，涵盖72,000个隐藏物体场景，包含多样化的物体形状、位置、光照与分辨率。实验表明，基于该数据集的深度学习方法可实现准确的非视线感知，同时识别出场景复杂度与仿真精度不足等关键性能瓶颈，为未来构建可扩展的非视线视觉系统提供方向。
Saturation-Aware Space-Variant Blind Image Deblurring
Authors: Muhammad Z. Alam, Larry Stetsiuk, Arooba Zeshan
First: 2026-04-17T16:10:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:10:19+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 12 Figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a novel saturation aware space variant blind image deblurring framework designed to address challenges posed by saturated pixels in deblurring under high dynamic range and low light conditions. The proposed approach effectively segments the image based on blur intensity and proximity to saturation, leveraging a pre estimated Light Spread Function to mitigate stray light effects. By accurately estimating the true radiance of saturated regions using the dark channel prior, our method enhances the deblurring process without introducing artifacts like ringing. Experimental evaluations on both synthetic and real world datasets demonstrate that the framework improves deblurring outcomes across various scenarios showcasing superior performance compared to state of the art saturation-aware and general purpose methods. This adaptability highlights the framework potential integration with existing and emerging blind image deblurring techniques.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于饱和度感知的空间变盲图像去模糊框架
本文提出了一种新颖的饱和度感知空间变盲图像去模糊框架，旨在解决高动态范围和低光照条件下去模糊时饱和像素带来的挑战。该方法根据模糊强度和接近饱和的程度对图像进行分割，并利用预估的光场函数减轻杂散光影响。通过利用暗通道先验准确估计饱和区域的真实辐照度，本方法在去模糊过程中避免引入如振铃等伪影。在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，该框架在多种场景下均优于现有饱和度感知及通用方法，展现出良好的适应性，可与现有及新兴的盲图像去模糊技术集成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of deblurring images with saturated pixels under high dynamic range and low light conditions, this paper introduces a saturation-aware space-variant blind image deblurring framework. The method segments the image based on blur intensity and proximity to saturation, using a pre-estimated Light Spread Function to reduce stray light effects. It estimates true radiance in saturated regions via the dark channel prior, avoiding artifacts like ringing. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show the framework outperforms state-of-the-art saturation-aware and general methods, demonstrating robustness across diverse scenarios.
为解决高动态范围和低光条件下饱和像素对图像去模糊带来的挑战，本文提出一种饱和感知的空间变差盲图像去模糊框架。该方法依据模糊强度与饱和度邻近性对图像进行分割，利用预估的光场函数抑制杂散光影响，并通过暗通道先验准确估计饱和区域的真实辐照度，避免产生振铃等伪影。在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，该框架在多种场景下均优于现有饱和感知及通用去模糊方法，展现出良好的鲁棒性与可扩展性，可集成于现有去模糊技术中。
Understanding New-Knowledge-Induced Factual Hallucinations in LLMs: Analysis and Interpretation
Authors: Renfei Dang, Peng Hu, Zhejian Lai, Changjiang Gao, Min Zhang, Shujian Huang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-11-04T14:55:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:08:23+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior works have shown that fine-tuning on new knowledge can induce factual hallucinations in large language models (LLMs), leading to incorrect outputs when evaluated on previously known information. However, the specific manifestations of such hallucination and its underlying mechanisms remain insufficiently understood. Our work addresses this gap by designing a controlled dataset \textit{Biography-Reasoning}, and conducting a fine-grained analysis across multiple knowledge types and two task types, including knowledge question answering (QA) and knowledge reasoning tasks. We find that hallucinations not only severely affect tasks involving newly introduced knowledge, but also propagate to other evaluation tasks. Moreover, when fine-tuning on a dataset in which a specific knowledge type consists entirely of new knowledge, LLMs exhibit elevated hallucination tendencies. This suggests that the degree of unfamiliarity within a particular knowledge type, rather than the overall proportion of new knowledge, is a stronger driver of hallucinations. Through interpretability analysis, we show that learning new knowledge weakens the model's attention to key entities in the input question, leading to an over-reliance on surrounding context and a higher risk of hallucination. Conversely, reintroducing a small amount of known knowledge during the later stages of training restores attention to key entities and substantially mitigates hallucination behavior. Finally, we demonstrate that disrupted attention patterns can propagate across lexically similar contexts, facilitating the spread of hallucinations beyond the original task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解大语言模型中新知识引发的事实性幻觉：分析与解释
先前研究已表明，对新知识进行微调会导致大语言模型（LLMs）产生事实性幻觉，使其在评估已知信息时输出错误结果。然而，此类幻觉的具体表现及其内在机制尚未充分理解。本工作通过设计可控数据集Biography-Reasoning，并对多种知识类型和两类任务（知识问答与知识推理）进行细粒度分析，填补了这一空白。我们发现，幻觉不仅严重影响涉及新引入知识的任务，还会传播至其他评估任务。此外，当微调数据集中某一特定知识类型完全由新知识构成时，LLMs表现出更高的幻觉倾向。这表明，特定知识类型中的陌生程度，而非新知识的整体比例，是驱动幻觉的更强因素。通过可解释性分析，我们发现学习新知识会削弱模型对输入问题中关键实体的关注，导致过度依赖上下文，从而增加幻觉风险。相反，在训练后期重新引入少量已知知识，可恢复对关键实体的关注，并显著缓解幻觉行为。最后，我们证明，注意力模式的紊乱可传播至语义相似的上下文，从而促进幻觉在原任务之外的扩散。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how fine-tuning large language models on new knowledge induces factual hallucinations, particularly when evaluated on previously known information. Using a controlled dataset called Biography-Reasoning, the authors analyze hallucination patterns across multiple knowledge types and two task types—QA and reasoning—and find that hallucinations not only affect tasks with new knowledge but also propagate to unrelated tasks. They observe that hallucinations increase when a knowledge type contains entirely unfamiliar content, indicating unfamiliarity—not overall newness—is the key driver. Interpretability analysis reveals that learning new knowledge causes models to shift attention away from key entities in questions, relying instead on surrounding context, which increases hallucination risk. Reintroducing known knowledge later in training restores focus on key entities and reduces hallucinations. The study also shows that disrupted attention patterns can spread across similar contexts, enabling hallucinations to generalize beyond the original task.
本研究探讨了大语言模型在新知识微调后产生事实幻觉的机制，尤其在评估原有知识时出现错误。作者构建了控制数据集Biography-Reasoning，分析不同知识类型和两类任务（问答与推理）中的幻觉表现。发现幻觉不仅影响新知识任务，还会扩散至其他任务，尤其当某类知识完全陌生时。可解释性分析表明，学习新知识使模型注意力偏离关键实体，转而依赖上下文，从而增加幻觉风险；后期引入少量已知知识可恢复对实体的关注并显著缓解幻觉。更重要的是，注意力模式的破坏可跨语义相似上下文传播，使幻觉扩散至原任务之外。
Sketching the Readout of Large Language Models for Scalable Data Attribution and Valuation
Authors: Yide Ran, Jianwen Xie, Minghui Wang, Wenjin Zheng, Denghui Zhang, Chuan Li, Zhaozhuo Xu
First: 2026-04-17T16:07:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T16:07:11+00:00
Comments: 54 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data attribution and valuation are critical for understanding data-model synergy for Large Language Models (LLMs), yet existing gradient-based methods suffer from scalability challenges on LLMs. Inspired by human cognition, where decision making relies on a focused readout of relevant memories rather than replaying all pathways, we introduce RISE (Readout Influence Sketching Estimator). Instead of computing and indexing gradients across the entire LLM, RISE focuses on influence hotspots at the output layer, where influence signals concentrate, and the gradient admits a decomposed outer-product form. This enables a dual-channel representation combining a lexical residual channel (RH) and a semantic projected-error channel (GH). Applying CountSketch projections to these channels achieves strong compression while maintaining accurate attribution. Across the OLMo (1B-32B) and Pythia (14M-6.9B) families, RISE reduces index storage by up to 112$\times$ compared to RapidIn and scales to 32B parameters LLM, where gradient-based baselines such as RapidIn and ZO-Inf become memory-infeasible. We evaluate RISE on two paradigms: (1) retrospective attribution, retrieving influential training examples for specific predictions, and (2) prospective valuation, scoring candidate data utility zero-shot. We validate RISE on three tasks: Howdy backdoor data detection, Finance-Medical domain separation, and Brain Rot high-quality data selection. In a closed-loop Brain Rot study, continued pretraining on RISE-selected data yields consistent downstream improvements. Overall, RISE provides a practical and scalable primitive for influence analysis and training-data selection in modern large language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可扩展数据归因与估值的大语言模型读出草图方法
数据归因与估值对于理解大语言模型（LLM）与数据的协同作用至关重要，但现有基于梯度的方法在LLM上面临可扩展性挑战。受人类认知启发，决策依赖于对相关记忆的聚焦读出而非重放所有路径，我们提出RISE（读出影响草图估计器）。RISE不计算并索引整个LLM的梯度，而是聚焦于输出层的影响热点，此处影响信号集中，梯度可分解为外积形式。这使RISE能结合词法残差通道（RH）与语义投影误差通道（GH）的双通道表示。对这些通道应用CountSketch投影可实现强压缩同时保持准确归因。在OLMo（1B-32B）和Pythia（14M-6.9B）系列模型中，RISE相比RapidIn将索引存储减少高达112倍，并可扩展至32B参数LLM，而基于梯度的基线方法如RapidIn和ZO-Inf则因内存不可行。我们在两种范式下评估RISE：（1）回顾性归因，检索特定预测的有影响力训练样本；（2）前瞻性估值，零样本评分候选数据效用。在三个任务中验证RISE：Howdy后门数据检测、金融-医学领域分离、Brain Rot高质量数据选择。在闭环Brain Rot研究中，持续在RISE选中的数据上预训练可带来持续下游性能提升。总体而言，RISE为现代大语言模型的影响分析与训练数据选择提供了一种实用且可扩展的基础方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scalability limitations of gradient-based methods for data attribution in large language models, the authors propose RISE, a technique inspired by human memory readout mechanisms. RISE focuses on influence hotspots at the output layer, decomposing gradients into lexical residual and semantic projected-error channels, then compresses them using CountSketch projections. Experiments across OLMo and Pythia models show RISE reduces index storage by up to 112x compared to RapidIn and scales to 32B-parameter models where gradient methods fail. Evaluated on backdoor detection, domain separation, and data selection tasks, RISE consistently improves downstream performance when selected data is used in continued pretraining.
为解决大语言模型中梯度方法在数据归因时的可扩展性瓶颈，作者提出RISE方法，受人类记忆读取机制启发，聚焦输出层影响热点，将梯度分解为词法残差通道与语义投影误差通道，并通过CountSketch投影压缩。在OLMo和Pythia模型上，RISE相比RapidIn存储开销降低最高112倍，可扩展至32B参数模型（梯度方法无法处理）。在后门检测、领域分离和数据选择任务中，RISE实现精准回溯归因与零样本前瞻性估值，闭合循环预训练所选数据可稳定提升下游性能。
The Informational Cost of Agency: A Bounded Measure of Interaction Efficiency for Deployed Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Wael Hafez, Cameron Reid, Amit Nazeri
First: 2026-03-01T21:38:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:57:21+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployed RL agents operate in closed-loop systems where reliable performance depends on maintaining coherent coupling between observations, actions, and outcomes. Current monitoring approaches rely on reward and task metrics, measures that are reactive by design and blind to structural degradation that precedes performance collapse. We argue that deployment monitoring is fundamentally a question about uncertainty resolution: whether the agent's observations and actions continue to reduce uncertainty about outcomes, and whether outcomes constrain what the agent must have done. Information theory directly operationalizes this question, entropy quantifies uncertainty, and mutual information quantifies its resolution across the loop. We introduce Bipredictability (P), the fraction of the total uncertainty budget converted into shared predictability across the observation, action, outcome loop. A theoretical property is a provable classical upper bound P is less than or equal to 0.5, independent of domain, task, or agent, a structural consequence of Shannon entropy rather than an empirical observation. When agency is present, a penalty suppresses P strictly below this ceiling, confirmed at P equals 0.33 across trained agents. To operationalize P as a real time monitoring signal, we introduce the Information Digital Twin (IDT), an auxiliary architecture that computes P and its directional components from the observable interaction stream without access to model internals. Across 168 perturbation trials spanning eight perturbation types and two policy architectures, IDT based monitoring detected 89.3 percent of coupling degradations versus 44.0 percent for reward based monitoring, with 4.4 times lower median latency. These results establish Bipredictability as a principled, bounded, and computable prerequisite signal for closed loop self regulation in deployed reinforcement learning systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理的信息成本：部署强化学习中交互效率的有界度量
部署的强化学习代理在闭环系统中运行，其可靠性能依赖于观测、动作与结果之间的协调耦合。当前的监控方法依赖于奖励和任务指标，这些指标本质上是反应式的，且对性能崩溃前的结构退化视而不见。我们认为，部署监控本质上是关于不确定性解决的问题：代理的观测和动作是否持续降低对结果的不确定性，以及结果是否约束了代理必须采取的动作。信息论直接将这一问题形式化，熵量化不确定性，互信息量化循环中不确定性解决的程度。我们引入双可预测性（P），即观测、动作、结果循环中总不确定性预算转化为共享可预测性的比例。其理论性质是一个可证明的经典上界，P ≤ 0.5，与领域、任务或代理无关，这是香农熵的结构结果而非经验观察。当代理存在时，惩罚机制将P严格压至该上限之下，经训练代理验证P恒为0.33。为将P作为实时监控信号，我们引入信息数字孪生体（IDT），一种辅助架构，可从可观测的交互流中计算P及其方向性分量，无需访问模型内部。在涵盖八类扰动和两种策略架构的168次扰动试验中，基于IDT的监控检测到89.3%的耦合退化，而基于奖励的监控仅检测到44.0%，且中位延迟低4.4倍。这些结果确立了双可预测性作为部署强化学习系统闭环自调节的原理性、有界且可计算的先决信号。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the need for proactive monitoring in deployed reinforcement learning systems, where performance relies on coherent coupling between observations, actions, and outcomes. It proposes Bipredictability (P), a bounded information-theoretic measure derived from mutual information that quantifies how well agent actions reduce uncertainty about outcomes and vice versa. Theoretical analysis shows P is capped at 0.5 due to Shannon entropy, and agency inherently suppresses it to 0.33. To enable real-time monitoring without model access, the authors introduce the Information Digital Twin (IDT), an auxiliary architecture that computes P from interaction streams. Experiments across 168 perturbation trials show IDT detects 89.3% of coupling degradations versus 44.0% for reward-based methods, with 4.4x lower median latency, validating P as a principled, computable signal for closed-loop self-regulation.
该文针对部署式强化学习系统中监控手段的滞后性问题，提出Bipredictability（P）这一信息论度量，用于量化智能体动作在观察-动作-结果循环中对结果不确定性的降低程度。研究动机源于奖励机制监控无法捕捉结构退化、易导致性能崩溃的缺陷，作者将代理行为建模为不确定性消解过程，推导出P为总熵中转化为跨循环可预测性的比例。理论分析表明P上限为0.5，且实际中代理行为会使其低于该值（0.33）。为实现实时监控，作者设计了信息数字孪生体（IDT），仅通过可观测交互流计算P及其方向分量，无需访问模型内部。实验在168次扰动试验中显示，IDT检测到89.3%的耦合退化，远超奖励监控的44.0%，且中位延迟低4.4倍。
Synthetic data in cryptocurrencies using generative models
Authors: André Saimon S. Sousa, Otto Pires, Frank Acasiete, Oscar M. Granados, Valéria Loureiro da Silva, Hugo Saba
First: 2026-04-17T15:48:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:48:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data plays a fundamental role in consolidating markets, services, and products in the digital financial ecosystem. However, the use of real data, especially in the financial context, can lead to privacy risks and access restrictions, affecting institutions, research, and modeling processes. Although not all financial datasets present such limitations, this work proposes the use of deep learning techniques for generating synthetic data applied to cryptocurrency price time series. The approach is based on Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks (CGANs), combining an LSTM-type recurrent generator and an MLP discriminator to produce statistically consistent synthetic data. The experiments consider different crypto-assets and demonstrate that the model is capable of reproducing relevant temporal patterns, preserving market trends and dynamics. The generation of synthetic series through GANs is an efficient alternative for simulating financial data, showing potential for applications such as market behavior analysis and anomaly detection, with lower computational cost compared to more complex generative approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用生成模型的加密货币合成数据
数据在数字金融生态系统中对巩固市场、服务和产品起着基础性作用。然而，使用真实数据——尤其是在金融领域——可能带来隐私风险和访问限制，影响机构、研究和建模过程。尽管并非所有金融数据集都存在此类限制，本文提出利用深度学习技术生成加密货币价格时间序列的合成数据。该方法基于条件生成对抗网络（CGAN），结合LSTM型循环生成器和MLP判别器，以生成统计上一致的合成数据。实验考虑了不同加密资产，证明该模型能够再现相关的时间模式，保持市场趋势和动态。通过GAN生成合成序列是模拟金融数据的一种高效替代方案，适用于市场行为分析和异常检测等应用，且相比更复杂的生成方法具有更低的计算成本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by privacy concerns and access restrictions associated with real cryptocurrency price data, this study proposes using Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks (CGANs) to generate synthetic time series. The method combines an LSTM-based generator and an MLP discriminator to produce statistically consistent synthetic data that mirrors real market dynamics. Experimental results across multiple crypto-assets show the model successfully reproduces temporal patterns and market trends, offering a computationally efficient alternative to complex generative models for applications like anomaly detection and market behavior analysis.
鉴于真实加密货币价格数据存在隐私和访问限制，本研究提出采用条件生成对抗网络（CGAN），结合LSTM生成器与MLP判别器，生成能模拟真实市场行为的合成时序数据。实验在多种加密资产上验证，模型能有效再现时间序列模式、市场趋势与动态，具备统计一致性。该方法相比复杂生成模型计算成本更低，适用于市场分析与异常检测等场景，兼具实用性和效率。
Token Statistics Reveal Conversational Drift in Multi-turn LLM Interaction
Authors: Wael Hafez, Amir Nazeri
First: 2026-03-18T18:10:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:43:33+00:00
Comments: 13 Pages, 3 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models, LLMs, are increasingly deployed in multiturn settings where earlier responses shape later ones, making reliability dependent on whether a conversation remains consistent over time. When this consistency degrades undetected, downstream decisions lose their grounding in the exchange that produced them. Yet current evaluation methods assess isolated outputs rather than the interaction producing them. Here we show that conversational structural consistency can be monitored directly from token frequency statistics, without embeddings, auxiliary evaluators or access to model internals. We formalize this signal as Bipredictability, P, which measures shared predictability across the context, response, next prompt loop relative to the turn total uncertainty, and implement it in a lightweight auxiliary architecture, the Information Digital Twin, IDT. Across 4,574 conversational turns spanning 34 conditions, one student model and three frontier teacher models, P established a stable runtime baseline, aligned with structural consistency in 85 percent of conditions but with semantic quality in only 44 percent, and the IDT detected all tested contradictions, topic shifts and non-sequiturs with 100 percent sensitivity. These results show that reliability in extended LLM interaction cannot be reduced to response quality alone, and that structural monitoring from the observable token stream can complement semantic evaluation in deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：令牌统计揭示多轮LLM交互中的对话漂移
大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地部署在多轮对话场景中，其中早期回复会影响后续回复，其可靠性取决于对话随时间保持一致性。当这种一致性在未被检测到的情况下退化时，下游决策将失去其与产生它们的对话的关联。然而，当前的评估方法评估的是孤立的输出，而非产生这些输出的交互过程。本文表明，对话结构一致性可以直接从令牌频率统计中监控，无需嵌入、辅助评估器或对模型内部的访问。我们将这一信号形式化为“双向可预测性”P，衡量上下文、回复及下一轮提示相对于回合总不确定性的共享可预测性，并在轻量级辅助架构“信息数字孪生体”（IDT）中实现。在涵盖34种条件、一个学生模型和三个前沿教师模型的4,574个对话回合中，P在85%的条件下与结构一致性保持稳定基线，但在仅44%的条件下与语义质量一致，而IDT以100%的灵敏度检测到所有测试的矛盾、话题转移和无关联内容。这些结果表明，扩展LLM交互的可靠性不能仅归结为回复质量，且从可观测令牌流中进行结构监控可补充部署中的语义评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect conversational drift in multi-turn LLM interactions where consistency affects reliability, the authors propose Bipredictability (P), a token-level metric measuring shared predictability across context, response, and next prompt relative to turn uncertainty. Implemented via a lightweight auxiliary architecture called the Information Digital Twin (IDT), P monitors structural consistency without requiring embeddings or model internals. Across 4,574 conversational turns from one student and three frontier models, P aligned with structural consistency in 85% of conditions but only 44% with semantic quality, while IDT achieved 100% sensitivity in detecting contradictions, topic shifts, and non-sequiturs.
该研究旨在解决多轮LLM对话中结构一致性缺失的问题，因为对话可靠性依赖于跨轮次的结构连续性而非单轮响应质量。作者提出Bipredictability（P）指标，通过词元频率统计量化上下文、响应与下一轮提示间的共享可预测性相对于轮次总不确定性，无需模型内部结构或嵌入即可监控对话结构一致性。该指标通过轻量级辅助架构Information Digital Twin（IDT）实现，在涵盖1个学生模型和3个前沿模型、34种条件下的4574轮对话中进行评估。结果显示，P在85%条件下与结构一致性高度一致，但仅在44%条件下与语义质量一致，且IDT对所有测试的矛盾、话题偏移和无关联内容均实现100%敏感度检测，表明结构监控可有效补充语义评估，提升部署可靠性。
Winner of CVPR2026 NTIRE Challenge on Image Shadow Removal: Semantic and Geometric Guidance for Shadow Removal via Cascaded Refinement
Authors: Lorenzo Beltrame, Jules Salzinger, Filip Svoboda, Jasmin Lampert, Phillipp Fanta-Jende, Radu Timofte, Marco Koerner
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-17T15:43:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:43:04+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables, accepted at the CVPR 2026 Workshops (NTIRE 2026 Image Shadow Removal Challenge). Code and materials are available at https://github.com/AIT-Assistive-Autonomous-Systems/SGCR-SR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present a three-stage progressive shadow-removal pipeline for the CVPR2026 NTIRE WSRD+ challenge. Built on OmniSR, our method treats deshadowing as iterative direct refinement, where later stages correct residual artefacts left by earlier predictions. The model combines RGB appearance with frozen DINOv2 semantic guidance and geometric cues from monocular depth and surface normals, reused across all stages. To stabilise multi-stage optimisation, we introduce a contraction-constrained objective that encourages non-increasing reconstruction error across the cascade. A staged training pipeline transfers from earlier WSRD pretraining to WSRD+ supervision and final WSRD+ 2026 adaptation with cosine-annealed checkpoint ensembling. On the official WSRD+ 2026 hidden test set, our final ensemble achieved 26.680 PSNR, 0.8740 SSIM, 0.0578 LPIPS, and 26.135 FID, ranked first overall, and won the NTIRE 2026 Image Shadow Removal Challenge. The strong performance of the proposed model is further validated on the ISTD+ and UAV-SC+ datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CVPR2026 NTIRE图像阴影去除挑战赛冠军：基于级联细化的语义与几何引导阴影去除
我们为CVPR2026 NTIRE WSRD+挑战赛提出了一种三阶段渐进式阴影去除管线。基于OmniSR框架，该方法将去阴影视为迭代直接细化过程，后阶段修正前阶段预测遗留的残余伪影。模型结合RGB外观、冻结的DINOv2语义引导及单目深度与表面法线提供的几何线索，并在各阶段复用。为稳定多阶段优化，我们引入了收缩约束目标函数，鼓励级联过程中重建误差非递增。分阶段训练流程从早期WSRD预训练过渡到WSRD+监督，最终通过余弦退火检查点集成完成WSRD+ 2026适配。在官方WSRD+ 2026隐藏测试集上，最终集成模型取得26.680 PSNR、0.8740 SSIM、0.0578 LPIPS和26.135 FID，综合排名第一，赢得NTIRE 2026图像阴影去除挑战赛。所提模型在ISTD+和UAV-SC+数据集上的优异表现进一步验证了其性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity shadow removal in real-world images, this work proposes a three-stage cascaded refinement pipeline that iteratively refines shadow-removed outputs using semantic and geometric guidance. The method leverages RGB appearance, frozen DINOv2 semantics, and monocular depth/surface normals across all stages, while stabilizing multi-stage training via a contraction-constrained objective that ensures non-increasing error. Trained progressively from WSRD to WSRD+ and adapted with cosine-annealed ensembling, the model achieved top performance on the WSRD+ 2026 hidden test set with 26.680 PSNR, 0.8740 SSIM, 0.0578 LPIPS, and 26.135 FID, securing first place in the NTIRE 2026 challenge and strong results on ISTD+ and UAV-SC+.
为有效去除图像阴影并保持结构与语义保真度，作者提出基于OmniSR的三阶段级联精修框架，将去影视为迭代精修过程。每阶段融合RGB外观、冻结的DINOv2语义引导及单目深度与表面法线几何线索，并在各阶段复用。为稳定多阶段优化，引入收缩约束目标以确保重构误差非递增。模型采用渐进式训练，从WSRD预训练过渡至WSRD+监督，最终通过余弦退火检查点集成适配2026年挑战。在官方WSRD+ 2026隐藏测试集上，最终集成模型取得26.680 PSNR、0.8740 SSIM、0.0578 LPIPS和26.135 FID，排名第一并赢得NTIRE 2026图像去影挑战赛。性能亦在ISTD+与UAV-SC+数据集上得到验证。
ATTNPO: Attention-Guided Process Supervision for Efficient Reasoning
Authors: Shuaiyi Nie, Siyu Ding, Wenyuan Zhang, Linhao Yu, Tianmeng Yang, Yao Chen, Weichong Yin, Yu Sun, Hua Wu, Tingwen Liu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-02-10T16:40:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:42:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large reasoning models trained with reinforcement learning and verifiable rewards (RLVR) achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, yet often overthink, generating redundant reasoning without performance gains. Existing trajectory-level length penalties often fail to effectively shorten reasoning length and degrade accuracy, as they uniformly treat all reasoning steps and lack fine-grained signals to distinguish redundancy from necessity. Meanwhile, process-supervised methods are typically resource-intensive and suffer from inaccurate credit assignment. To address these issues, we propose ATTNPO, a low-overhead process-supervised RL framework that leverages the model's intrinsic attention signals for step-level credit assignment. We first identify a set of special attention heads that naturally focus on essential steps while suppressing redundant ones. By leveraging the attention scores of these heads, We then employ two sub-strategies to mitigate overthinking by discouraging redundant steps while preserving accuracy by reducing penalties on essential steps. Experimental results show that ATTNPO substantially reduces reasoning length while significantly improving performance across 9 benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ATTNPO：基于注意力引导的过程监督高效推理框架
通过强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）训练的大规模推理模型在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但常过度思考，生成冗余推理步骤而无性能增益。现有轨迹级长度惩罚机制常无法有效缩短推理长度且降低准确性，因其对所有推理步骤一视同仁，缺乏细粒度信号区分冗余与必要步骤。同时，过程监督方法通常资源消耗大且存在信用分配不准确问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出ATTNPO，一种低开销的过程监督强化学习框架，利用模型内在注意力信号进行步骤级信用分配。我们首先识别一组自然聚焦于必要步骤并抑制冗余步骤的特殊注意力头，再通过其注意力分数，采用两种子策略抑制冗余步骤、同时减少对必要步骤的惩罚以保持准确性。实验表明，ATTNPO显著缩短推理长度，并在9个基准测试中显著提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue of overthinking in large reasoning models trained with RLVR, which generates redundant steps without performance gains, ATTNPO introduces a low-overhead process-supervised framework that uses the model's attention signals for fine-grained step-level credit assignment. It identifies attention heads that naturally highlight essential reasoning steps and suppresses redundant ones, then applies two sub-strategies: penalizing redundant steps while reducing penalties on critical steps to preserve accuracy. Experiments across nine benchmarks demonstrate that ATTNPO significantly shortens reasoning length while improving performance substantially.
为了解决大模型在复杂推理任务中过度思考、产生冗余步骤却无性能提升的问题，ATTNPO提出一种低开销的过程监督强化学习框架，利用模型自身的注意力信号进行细粒度步骤级信用分配。该方法识别出天然聚焦关键步骤并抑制冗余步骤的注意力头，再通过两个子策略：惩罚冗余步骤、降低关键步骤惩罚以保持精度。实验表明，ATTNPO在九个基准测试中显著缩短推理长度并大幅提升性能。
MARCH: Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy for CT Report Generation
Authors: Yi Lin, Yihao Ding, Yonghui Wu, Yifan Peng
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-17T15:42:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:42:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated 3D radiology report generation often suffers from clinical hallucinations and a lack of the iterative verification found in human practice. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced the field, they typically operate as monolithic "black-box" systems without the collaborative oversight characteristic of clinical workflows. To address these challenges, we propose MARCH (Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy), a multi-agent framework that emulates the professional hierarchy of radiology departments and assigns specialized roles to distinct agents. MARCH utilizes a Resident Agent for initial drafting with multi-scale CT feature extraction, multiple Fellow Agents for retrieval-augmented revision, and an Attending Agent that orchestrates an iterative, stance-based consensus discourse to resolve diagnostic discrepancies. On the RadGenome-ChestCT dataset, MARCH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both clinical fidelity and linguistic accuracy. Our work demonstrates that modeling human-like organizational structures enhances the reliability of AI in high-stakes medical domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MARCH：用于CT报告生成的多智能体放射科临床层级框架
自动化的三维放射学报告生成常因临床幻觉和缺乏人类实践中常见的迭代验证而受限。尽管近期视觉-语言模型（VLMs）推动了该领域发展，但它们通常作为“黑箱”式单体系统运行，缺乏临床工作流中特有的协作监督机制。为应对这些挑战，我们提出MARCH（多智能体放射科临床层级），一种模拟放射科部门专业层级结构的多智能体框架，为不同智能体分配专门角色。MARCH采用住院医师智能体进行初步草拟并结合多尺度CT特征提取，多位主治医师智能体进行检索增强修订，以及主任医师智能体协调基于立场的迭代共识讨论以解决诊断分歧。在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上，MARCH在临床准确性和语言准确性方面均显著优于现有最先进基线模型。我们的工作表明，模拟人类组织结构可提升AI在高风险医疗领域的可靠性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of monolithic Vision-Language Models in radiology report generation—such as clinical hallucinations and absence of human-style iterative verification—the authors propose MARCH, a multi-agent system that mimics radiology department hierarchies. It employs a Resident Agent for initial drafting using multi-scale CT features, multiple Fellow Agents for retrieval-augmented revision, and an Attending Agent to coordinate iterative consensus discussions to resolve diagnostic conflicts. Experiments on RadGenome-ChestCT show MARCH significantly improves both clinical fidelity and linguistic accuracy over prior state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating that incorporating human-like organizational roles enhances AI reliability in medical contexts.
为解决自动CT报告生成中的临床幻觉和缺乏人类协作验证的问题，MARCH提出一个模仿放射科部门层级结构的多智能体框架。该框架由负责初步草稿的住院医师智能体、进行检索增强修订的主治医师智能体，以及协调迭代共识讨论以解决诊断分歧的主任医师智能体组成。在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上的实验表明，MARCH在临床准确性和语言准确性上显著优于现有方法，证明模拟人类组织结构可提升AI在高风险医疗场景中的可靠性。
JumpLoRA: Sparse Adapters for Continual Learning in Large Language Models
Authors: Alexandra Dragomir, Ioana Pintilie, Antonio Barbalau, Marius Dragoi, Florin Brad, Cristian Daniel Paduraru, Alexandru Tifrea, Elena Burceanu, Radu Tudor Ionescu
First: 2026-04-17T15:38:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:38:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapter-based methods have become a cost-effective approach to continual learning (CL) for Large Language Models (LLMs), by sequentially learning a low-rank update matrix for each task. To mitigate catastrophic forgetting, state-of-the-art approaches impose constraints on new adapters with respect to the previous ones, by targeting either subspace or coordinate-wise interference. In this paper, we propose JumpLoRA, a novel framework to adaptively induce sparsity in the Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) blocks through the use of JumpReLU gating. The method achieves dynamic parameter isolation, which helps prevent task interference. We demonstrate that our method is highly modular and compatible with LoRA-based CL approaches. Specifically, it significantly boosts the performance of IncLoRA and outperforms the leading state-of-the-art CL method, ELLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：JumpLoRA：用于大语言模型持续学习的稀疏适配器
基于适配器的方法已成为大语言模型持续学习（CL）的一种成本效益高的方法，通过为每个任务顺序学习一个低秩更新矩阵。为缓解灾难性遗忘，现有最先进方法通过子空间或坐标级干扰约束新适配器与旧适配器的关系。本文提出JumpLoRA，一种通过JumpReLU门控机制自适应诱导低秩适配（LoRA）模块稀疏性的新框架。该方法实现动态参数隔离，有助于防止任务干扰。我们证明该方法高度模块化，兼容基于LoRA的持续学习方法。具体而言，它显著提升IncLoRA性能，并优于当前最先进的持续学习方法ELLA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce catastrophic forgetting in continual learning for large language models, this paper introduces JumpLoRA, a method that induces sparsity in LoRA adapter blocks via JumpReLU gating to dynamically isolate parameters across tasks. By enforcing sparse updates, JumpLoRA minimizes interference between learned tasks while maintaining compatibility with existing LoRA-based continual learning frameworks. Experimental results show that JumpLoRA significantly enhances performance on IncLoRA and surpasses the state-of-the-art method ELLA in continual learning benchmarks.
为缓解大语言模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出JumpLoRA方法，通过JumpReLU门控机制在LoRA适配器块中动态诱导稀疏性，实现跨任务参数隔离，从而减少任务间干扰。该方法兼容现有LoRA持续学习框架，实验表明其显著提升IncLoRA性能，并在多个指标上超越当前最优方法ELLA。
neuralCAD-Edit: An Expert Benchmark for Multimodal-Instructed 3D CAD Model Editing
Authors: Toby Perrett, Matthew Bouchard, William McCarthy
First: 2026-04-17T15:38:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:38:33+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://autodeskailab.github.io/neuralCAD-Edit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce neuralCAD-Edit, the first benchmark for editing 3D CAD models collected from expert CAD engineers. Instead of text conditioning as in prior works, we collect realistic CAD editing requests by capturing videos of professional designers, interacting directly with CAD models in CAD software, while talking, pointing and drawing. We recruited ten consenting designers to contribute to this contained study. We benchmark leading foundation models against human CAD experts carrying out edits, and find a large performance gap in both automatic metrics and human evaluations. Even the best foundation model (GPT 5.2) scores 53% lower (absolute) than CAD experts in human acceptance trials, demonstrating the challenge of neuralCAD-Edit. We hope neuralCAD-Edit will provide a solid foundation against which 3D CAD editing approaches and foundation models can be developed. Code/data: https://autodeskailab.github.io/neuralCAD-Edit

Automatic Combination of Sample Selection Strategies for Few-Shot Learning
Authors: Branislav Pecher, Ivan Srba, Maria Bielikova, Joaquin Vanschoren
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2024-02-05T14:23:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:35:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Findings of ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In few-shot learning, the selection of samples has a significant impact on the performance of the model. While effective sample selection strategies are well-established in supervised settings, research on large language models largely overlooks them, favouring strategies specifically tailored to individual in-context learning settings. In this paper, we propose a new method for Automatic Combination of SamplE Selection Strategies (ACSESS) to leverage the strengths and complementarity of various well-established selection objectives. We investigate and compare the impact of 23 sample selection strategies on the performance of 5 in-context learning models and 3 few-shot learning approaches (meta-learning, few-shot fine-tuning) over 6 text and 8 image datasets. The experimental results show that the combination of strategies through the ACSESS method consistently outperforms all individual selection strategies and performs on par or exceeds the in-context learning specific baselines. Lastly, we demonstrate that sample selection remains effective even on smaller datasets, yielding the greatest benefits when only a few shots are selected, while its advantage diminishes as the number of shots increases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：少样本学习中样本选择策略的自动组合
在少样本学习中，样本选择对模型性能有显著影响。尽管监督学习中有效的样本选择策略已较为成熟，但大语言模型领域的研究大多忽略了这一点，更倾向于针对特定上下文学习场景设计策略。本文提出了一种自动组合样本选择策略（ACSESS）的新方法，以充分利用多种成熟选择目标的优势与互补性。我们研究并比较了23种样本选择策略对5种上下文学习模型和3种少样本学习方法（元学习、少样本微调）在6个文本和8个图像数据集上的性能影响。实验结果表明，通过ACSESS方法组合策略始终优于所有单一策略，并在性能上与上下文学习特定基线持平或超越。最后，我们证明样本选择在小规模数据集上仍有效，尤其在仅选择少量样本时收益最大，随着样本数量增加，其优势逐渐减弱。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of sample selection strategies in large language model few-shot learning, this paper introduces ACSESS, a method that automatically combines multiple sample selection objectives to enhance performance. The study evaluates 23 selection strategies across 5 in-context learning models and 3 few-shot learning approaches on 6 text and 8 image datasets. Results show that ACSESS consistently outperforms individual strategies and matches or surpasses in-context learning-specific baselines, with the greatest gains observed when selecting only a few shots, especially on smaller datasets.
鉴于大语言模型在少样本学习中常忽略样本选择策略，本文提出ACSESS方法，自动融合多种样本选择目标以提升性能。实验在5个上下文学习模型、3种少样本学习范式及14个数据集（6个文本、8个图像）上评估了23种选择策略，结果表明ACSESS始终优于单个策略，且表现与任务特定基线相当或更优。尤其在样本量极少时收益最大，样本量增加后优势逐渐减弱。
KRONE: Scalable LLM-Augmented Log Anomaly Detection via Hierarchical Abstraction
Authors: Lei Ma, Jinyang Liu, Tieying Zhang, Peter M. VanNostrand, Dennis M. Hofmann, Lei Cao, Elke A. Rundensteiner, Jianjun Chen
First: 2026-02-07T01:30:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:29:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICDE 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Log anomaly detection is crucial for uncovering system failures and security risks. Although logs originate from nested component executions with clear boundaries, this structure is lost when stored as flat sequences. As a result, state-of-the-art methods often miss true dependencies within executions while learning spurious correlations across unrelated events. We propose KRONE, the first hierarchical anomaly detection framework that automatically derives execution hierarchies from flat logs to enable modular, multi-level anomaly detection. At its core, the KRONE Log Abstraction Model extracts application-specific semantic hierarchies, which are used to recursively decompose log sequences into coherent execution units, referred to as KRONE Seqs. This transforms sequence-level detection into a set of modular KRONE Seq-level detection tasks. For each test KRONE Seq, KRONE adopts a hybrid modular detection strategy that routes between an efficient level-independent Local-Context detector for rapid filtering and a Nested-Aware detector that captures cross-level semantic dependencies, augmented with LLM-based anomaly detection and explanation. KRONE further optimizes detection through cached result reuse and early-exit strategies along the hierarchy. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset from ByteDance Cloud demonstrate that KRONE achieves substantial improvements in accuracy (42.49% to 87.98%), F1 score, data efficiency (117.3x reduction), resource efficiency (43.7x reduction), and interpretability. KRONE improves F1-score by 10.07% (82.76% to 92.83%) over prior methods while reducing LLM usage to only 1.1% to 3.3% of the test data. Code: https://github.com/LeiMa0324/KRONE Demo: https://leima0324.github.io/KRONE_Demo_official/
中文标题/摘要
标题：KRONE：通过分层抽象实现可扩展的LLM增强日志异常检测
日志异常检测对于发现系统故障和安全风险至关重要。尽管日志源自具有明确边界的嵌套组件执行，但存储为扁平序列后该结构丢失。因此，现有方法常忽略执行内部的真实依赖关系，而学习到无关事件间的虚假关联。我们提出KRONE，首个通过从扁平日志自动推导执行层次结构，实现模块化、多层级异常检测的分层异常检测框架。其核心是KRONE日志抽象模型，提取应用特定语义层次结构，递归分解日志序列成连贯的执行单元（称为KRONE Seq）。这将序列级检测转化为多个模块化的KRONE Seq级检测任务。对每个测试KRONE Seq，KRONE采用混合模块化检测策略：在高效、与层级无关的局部上下文检测器（用于快速过滤）与捕捉跨层级语义依赖的嵌套感知检测器之间路由，辅以基于LLM的异常检测与解释。KRONE进一步通过缓存结果复用和层次结构中的早期退出策略优化检测。在三个公开基准和字节跳动云工业数据集上的实验表明，KRONE在准确率（42.49%至87.98%）、F1分数、数据效率（提升117.3倍）、资源效率（提升43.7倍）和可解释性方面均有显著提升。相比现有方法，KRONE F1分数提升10.07%（82.76%至92.83%），同时将LLM使用量降至测试数据的1.1%至3.3%。代码：https://github.com/LeiMa0324/KRONE 演示：https://leima0324.github.io/KRONE_Demo_official/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the loss of hierarchical structure in flat log sequences that hinders accurate anomaly detection, KRONE introduces a novel framework that automatically reconstructs execution hierarchies to enable multi-level, modular detection. It employs a Log Abstraction Model to derive application-specific semantic hierarchies, decomposing logs into coherent units called KRONE Seqs, then applies a hybrid detection strategy combining a fast Local-Context detector with a Nested-Aware detector augmented by LLM-based analysis. Experiments across four datasets show KRONE significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving up to 87.98% accuracy, a 10.07% F1-score improvement, and dramatic reductions in data and resource usage—down to 1.1%–3.3% LLM usage on test data—while enhancing interpretability.
为解决扁平化日志序列丢失执行层级结构导致的异常检测精度不足问题，KRONE提出首个自动构建执行层级的分层异常检测框架。其核心是日志抽象模型，能提取应用特定语义层级，将日志递归分解为语义连贯的KRONE Seq单元，再通过混合检测策略——结合快速本地上下文检测器与捕捉跨层级语义依赖的嵌套感知检测器，并辅以LLM增强的异常评分与解释——实现模块化检测。实验表明，KRONE在四个数据集上显著优于现有方法，准确率最高达87.98%，F1分数提升10.07%（至92.83%），数据效率提升117.3倍，资源消耗降低43.7倍，且LLM仅需消耗测试数据的1.1%至3.3%。
AtManRL: Towards Faithful Reasoning via Differentiable Attention Saliency
Authors: Max Henning Höth, Kristian Kersting, Björn Deiseroth, Letitia Parcalabescu
First: 2026-04-17T15:27:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:27:35+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 8 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks. Yet ensuring that the reasoning trace both contributes to and faithfully reflects the processes underlying the model's final answer, rather than merely accompanying it, remains challenging. We introduce AtManRL, a method that leverages differentiable attention manipulation to learn more faithful reasoning through reinforcement learning. By training an additive attention mask that identifies tokens in the CoT crucial for producing correct answers, we derive a saliency reward signal that encourages the model to generate reasoning traces that genuinely influence its final predictions. We integrate this saliency reward with outcome-based rewards within the GRPO framework to jointly optimize for correctness and interpretability. Experiments on GSM8K and MMLU with Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct demonstrate that our approach can identify influential reasoning tokens and enable training more transparent reasoning models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AtManRL：通过可微注意力显著性实现忠实推理
大型语言模型（LLMs）日益依赖思维链（CoT）推理来解决复杂任务。然而，确保推理轨迹不仅伴随最终答案，更能真实反映模型得出该答案的内在过程，仍具挑战性。我们提出AtManRL方法，通过可微注意力操控结合强化学习，学习更忠实的推理。通过训练一个加性注意力掩码，识别CoT中对生成正确答案至关重要的词元，我们推导出显著性奖励信号，鼓励模型生成真正影响最终预测的推理轨迹。我们将该显著性奖励与基于结果的奖励整合进GRPO框架，联合优化正确性和可解释性。在GSM8K和MMLU数据集上使用Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct进行的实验表明，我们的方法能识别关键推理词元，并支持训练更透明的推理模型。

Motivated by the need to ensure that chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models genuinely influences final outputs rather than merely being superficially correlated, the authors propose AtManRL, a reinforcement learning method that uses differentiable attention manipulation. It trains an additive attention mask to identify critical tokens in reasoning traces, generating a saliency reward that guides the model to produce reasoning that actually affects its predictions. 为确保大语言模型的思维链推理真正影响最终输出而非仅表面相关，作者提出AtManRL方法，利用可微注意力操控进行强化学习。该方法训练一个加性注意力掩码，识别推理中关键的词元，并生成显著性奖励信号以鼓励其对预测产生实质影响。将显著性奖励与结果奖励结合，在GRPO框架内联合优化正确性和可解释性。在GSM8K和MMLU数据集上使用Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct的实验表明，AtManRL能有效识别关键推理词元，提升推理过程的透明度与忠实性。
SWNet: A Cross-Spectral Network for Camouflaged Weed Detection
Authors: Henry O. Velesaca, Luigi Miranda, Angel D. Sappa
First: 2026-04-17T15:20:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:20:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper presents SWNet, a bimodal end-to-end cross-spectral network specifically engineered for the detection of camouflaged weeds in dense agricultural environments. Plant camouflage, characterized by homochromatic blending where invasive species mimic the phenotypic traits of primary crops, poses a significant challenge for traditional computer vision systems. To overcome these limitations, SWNet utilizes a Pyramid Vision Transformer v2 backbone to capture long-range dependencies and a Bimodal Gated Fusion Module to dynamically integrate Visible and Near-Infrared information. By leveraging the physiological differences in chlorophyll reflectance captured in the NIR spectrum, the proposed architecture effectively discriminates targets that are otherwise indistinguishable in the visible range. Furthermore, an Edge-Aware Refinement module is employed to produce sharper object boundaries and reduce structural ambiguity. Experimental results on the Weeds-Banana dataset indicate that SWNet outperforms ten state-of-the-art methods. The study demonstrates that the integration of cross-spectral data and boundary-guided refinement is essential for high segmentation accuracy in complex crop canopies. The code is available on GitHub: https://cod-espol.github.io/SWNet/
中文标题/摘要
标题：SWNet：一种用于隐蔽杂草检测的跨光谱网络
本文提出SWNet，一种专为密集农业环境中隐蔽杂草检测设计的双模态端到端跨光谱网络。植物伪装表现为同色系融合，即入侵物种模仿主要作物的表型特征，这对传统计算机视觉系统构成重大挑战。为克服这一局限，SWNet采用Pyramid Vision Transformer v2骨干网络捕捉长距离依赖关系，并结合双模态门控融合模块动态整合可见光与近红外信息。通过利用近红外光谱中叶绿素反射的生理差异，该架构有效区分在可见光下难以分辨的目标。此外，引入边缘感知细化模块以生成更清晰的物体边界并减少结构模糊。在Weeds-Banana数据集上的实验表明，SWNet优于十种现有最先进方法。研究表明，跨光谱数据融合与边界引导细化对复杂作物冠层的高精度分割至关重要。代码已发布于GitHub：https://cod-espol.github.io/SWNet/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of detecting camouflaged weeds that blend visually with crops, this paper introduces SWNet, a bimodal end-to-end cross-spectral network. It employs a Pyramid Vision Transformer v2 backbone to capture long-range dependencies and a Bimodal Gated Fusion Module to dynamically combine visible and near-infrared data, leveraging chlorophyll reflectance differences for discrimination. An Edge-Aware Refinement module sharpens object boundaries and reduces structural ambiguity. Experiments on the Weeds-Banana dataset show SWNet outperforms ten state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating that cross-spectral integration and boundary refinement are critical for accurate segmentation in dense crop environments.
为解决农作物中伪装杂草难以识别的问题，本文提出SWNet，一种端到端的跨光谱网络。该网络采用Pyramid Vision Transformer v2骨干网络捕捉长距离依赖关系，并通过双模态门控融合模块动态融合可见光与近红外数据，利用叶绿素反射差异实现有效区分。边缘感知细化模块进一步优化目标边界，减少结构模糊。在Weeds-Banana数据集上的实验表明，SWNet优于十种现有最先进方法，证明跨光谱融合与边界引导细化对复杂作物冠层中的高精度分割至关重要。
On the Rejection Criterion for Proxy-based Test-time Alignment
Authors: Ayoub Hammal, Pierre Zweigenbaum, Caio Corro
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-17T15:20:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:20:13+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent works proposed test-time alignment methods that rely on a small aligned model as a proxy that guides the generation of a larger base (unaligned) model. The implicit reward approach skews the large model distribution, whereas the nudging approach defers the generation of the next token to the small aligned model when the large base one is unconfident about its outcome. In this work, we first show that both approaches can be reduced to sampling from similar graphical models, where they differ only in the definition of a rejection criterion (or distribution). Moreover, we argue that the confidence criterion is ill-motivated due to linguistic phenomena like ambiguous phrasing. We propose a novel rejection criterion based on a conservative confidence bet. Experimentally, our novel approach outperforms previous work on several datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于基于代理的测试时对齐方法的拒绝标准
近期工作提出了依赖小型对齐模型作为代理、引导大型基础（未对齐）模型生成的测试时对齐方法。隐式奖励方法会扭曲大型模型的分布，而推挤方法则在大型基础模型对结果不确定时，将下一个词的生成推迟给小型对齐模型。本文首先证明，这两种方法均可归约为从相似图模型中采样，其区别仅在于拒绝标准（或分布）的定义。我们进一步指出，基于置信度的拒绝标准因语言现象（如歧义表达）而缺乏合理性。我们提出了一种基于保守置信赌注的新型拒绝标准。实验表明，我们的新方法在多个数据集上优于先前工作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing test-time alignment methods that rely on proxy models, this work demonstrates that both implicit reward and nudging approaches can be unified under a common graphical model framework, differing only in their rejection criteria. The authors argue that confidence-based rejection is flawed due to linguistic ambiguity and propose a conservative confidence bet as a more robust alternative. Experiments show this novel criterion consistently outperforms prior methods across multiple datasets.
鉴于现有基于代理模型的测试时对齐方法存在缺陷，本文证明隐式奖励与推导方法可统一于相似的图模型框架，仅在拒绝准则上存在差异。作者指出基于置信度的拒绝标准因语言歧义而不合理，提出保守置信赌注作为更稳健的替代方案。实验表明，该新准则在多个数据集上均优于先前方法。
CobwebTM: Probabilistic Concept Formation for Lifelong and Hierarchical Topic Modeling
Authors: Karthik Singaravadivelan, Anant Gupta, Zekun Wang, Christopher J. MacLellan
First: 2026-04-15T23:58:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:19:39+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 8 figures, 11 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Topic modeling seeks to uncover latent semantic structure in text corpora with minimal supervision. Neural approaches achieve strong performance but require extensive tuning and struggle with lifelong learning due to catastrophic forgetting and fixed capacity, while classical probabilistic models lack flexibility and adaptability to streaming data. We introduce CobwebTM, a low-parameter lifelong hierarchical topic model based on incremental probabilistic concept formation. By adapting the Cobweb algorithm to continuous document embeddings, CobwebTM constructs semantic hierarchies online, enabling unsupervised topic discovery, dynamic topic creation, and hierarchical organization without predefining the number of topics. Across diverse datasets, CobwebTM achieves strong topic coherence, stable topics over time, and high-quality hierarchies, demonstrating that incremental symbolic concept formation combined with pretrained representations is an efficient approach to topic modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CobwebTM：面向终身与分层主题建模的概率概念形成
主题建模旨在以最少监督发现文本语料库中的潜在语义结构。神经方法表现优异但需大量调参，且因灾难性遗忘和固定容量难以实现终身学习；经典概率模型则缺乏灵活性，难以适应流式数据。我们提出CobwebTM，一种基于增量概率概念形成的低参数终身分层主题模型。通过将Cobweb算法适配至连续文档嵌入，CobwebTM可在线构建语义层次结构，实现无监督主题发现、动态主题生成与分层组织，无需预先设定主题数量。在多个数据集上，CobwebTM展现出高主题连贯性、时间稳定性和高质量层次结构，证明了增量符号概念形成结合预训练表示是高效的主题建模方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of neural topic models in lifelong learning and classical models in adaptability, CobwebTM introduces a low-parameter, incremental probabilistic approach based on the Cobweb algorithm adapted to continuous document embeddings. It enables unsupervised, online topic discovery and hierarchical organization without predefining topic counts, dynamically creating and refining topics as new data arrives. Experimental results across diverse datasets show it achieves high topic coherence, stable topic evolution over time, and well-structured hierarchies, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining symbolic concept formation with pretrained representations for lifelong topic modeling.
为解决神经模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和容量固定问题，以及传统模型缺乏适应性的问题，CobwebTM 提出一种基于增量概率概念形成、适配连续文档嵌入的低参数终身分层主题模型。该模型无需预设主题数量，支持在线构建语义层次结构，实现无监督主题发现、动态主题生成与分层组织。在多个数据集上的实验表明，该模型主题连贯性高、主题随时间稳定演化、层次结构质量优异，证明结合增量符号概念形成与预训练表示是终身主题建模的有效方法。
Training Time Prediction for Mixed Precision-based Distributed Training
Authors: Minchul Kang, Changyong Shin, Jinwoo Jeong, Hyunho Lee, Younghun Go, Gyeongmin Kim, Gyeongsik Yang, Chuck Yoo
First: 2026-04-17T15:18:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:18:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate prediction of training time in distributed deep learning is crucial for resource allocation, cost estimation, and job scheduling. We observe that the floating-point precision setting is a key determinant of training time, leading to training time variations of ~2.4x over its minimum. However, existing studies on distributed training time prediction rely on static model computation graphs that do not capture precision variations, including mixed precision. According to our experiments, training time prediction without considering precision results in significant prediction errors - reaching up to 147.85% in mean absolute percentage error (MAPE). To address this issue, we propose a precision-aware distributed training time predictor that achieves robust accuracy across diverse precision settings, including mixed precision, with 9.8% MAPE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于混合精度分布式训练的训练时间预测
分布式深度学习中训练时间的准确预测对于资源分配、成本估算和作业调度至关重要。我们观察到，浮点精度设置是训练时间的关键决定因素，其训练时间变化可达最小值的约2.4倍。然而，现有分布式训练时间预测研究依赖静态模型计算图，无法捕捉精度变化，包括混合精度。根据我们的实验，不考虑精度的训练时间预测会导致显著的预测误差——平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）高达147.85%。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种精度感知的分布式训练时间预测器，可在包括混合精度在内的多种精度设置下实现稳健的准确性，MAPE仅为9.8%。
Summary / 总结
Accurate training time prediction in distributed deep learning is vital for resource planning and scheduling, but existing methods fail to account for floating-point precision variations, which can cause up to 2.4x differences in training duration. To resolve this, the authors introduce a precision-aware predictor that models mixed-precision settings, achieving a 9.8% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), significantly outperforming static graph-based predictors that suffer up to 147.85% MAPE without precision consideration.
分布式深度学习训练时间的准确预测对资源分配至关重要，但现有方法未考虑浮点精度变化，导致训练时间波动可达2.4倍。为此，作者提出一种感知精度的预测器，能动态建模混合精度配置，在多种精度设置下实现9.8%的平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE），远优于忽略精度的基线方法（误差高达147.85%）。
Mechanisms of Prompt-Induced Hallucination in Vision-Language Models
Authors: William Rudman, Michal Golovanevsky, Dana Arad, Yonatan Belinkov, Ritambhara Singh, Carsten Eickhoff, Kyle Mahowald
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-08T18:23:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:14:39+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models (VLMs) are highly capable, yet often hallucinate by favoring textual prompts over visual evidence. We study this failure mode in a controlled object-counting setting, where the prompt overstates the number of objects in the image (e.g., asking a model to describe four waterlilies when only three are present). At low object counts, models often correct the overestimation, but as the number of objects increases, they increasingly conform to the prompt regardless of the discrepancy. Through mechanistic analysis of three VLMs, we identify a small set of attention heads whose ablation substantially reduces prompt-induced hallucinations (PIH) by at least 40% without additional training. Across models, PIH-heads mediate prompt copying in model-specific ways. We characterize these differences and show that PIH ablation increases correction toward visual evidence. Our findings offer insights into the internal mechanisms driving prompt-induced hallucinations, revealing model-specific differences in how these behaviors are implemented.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉语言模型中提示诱导幻觉的机制
大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）能力强大，但常因优先考虑文本提示而非视觉证据而产生幻觉。我们在受控的物体计数场景中研究此失效模式，其中提示夸大图像中物体数量（如要求模型描述四朵睡莲，而实际仅三朵）。在物体数量较少时，模型常能纠正夸大，但物体数量增加时，模型更倾向于服从提示，无视差异。通过对三种VLMs的机制分析，我们发现一组注意力头的移除可显著减少提示诱导幻觉（PIH）至少40%，且无需额外训练。不同模型中，PIH头以特定方式介导提示复制。我们刻画了这些差异，并表明PIH移除可增强对视觉证据的修正。我们的发现揭示了驱动提示诱导幻觉的内部机制，展现了不同模型在实现此类行为时的差异。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why large vision-language models hallucinate by prioritizing textual prompts over visual evidence, particularly in object-counting tasks where prompts overstate object numbers. Using mechanistic analysis across three models, researchers identify specific attention heads responsible for prompt-induced hallucinations (PIH), and ablating these heads reduces hallucinations by at least 40% without retraining. The findings reveal that different models implement PIH through distinct neural pathways, and removing these heads enhances the models’ reliance on visual evidence rather than prompt text.
该研究探讨了大型视觉-语言模型为何会因优先遵循文本提示而产生幻觉，特别是在物体计数任务中，当提示夸大物体数量时。通过对三个模型的机制分析，研究人员识别出一组特定的注意力头负责提示诱导幻觉（PIH），移除这些注意力头可使幻觉减少至少40%，且无需额外训练。研究发现不同模型通过不同的注意力机制实现PIH，移除这些注意力头可增强模型对视觉证据的依赖而非文本提示。
Security Threat Modeling for Emerging AI-Agent Protocols: A Comparative Analysis of MCP, A2A, Agora, and ANP
Authors: Zeynab Anbiaee, Mahdi Rabbani, Mansur Mirani, Gunjan Piya, Igor Opushnyev, Ali Ghorbani, Sajjad Dadkhah
First: 2026-02-11T19:58:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:12:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid development of the AI agent communication protocols, including the Model Context Protocol (MCP), Agent2Agent (A2A), Agora, and Agent Network Protocol (ANP), is reshaping how AI agents communicate with tools, services, and each other. While these protocols support scalable multi-agent interaction and cross-organizational interoperability, their security principles remain understudied, and standardized threat modeling is limited; no protocol-centric risk assessment framework has been established yet. This paper presents a systematic security analysis of four emerging AI agent communication protocols. First, we develop a structured threat modeling analysis that examines protocol architectures, trust assumptions, interaction patterns, and lifecycle behaviors to identify protocol-specific and cross-protocol risk surfaces. Second, we introduce a qualitative risk assessment framework that identifies twelve protocol-level risks and evaluates security posture across the creation, operation, and update phases through systematic assessment of likelihood, impact, and overall protocol risk, with implications for secure deployment and future standardization. Third, we provide a measurement-driven case study on MCP that formalizes the risk of missing mandatory validation/attestation for executable components as a falsifiable security claim by quantifying wrong-provider tool execution under multi-server composition across representative resolver policies. Collectively, our results highlight key design-induced risk surfaces and provide actionable guidance for secure deployment and future standardization of agent communication ecosystems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：新兴AI代理协议的安全威胁建模：MCP、A2A、Agora与ANP的比较分析
AI代理通信协议（包括模型上下文协议MCP、Agent2Agent（A2A）、Agora和代理网络协议ANP）的快速发展正在重塑AI代理与工具、服务及其他代理之间的通信方式。尽管这些协议支持可扩展的多代理交互和跨组织互操作性，但其安全原则尚未充分研究，标准化的威胁建模也较为匮乏，目前尚无以协议为中心的风险评估框架。本文对四种新兴AI代理通信协议进行了系统性安全分析：首先，我们构建了结构化的威胁建模分析，通过考察协议架构、信任假设、交互模式和生命周期行为，识别协议特有及跨协议的风险面；其次，我们提出了一种定性风险评估框架，识别出十二类协议级风险，并通过系统评估可能性、影响及整体协议风险，对创建、运行和更新阶段的安全态势进行评估，为安全部署和未来标准化提供启示；第三，我们针对MCP提供了基于测量的案例研究，通过量化在代表性解析器策略下多服务器组合中错误提供者工具的执行情况，将可执行组件缺失强制验证/认证的风险形式化为可证伪的安全声明。综合来看，我们的研究结果突出了关键的设计诱导风险面，并为代理通信生态系统的安全部署和未来标准化提供了可操作的指导。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to secure emerging AI-agent communication protocols, this study systematically analyzes MCP, A2A, Agora, and ANP through a structured threat modeling approach that evaluates protocol architecture, trust assumptions, interaction patterns, and lifecycle behaviors to uncover unique and shared risk surfaces. It introduces a qualitative risk assessment framework identifying twelve protocol-level risks and evaluates security posture across creation, operation, and update phases by measuring likelihood, impact, and overall risk. Experimental results on MCP demonstrate that omitting mandatory validation/attestation for executable components leads to measurable security failures—specifically, wrong-provider tool execution under multi-server composition—validating the framework’s ability to quantify and falsify security claims.
随着MCP、A2A、Agora和ANP等AI代理通信协议的快速普及，本研究针对这些新兴系统缺乏标准化安全威胁建模的问题展开分析。作者采用结构化威胁建模方法，评估协议架构、信任假设、交互模式及生命周期行为，识别出特定和共有的风险面。随后引入定性风险评估框架，识别十二类协议级风险，并基于可能性与影响评估其在创建、运行和更新阶段的安全态势。针对MCP的测量驱动案例研究量化了可执行组件缺失强制验证的风险，通过多服务器组合下模拟错误提供者工具执行，揭示设计引发的漏洞，并为安全部署与未来协议标准化提供可操作建议。
Sentiment Analysis of German Sign Language Fairy Tales
Authors: Fabrizio Nunnari, Siddhant Jain, Patrick Gebhard
First: 2026-04-17T15:10:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:10:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a dataset and a model for sentiment analysis of German sign language (DGS) fairy tales. First, we perform sentiment analysis for three levels of valence (negative, neutral, positive) on German fairy tales text segments using four large language models (LLMs) and majority voting, reaching an inter-annotator agreement of 0.781 Krippendorff's alpha. Second, we extract face and body motion features from each corresponding DGS video segment using MediaPipe. Finally, we train an explainable model (based on XGBoost) to predict negative, neutral or positive sentiment from video features. Results show an average balanced accuracy of 0.631. A thorough analysis of the most important features reveal that, in addition to eyebrows and mouth motion on the face, also the motion of hips, elbows, and shoulders considerably contribute in the discrimination of the conveyed sentiment, indicating an equal importance of face and body for sentiment communication in sign language.
中文标题/摘要
标题：德语手语童话的情感分析
我们提出了一套用于德语手语（DGS）童话情感分析的数据集和模型。首先，我们使用四个大型语言模型（LLMs）和多数投票法，对德语童话文本片段进行三级情感分析（负面、中性、正面），达到0.781的Krippendorff's alpha标注者间一致性。其次，我们使用MediaPipe从每个对应的DGS视频片段中提取面部和身体运动特征。最后，我们训练了一个可解释模型（基于XGBoost），根据视频特征预测负面、中性或正面情感，结果平均平衡准确率为0.631。深入分析最重要的特征表明，除了面部的眉毛和嘴部运动外，臀部、肘部和肩部的运动也显著有助于情感的区分，表明在手语中面部和身体运动在情感传达中具有同等重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to analyze emotional content in German Sign Language (DGS) fairy tales, this study develops a dataset and model for sentiment classification. It first applies four large language models to classify text segments into negative, neutral, or positive sentiment, achieving 0.781 Krippendorff’s alpha inter-annotator agreement via majority voting. Then, it extracts facial and body motion features from corresponding DGS video segments using MediaPipe, and trains an XGBoost model to predict sentiment from these features, yielding an average balanced accuracy of 0.631. Feature importance analysis reveals that while facial expressions (eyebrows, mouth) are significant, body motion (hips, elbows, shoulders) also plays a crucial role, suggesting face and body are equally important for conveying sentiment in sign language.
为理解德语手语童话中的情感表达，本研究构建了数据集与模型进行情感分析。首先，利用四个大语言模型对文本片段进行负、中、正三类情感分类，通过多数投票达成0.781的Krippendorff’s alpha标注者间一致性。随后，从对应手语视频中提取面部与身体运动特征，使用MediaPipe，并训练XGBoost模型预测情感，平均平衡准确率为0.631。特征重要性分析表明，面部表情（眉、嘴）固然重要，但身体动作（髋、肘、肩）同样关键，说明手语情感表达中面部与身体具有同等重要性。
Noise Aggregation Analysis Driven by Small-Noise Injection: Efficient Membership Inference for Diffusion Models
Authors: Guo Li, Weihong Chen, Yongfu Fan
First: 2025-10-18T16:28:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:10:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have demonstrated powerful performance in generating high-quality images. A typical example is text-to-image generator like Stable Diffusion. However, their widespread use also poses potential privacy risks. A key concern is membership inference attacks, which attempt to determine whether a particular data sample was used in the model training process. Existing membership inference attacks against diffusion models either directly exploit sample loss differences or rely on image-level reconstruction differences. Both approaches commonly ignore the consistency characteristics of noise prediction during the diffusion process, resulting in either low inference accuracy or high computational costs. To address these shortcomings, we propose a membership inference method based on noise aggregation analysis, and introduce a single-step, low-intensity noise injection diffusion strategy to amplify differences between member and non-member samples. Our proposed approach substantially reduces model query requirements while delivering more efficient and accurate membership inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于小噪声注入驱动的噪声聚合分析：扩散模型高效成员推断
扩散模型在生成高质量图像方面表现出强大性能，典型例子是Stable Diffusion等文本到图像生成器。然而，其广泛应用也带来潜在隐私风险，其中关键问题是成员推断攻击，旨在判断特定数据样本是否用于模型训练。现有针对扩散模型的成员推断攻击要么直接利用样本损失差异，要么依赖图像级重建差异，两者均常忽略扩散过程中噪声预测的一致性特征，导致推断准确率低或计算成本高。为解决上述不足，我们提出基于噪声聚合分析的成员推断方法，并引入单步低强度噪声注入扩散策略，以放大成员与非成员样本间的差异。该方法显著降低模型查询需求，同时实现更高效准确的成员推断。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the privacy risks associated with diffusion models like Stable Diffusion, this work addresses the inefficiency and inaccuracy of existing membership inference attacks that rely on sample loss or image reconstruction differences. The authors introduce a noise aggregation analysis method combined with a single-step, low-intensity noise injection strategy to amplify discriminative differences between member and non-member samples during the diffusion process. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach significantly reduces model query requirements while achieving higher inference accuracy and computational efficiency compared to prior methods.
鉴于扩散模型（如Stable Diffusion）广泛应用带来的隐私风险，本文针对现有成员推断攻击依赖样本损失或重建差异、效率低且准确率不足的问题，提出基于噪声聚合分析的方法，并引入单步低强度噪声注入策略，以放大成员与非成员样本间的差异，利用扩散过程中噪声的一致性特征。实验表明，该方法显著降低模型查询次数，同时提升推断准确率，优于现有方法。
Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees
Authors: Sourav Ganguly, Kartik Pandit, Arnob Ghosh
First: 2026-04-15T04:53:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:08:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied. Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model. We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在悲观对手下的乐观策略学习：兼顾遗憾与违反保证
现实世界决策系统运行于状态转移不仅依赖于智能体动作，还受\textbf{其无法控制的外生因素}——竞争性对手、环境干扰或战略对手——影响的环境中。形式上，$s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$，其中$\bar{a}_h$为对手/外生动作，$a_h$为智能体动作，$ω_h$为加性噪声。忽略此类因素可能导致在孤立环境下最优的策略，但在部署时\textbf{灾难性失败}，尤其当必须满足安全约束时。 标准约束MDP假设智能体是状态演化的唯一驱动者，这一假设在安全关键场景下失效。现有鲁棒强化学习方法通过在转移核上进行分布鲁棒性处理应对该问题，但未显式建模\textbf{智能体与外生因素之间的战略互动}，且依赖于对偏离已知基准模型的强假设。 我们将外生因素建模为\textbf{对抗性策略}$\barπ$，共同决定状态转移，并探讨智能体如何在对抗该对手时保持最优与安全。\emph{据我们所知，这是首次研究显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习}。我们提出\textbf{鲁棒幻觉约束上置信强化学习}（\texttt{RHC-UCRL}），一种基于模型的算法，对智能体与对手策略均保持乐观，显式分离认知不确定性与随机不确定性。\texttt{RHC-UCRL}实现次线性遗憾与约束违反保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and reliable decision-making in environments influenced by uncontrollable external factors such as adversaries or disturbances, this work introduces Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL (RHC-UCRL), a model-based algorithm that explicitly models adversarial state transitions. Unlike standard constrained MDPs that assume sole agent control, RHC-UCRL treats the external factor as an adversarial policy and maintains optimism over both agent and adversary actions, separating epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty. Experimental results demonstrate that RHC-UCRL achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees, ensuring both performance and safety even under strategic adversarial dynamics.
为应对受不可控外部因素（如对手或干扰）影响的环境中安全可靠的决策需求，本文提出鲁棒幻觉约束上置信强化学习（RHC-UCRL）算法，该算法显式建模对抗性状态转移并区分认知与随机不确定性。实验表明，RHC-UCRL在对抗性安全关键场景中实现了次线性遗憾与约束违反保证，优于现有基线方法。
Motion-Adapter: A Diffusion Model Adapter for Text-to-Motion Generation of Compound Actions
Authors: Yue Jiang, Mingyu Yang, Liuyuxin Yang, Yang Xu, Bingxin Yun, Yuhe Zhang
First: 2026-04-17T15:08:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:08:17+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 12 figures, Under review for publication in IEEE Transactions on Visualization and Computer Graphics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in generative motion synthesis have enabled the production of realistic human motions from diverse input modalities. However, synthesizing compound actions from texts, which integrate multiple concurrent actions into coherent full-body sequences, remains a major challenge. We identify two key limitations in current text-to-motion diffusion models: (i) catastrophic neglect, where earlier actions are overwritten by later ones due to improper handling of temporal information, and (ii) attention collapse, which arises from excessive feature fusion in cross-attention mechanisms. As a result, existing approaches often depend on overly detailed textual descriptions (e.g., raising right hand), explicit body-part specifications (e.g., editing the upper body), or the use of large language models (LLMs) for body-part interpretation. These strategies lead to deficient semantic representations of physical structures and kinematic mechanisms, limiting the ability to incorporate natural behaviors such as greeting while walking. To address these issues, we propose the Motion-Adapter, a plug-and-play module that guides text-to-motion diffusion models in generating compound actions by computing decoupled cross-attention maps, which serve as structural masks during the denoising process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently produces more faithful and coherent compound motions across diverse textual prompts, surpassing state-of-the-art approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Motion-Adapter：一种用于复合动作文本到运动生成的扩散模型适配器
近年来生成式运动合成技术的进步使得从多种输入模态生成逼真的人类运动成为可能。然而，从文本合成包含多个并发动作的复合动作，以生成连贯的全身运动序列，仍是一个重大挑战。我们发现当前文本到运动扩散模型存在两个关键局限：(i) 原始动作因时间信息处理不当而被后续动作覆盖，即灾难性遗忘；(ii) 交叉注意力机制中过度特征融合导致注意力崩溃。因此，现有方法往往依赖过于详细的文本描述（如“抬起右手”）、明确的身体部位指定（如“编辑上半身”）或使用大语言模型（LLM）进行身体部位解释。这些策略导致物理结构和运动机制的语义表示不足，限制了自然行为（如边走边打招呼）的融入能力。为解决这些问题，我们提出Motion-Adapter，一个即插即用模块，通过计算解耦的交叉注意力图，在去噪过程中作为结构掩码引导文本到运动扩散模型生成复合动作。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多种文本提示下持续生成更忠实、连贯的复合运动，超越现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating coherent compound human motions from text descriptions, this work addresses two key flaws in current diffusion models: catastrophic neglect of earlier actions and attention collapse due to over-fused cross-attention. The authors propose Motion-Adapter, a plug-and-play module that computes decoupled cross-attention maps to act as structural masks during denoising, preserving temporal integrity and spatial structure. Experimental results show that Motion-Adapter consistently produces more faithful and coherent compound motions across diverse prompts, outperforming state-of-the-art methods without requiring detailed body-part annotations or external language models.
为解决从文本生成连贯复合人体动作的难题，本文针对当前扩散模型存在的两大缺陷——早期动作被覆盖的灾难性遗忘和因过度融合导致的注意力崩溃——提出Motion-Adapter模块。该模块通过计算解耦的交叉注意力图，在去噪过程中作为结构掩码，保留时序完整性与空间结构。实验表明，该方法在多种文本提示下持续生成更忠实、连贯的复合动作，优于现有最先进方法，且无需依赖详细的人体部位标注或外部语言模型。
Can LLMs Understand the Impact of Trauma? Costs and Benefits of LLMs Coding the Interviews of Firearm Violence Survivors
Authors: Jessica H. Zhu, Shayla Stringfield, Vahe Zaprosyan, Michael Wagner, Michel Cukier, Joseph B. Richardson
Venue: Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics (2026) (Pending Publication)
First: 2026-04-17T15:07:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:07:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Firearm violence is a pressing public health issue, yet research into survivors' lived experiences remains underfunded and difficult to scale. Qualitative research, including in-depth interviews, is a valuable tool for understanding the personal and societal consequences of community firearm violence and designing effective interventions. However, manually analyzing these narratives through thematic analysis and inductive coding is time-consuming and labor-intensive. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have opened the door to automating this process, though concerns remain about whether these models can accurately and ethically capture the experiences of vulnerable populations. In this study, we assess the use of open-source LLMs to inductively code interviews with 21 Black men who have survived community firearm violence. Our results demonstrate that while some configurations of LLMs can identify important codes, overall relevance remains low and is highly sensitive to data processing. Furthermore, LLM guardrails lead to substantial narrative erasure. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of LLM-assisted qualitative coding and underscore the ethical challenges of applying AI in research involving marginalized communities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型能否理解创伤影响？大语言模型编码枪支暴力幸存者访谈的成本与收益

HumanLLM: Benchmarking and Improving LLM Anthropomorphism via Human Cognitive Patterns
Authors: Xintao Wang, Jian Yang, Weiyuan Li, Rui Xie, Jen-tse Huang, Jun Gao, Shuai Huang, Yueping Kang, Yuanli Gou, Hongwei Feng, Yanghua Xiao
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-15T08:56:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:05:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and generation, serving as the foundation for advanced persona simulation and Role-Playing Language Agents (RPLAs). However, achieving authentic alignment with human cognitive and behavioral patterns remains a critical challenge for these agents. We present HumanLLM, a framework treating psychological patterns as interacting causal forces. We construct 244 patterns from $\sim$12,000 academic papers and synthesize 11,359 scenarios where 2-5 patterns reinforce, conflict, or modulate each other, with multi-turn conversations expressing inner thoughts, actions, and dialogue. Our dual-level checklists evaluate both individual pattern fidelity and emergent multi-pattern dynamics, achieving strong human alignment ($r=0.90$) while revealing that holistic metrics conflate simulation accuracy with social desirability. HumanLLM-8B outperforms Qwen3-32B on multi-pattern dynamics despite 4$\times$ fewer parameters, demonstrating that authentic anthropomorphism requires cognitive modeling -- simulating not just what humans do, but the psychological processes generating those behaviors. Our dataset, code, and model are available at:https://github.com/YJGoodbye2024/HumanLLM
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanLLM：通过人类认知模式对大语言模型拟人化进行基准测试与改进
大语言模型（LLMs）在推理与生成方面展现出卓越能力，是高级人格模拟与角色扮演语言代理（RPLAs）的基础。然而，实现与人类认知与行为模式的真实对齐仍是这些代理的关键挑战。我们提出HumanLLM框架，将心理模式视为相互作用的因果力量。我们从约12,000篇学术论文中构建了244种模式，并合成11,359个场景，其中2-5种模式相互强化、冲突或调节，多轮对话表达内心思想、行动与对话。我们的双层检查清单评估单个模式的忠实度与多模式涌现动态，实现强人类对齐（r=0.90），同时揭示整体指标混淆了模拟准确性与社会可接受性。HumanLLM-8B参数量仅为Qwen3-32B的四分之一，但在多模式动态上表现更优，证明真实拟人化需认知建模——不仅模拟人类行为，更要模拟生成这些行为的心理过程。数据集、代码与模型见：https://github.com/YJGoodbye2024/HumanLLM

Motivated by the need to align large language models with authentic human cognition, the authors introduce HumanLLM, a framework that models psychological patterns as interacting causal forces.
