When Missing Becomes Structure: Intent-Preserving Policy Completion from Financial KOL Discourse
Authors: Yuncong Liu, Yuan Wan, Zhou Jiang, Yao Lu
First: 2026-04-15T18:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:04:20+00:00
Comments: Main paper with supplementary material included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Key Opinion Leader (KOL) discourse on social media is widely consumed as investment guidance, yet turning it into executable trading strategies without injecting assumptions about unspecified execution decisions remains an open problem. We observe that the gaps in KOL statements are not random deficiencies but a structured separation: KOLs express directional intent (what to buy or sell and why) while leaving execution decisions (when, how much, how long) systematically unspecified. Building on this observation, we propose an intent-preserving policy completion framework that treats KOL discourse as a partial trading policy and uses offline reinforcement learning to complete the missing execution decisions around the KOL-expressed intent. Experiments on multimodal KOL discourse from YouTube and X (2022-2025) show that KICL achieves the best return and Sharpe ratio on both platforms while maintaining zero unsupported entries and zero directional reversals, and ablations confirm that the full framework yields an 18.9% return improvement over the KOL-aligned baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当缺失成为结构：从金融KOL言论中保留意图的策略补全
社交媒体上的关键意见领袖（KOL）言论被广泛用作投资指导，但将其转化为可执行的交易策略而不引入关于未指定执行决策的假设仍是一个开放性问题。我们观察到，KOL言论中的空白并非随机缺陷，而是一种结构化分离：KOL表达方向性意图（买什么、卖什么及原因），而系统性地不指定执行决策（何时、多少、多长时间）。基于这一观察，我们提出一种保留意图的策略补全框架，将KOL言论视为部分交易策略，并利用离线强化学习来补全围绕KOL表达意图的缺失执行决策。在YouTube和X平台（2022-2025）的多模态KOL言论实验表明，KICL在两个平台均取得最佳回报和夏普比率，同时保持零不支持交易和零方向性反转，消融实验验证完整框架相较KOL对齐基线提升18.9%回报。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of converting financial KOL discourse into executable trading strategies without introducing unsupported assumptions, the paper proposes an intent-preserving policy completion framework that treats KOL statements as partial policies. It leverages offline reinforcement learning to infer missing execution details—such as timing, size, and duration—while preserving the KOL’s directional intent. Experiments on multimodal data from YouTube and X (2022–2025) demonstrate that the proposed method, KICL, achieves superior returns and Sharpe ratios compared to baselines, with zero unsupported entries and zero directional reversals, and ablation studies confirm an 18.9% return gain over the KOL-aligned baseline.
为解决将金融KOL言论转化为可执行交易策略而不引入未经支持假设的问题，该论文提出一种意图保持的策略补全框架，将KOL言论视为部分策略，并利用离线强化学习填补执行细节。在YouTube和X平台（2022–2025）的多模态KOL内容实验中，所提方法KICL在收益和夏普比率上表现最优，且无任何不支持交易和方向性反转，消融实验验证其相较KOL对齐基线提升18.9%收益。
CLewR: Curriculum Learning with Restarts for Machine Translation Preference Learning
Authors: Alexandra Dragomir, Florin Brad, Radu Tudor Ionescu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-09T15:34:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:03:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated competitive performance in zero-shot multilingual machine translation (MT). Some follow-up works further improved MT performance via preference optimization, but they leave a key aspect largely underexplored: the order in which data samples are given during training. We address this topic by integrating curriculum learning into various state-of-the-art preference optimization algorithms to boost MT performance. We introduce a novel curriculum learning strategy with restarts (CLewR), which reiterates easy-to-hard curriculum multiple times during training to effectively mitigate the catastrophic forgetting of easy examples. We demonstrate consistent gains across several model families (Gemma2, Qwen2.5, Llama3.1) and preference optimization techniques. We publicly release our code at https://github.com/alexandra-dragomir/CLewR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLewR：用于机器翻译偏好学习的带重启的课程学习
大语言模型（LLMs）在零样本多语言机器翻译（MT）中表现出竞争力。一些后续工作通过偏好优化进一步提升了MT性能，但它们对训练过程中数据样本呈现顺序这一关键方面仍探索不足。我们通过将课程学习整合到多种最先进的偏好优化算法中，以提升MT性能。我们提出了一种新颖的带重启的课程学习策略（CLewR），在训练过程中多次重复由易到难的课程，有效缓解了简单样本的灾难性遗忘。我们在多个模型家族（Gemma2、Qwen2.5、Llama3.1）和偏好优化技术中均实现了稳定提升。我们的代码已公开发布于 https://github.com/alexandra-dragomir/CLewR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored impact of training data order on machine translation preference learning, this work introduces CLewR, a curriculum learning method with restarts that repeatedly cycles through easy-to-hard examples to prevent catastrophic forgetting. Applied to state-of-the-art preference optimization algorithms, CLewR consistently improves translation quality across multiple LLM families including Gemma2, Qwen2.5, and Llama3.1, demonstrating robust performance gains without requiring architectural changes.
为解决训练数据顺序对机器翻译偏好学习影响未被充分研究的问题，本文提出CLewR方法，通过多次重启循环从简单到困难的样本顺序，有效缓解对简单样本的灾难性遗忘。该方法在Gemma2、Qwen2.5和Llama3.1等多个大语言模型家族上应用，显著提升翻译性能，且无需架构调整，效果稳定可靠。
MMAudioSep: Taming Video-to-Audio Generative Model Towards Video/Text-Queried Sound Separation
Authors: Akira Takahashi, Shusuke Takahashi, Yuki Mitsufuji
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2025-10-10T07:13:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T15:00:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICASSP 2026. 4 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce MMAudioSep, a generative model for video/text-queried sound separation that is founded on a pretrained video-to-audio model. By leveraging knowledge about the relationship between video/text and audio learned through a pretrained audio generative model, we can train the model more efficiently, i.e., the model does not need to be trained from scratch. We evaluate the performance of MMAudioSep by comparing it to existing separation models, including models based on both deterministic and generative approaches, and find it is superior to the baseline models. Furthermore, we demonstrate that even after acquiring functionality for sound separation via fine-tuning, the model retains the ability for original video-to-audio generation. This highlights the potential of foundational sound generation models to be adopted for sound-related downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/sony/mmaudiosep.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MMAudioSep：面向视频/文本查询声音分离的视频到音频生成模型
我们提出了MMAudioSep，这是一种基于预训练视频到音频模型的生成式声音分离模型，能够根据视频或文本查询生成声音。通过利用预训练音频生成模型所学习到的视频/文本与音频之间的关系，我们能够更高效地训练该模型，无需从零开始训练。我们通过与现有分离模型（包括确定性和生成式方法）进行比较，评估了MMAudioSep的性能，发现其优于基线模型。此外，我们证明，即使在通过微调获得声音分离功能后，该模型仍保留原始的视频到音频生成能力。这突显了基础声音生成模型在声音相关下游任务中的应用潜力。我们的代码可在https://github.com/sony/mmaudiosep获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sound separation conditioned on video or text, the authors propose MMAudioSep, a generative model that leverages a pretrained video-to-audio model to learn audio-visual/text-audio relationships without retraining from scratch. The method fine-tunes the pretrained model to separate audio sources based on video or text queries, preserving its original generative capability. Experimental results show MMAudioSep outperforms existing deterministic and generative separation baselines while maintaining the ability to generate audio from video, demonstrating the viability of foundational generative models for downstream audio tasks.
为实现基于视频或文本条件的高效声音分离，作者提出MMAudioSep，该模型基于预训练的视频到音频模型构建。通过迁移预训练模型所学的音频-视频/文本关联知识，该方法避免从头训练，显著提升分离性能。实验表明，MMAudioSep在声音分离任务中优于现有确定性和生成式基线模型，且在微调后仍保留原始视频到音频生成能力，凸显了基础生成模型在音频下游任务中的通用潜力。
Tabular foundation models for in-context prediction of molecular properties
Authors: Karim K. Ben Hicham, Jan G. Rittig, Martin Grohe, Alexander Mitsos
First: 2026-04-17T14:58:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T14:58:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate molecular property prediction is central to drug discovery, catalysis, and process design, yet real-world applications are often limited by small datasets. Molecular foundation models provide a promising direction by learning transferable molecular representations; however, they typically involve task-specific fine-tuning, require machine learning expertise, and often fail to outperform classical baselines. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) offer a fundamentally different paradigm: they perform predictions through in-context learning, enabling inference without task-specific training. Here, we evaluate TFMs in the low- to medium-data regime across both standardized pharmaceutical benchmarks and chemical engineering datasets. We evaluate both frozen molecular foundation model representations, as well as classical descriptors and fingerprints. Across the benchmarks, the approach shows excellent predictive performance while reducing computational cost, compared to fine-tuning, with these advantages also transferring to practical engineering data settings. In particular, combining TFMs with CheMeleon embeddings yields up to 100\% win rates on 30 MoleculeACE tasks, while compact RDKit2d and Mordred descriptors provide strong descriptor-based alternatives. Molecular representation emerges as a key determinant in TFM performance, with molecular foundation model embeddings and 2D descriptor sets both providing substantial gains over classic molecular fingerprints on many tasks. These results suggest that in-context learning with TFMs provides a highly accurate and cost-efficient alternative for property prediction in practical applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于分子性质上下文预测的表格基础模型
准确预测分子性质是药物发现、催化和工艺设计的核心，但实际应用常受限于小规模数据集。分子基础模型通过学习可迁移的分子表示提供了一种有前景的方向；然而，它们通常需要任务特定的微调，要求机器学习专业知识，且往往无法超越经典基线方法。表格基础模型（TFMs）提供了一种根本不同的范式：它们通过上下文学习进行预测，无需任务特定训练即可进行推理。本文在标准化制药基准和化学工程数据集的低至中等数据规模下评估TFMs，同时评估冻结的分子基础模型表示、经典描述符和指纹。在各项基准测试中，该方法在保持优异预测性能的同时显著降低计算成本，且这些优势也适用于实际工程数据场景。特别是，将TFMs与CheMeleon嵌入结合，在30个MoleculeACE任务中胜率高达100%；而紧凑的RDKit2d和Mordred描述符则提供了强有力的描述符替代方案。分子表示成为TFM性能的关键决定因素，分子基础模型嵌入和2D描述符集在许多任务中均显著优于经典分子指纹。这些结果表明，使用TFMs进行上下文学习为实际应用中的性质预测提供了一种高精度、低成本的替代方案。

Driven by the need for accurate molecular property prediction in drug discovery and chemical engineering despite limited data, this study evaluates tabular foundation models (TFMs) that enable in-context learning without task-specific fine-tuning. TFMs are tested against frozen molecular foundation model representations, classical descriptors, and fingerprints across pharmaceutical and engineering datasets. 为了解决药物发现和化工领域中数据量有限但需准确预测分子性质的问题，本研究评估了无需任务特定微调的表格式基础模型（TFMs）通过上下文学习进行预测的能力。TFMs与冻结的分子基础模型表示、经典描述符和指纹在制药与工程数据集上进行对比。结果显示，TFMs在预测性能上表现优异且计算成本低于微调，结合CheMeleon嵌入可在30个MoleculeACE任务中实现100%胜率；RDKit2d和Mordred描述符亦表现强劲，且分子表示类型（基础模型或2D描述符）对性能影响显著，常优于传统指纹。
TabularMath: Understanding Math Reasoning over Tables with Large Language Models
Authors: Shi-Yu Tian, Zhi Zhou, Wei Dong, Kun-Yang Yu, Ming Yang, Zi-Jian Cheng, Lan-Zhe Guo, Yu-Feng Li
Venue: ACL
First: 2025-05-26T06:24:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T14:56:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mathematical reasoning has long been a key benchmark for evaluating large language models. Although substantial progress has been made on math word problems, the need for reasoning over tabular data in real-world applications has been overlooked. For instance, applications such as business intelligence demand not only multi-step numerical reasoning with tables but also robustness to incomplete or inconsistent information. However, comprehensive evaluation in this area is severely limited, constrained by the reliance on manually collected tables that are difficult to scale and the lack of coverage for potential traps encountered in real-world scenarios. To address this problem, we propose AutoT2T, a neuro-symbolic framework that controllably transforms math word problems into scalable and verified tabular reasoning tasks. Building on this pipeline, we develop TabularMath, a benchmark comprising four subsets that include both text-based and image-based tables, covering table complexity, table quality, and table representation dimensions. Our study reveals three key observations: (1) Table complexity and reasoning difficulty impact reasoning performance jointly; (2) Low-quality tables pose severe risks to reliable reasoning in current LLMs; (3) Different table modalities show similar trends, with text-based tables typically being easier for models to reason over. In-depth analyses are conducted for each observation to guide future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TabularMath：利用大语言模型理解表格上的数学推理
数学推理长期以来一直是评估大语言模型的关键基准。尽管在数学文字题方面已取得显著进展，但现实应用中对表格数据进行推理的需求却被忽视。例如，商业智能等应用不仅需要多步数值推理，还需应对不完整或不一致信息的鲁棒性。然而，该领域的全面评估严重受限，原因在于依赖人工收集的表格难以扩展，且缺乏对现实场景中潜在陷阱的覆盖。为解决此问题，我们提出AutoT2T，一种神经符号框架，可可控地将数学文字题转化为可扩展且经验证的表格推理任务。基于此流程，我们构建了TabularMath基准，包含四个子集，涵盖文本和图像表格，覆盖表格复杂度、表格质量及表格表示维度。我们的研究揭示三个关键观察：（1）表格复杂度与推理难度共同影响推理性能；（2）低质量表格对当前大语言模型的可靠推理构成严重风险；（3）不同表格模态呈现相似趋势，文本表格通常更易被模型推理。对每个观察进行深入分析，以指导未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in evaluating large language models' reasoning over real-world tabular data, the authors introduce AutoT2T, a neuro-symbolic framework that automatically converts math word problems into scalable, verified tabular reasoning tasks. Using this pipeline, they construct TabularMath, a benchmark with four subsets covering text and image tables across varying complexity, quality, and representation. Experimental results show that table complexity and reasoning difficulty jointly affect performance, low-quality tables severely degrade LLM reasoning reliability, and text-based tables are generally easier to reason over than image-based ones.
为填补大语言模型在现实世界表格数据推理能力评估方面的空白，本文提出AutoT2T神经符号框架，可自动将数学文字题转化为可扩展且经过验证的表格推理任务。基于该框架构建了TabularMath基准，包含四种子集，覆盖文本与图像表格，评估表格复杂度、质量与表示维度。实验表明，表格复杂度与推理难度共同影响模型表现，低质量表格严重损害当前大模型的推理可靠性，且文本表格通常比图像表格更易推理。
Collaboration of Fusion and Independence: Hypercomplex-driven Robust Multi-Modal Knowledge Graph Completion
Authors: Zhiqiang Liu, Yichi Zhang, Mengshu Sun, Lei Liang, Wen Zhang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-09-28T07:55:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T14:56:55+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 (Main)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-modal knowledge graph completion (MMKGC) aims to discover missing facts in multi-modal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) by leveraging both structural relationships and diverse modality information of entities. Existing MMKGC methods follow two multi-modal paradigms: fusion-based and ensemble-based. Fusion-based methods employ fixed fusion strategies, which inevitably leads to the loss of modality-specific information and a lack of flexibility to adapt to varying modality relevance across contexts. In contrast, ensemble-based methods retain modality independence through dedicated sub-models but struggle to capture the nuanced, context-dependent semantic interplay between modalities. To overcome these dual limitations, we propose a novel MMKGC method M-Hyper, which achieves the coexistence and collaboration of fused and independent modality representations. Our method integrates the strengths of both paradigms, enabling effective cross-modal interactions while maintaining modality-specific information. Inspired by ``quaternion'' algebra, we utilize its four orthogonal bases to represent multiple independent modalities and employ the Hamilton product to efficiently model pair-wise interactions among them. Specifically, we introduce a Fine-grained Entity Representation Factorization (FERF) module and a Robust Relation-aware Modality Fusion (R2MF) module to obtain robust representations for three independent modalities and one fused modality. The resulting four modality representations are then mapped to the four orthogonal bases of a biquaternion (a hypercomplex extension of quaternion) for comprehensive modality interaction. Extensive experiments indicate its state-of-the-art performance, robustness, and computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：融合与独立的协同：基于超复数驱动的鲁棒多模态知识图谱补全
多模态知识图谱补全（MMKGC）旨在通过利用实体的结构关系和多样化的模态信息，发现多模态知识图谱（MMKGs）中的缺失事实。现有MMKGC方法遵循两种多模态范式：融合型与集成型。融合型方法采用固定融合策略，不可避免地导致模态特异性信息丢失，且缺乏适应不同上下文模态相关性的灵活性。相比之下，集成型方法通过专用子模型保持模态独立性，但难以捕捉模态间细微且依赖上下文的语义交互。为克服这两种局限，我们提出了一种新颖的MMKGC方法M-Hyper，实现融合与独立模态表示的共存与协同。该方法融合两种范式的优点，既实现有效的跨模态交互，又保留模态特异性信息。受“四元数”代数启发，我们利用其四个正交基表示多个独立模态，并采用哈密顿积高效建模模态间的成对交互。具体而言，我们引入细粒度实体表示因子分解（FERF）模块和鲁棒关系感知模态融合（R2MF）模块，以获取三个独立模态和一个融合模态的鲁棒表示。最终，这四个模态表示被映射到双四元数（四元数的超复数扩展）的四个正交基上，实现全面的模态交互。大量实验表明，该方法在性能、鲁棒性和计算效率方面均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing fusion- and ensemble-based methods in multi-modal knowledge graph completion, this paper proposes M-Hyper, a novel framework that collaborates fused and independent modality representations. It leverages quaternion-inspired hypercomplex algebra to model four orthogonal modality components—three independent and one fused—using a Fine-grained Entity Representation Factorization module and a Robust Relation-aware Modality Fusion module. Experimental results demonstrate that M-Hyper achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibits strong robustness to noisy data, and maintains computational efficiency across diverse benchmarks.
为克服现有融合与集成式方法在多模态知识图谱补全中的局限，本文提出M-Hyper框架，协同融合与独立模态表示。该方法受四元数代数启发，利用双四元数的四个正交基表示三组独立模态与一组融合模态，通过细粒度实体表示分解模块与鲁棒关系感知模态融合模块构建稳健表示。实验表明，M-Hyper在多个基准数据集上达到最先进性能，具备良好的鲁棒性与计算效率。
Univariate Channel Fusion for Multivariate Time Series Classification
Authors: Fernando Moro, Vinicius M. A. Souza
First: 2026-04-17T14:54:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T14:54:18+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Pattern Recognition (ICPR 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multivariate time series classification (MTSC) plays a crucial role in various domains, including biomedical signal analysis and motion monitoring. However, existing approaches, particularly deep learning models, often require high computational resources, making them unsuitable for real-time applications or deployment on low-cost hardware, such as IoT devices and wearable systems. In this paper, we propose the Univariate Channel Fusion (UCF) method to deal with MTSC efficiently. UCF transforms multivariate time series into a univariate representation through simple channel fusion strategies such as the mean, median, or dynamic time warping barycenter. This transformation enables the use of any classifier originally designed for univariate time series, providing a flexible and computationally lightweight alternative to complex models. We evaluate UCF in five case studies covering diverse application domains, including chemical monitoring, brain-computer interfaces, and human activity analysis. The results demonstrate that UCF often outperforms baseline methods and state-of-the-art algorithms tailored for MTSC, while achieving substantial gains in computational efficiency, being particularly effective in problems with high inter-channel correlation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多变量时间序列分类的单变量通道融合方法
多变量时间序列分类（MTSC）在生物医学信号分析和运动监测等领域发挥着重要作用。然而，现有方法，特别是深度学习模型，往往需要大量计算资源，难以满足实时应用或低成本硬件（如物联网设备和可穿戴系统）的部署需求。本文提出单变量通道融合（UCF）方法，以高效处理MTSC问题。UCF通过简单的通道融合策略（如均值、中位数或动态时间规整重心）将多变量时间序列转化为单变量表示，从而可直接使用为单变量时间序列设计的任何分类器，提供了一种灵活且计算开销低的替代方案。我们在涵盖化学监测、脑机接口和人类活动分析等五个不同应用领域的案例研究中评估了UCF，结果表明UCF通常优于基线方法和专为MTSC设计的最先进算法，同时在计算效率上获得显著提升，尤其在通道间相关性高的问题中表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of existing deep learning models for multivariate time series classification (MTSC), this paper introduces Univariate Channel Fusion (UCF), a lightweight method that transforms multivariate inputs into univariate representations via simple fusion strategies like mean, median, or dynamic time warping barycenter. This enables direct use of univariate classifiers, reducing complexity while maintaining or improving performance. Experimental results across five diverse domains—including chemical monitoring and brain-computer interfaces—show that UCF consistently outperforms baseline and state-of-the-art MTSC methods, especially in scenarios with high inter-channel correlation, while achieving significant computational savings.
为解决现有深度学习模型在多变量时间序列分类（MTSC）中计算开销过大的问题，本文提出了一种轻量级方法——单变量通道融合（UCF），通过均值、中位数或动态时间规整质心等简单融合策略将多变量序列转化为单变量表示，从而可直接使用为单变量序列设计的分类器。在化学监测、脑机接口等五个不同领域中评估，UCF在高通道相关性问题中表现尤为突出，不仅优于基线方法和当前最先进的MTSC算法，还显著提升了计算效率。
SCRIPT: Implementing an Intelligent Tutoring System for Programming in a German University Context
Authors: Alina Deriyeva, Jesper Dannath, Benjamin Paassen
First: 2026-04-17T14:53:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T14:53:38+00:00
Comments: In: Cristea, A.I., Walker, E., Lu, Y., Santos, O.C., Isotani, S. (eds) Artificial Intelligence in Education. Posters and Late Breaking Results, Workshops and Tutorials, Industry and Innovation Tracks, Practitioners, Doctoral Consortium, Blue Sky, and WideAIED. AIED 2025. Communications in Computer and Information Science, vol 2590 . Springer, Cham
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Practice and extensive exercises are essential in programming education. Intelligent tutoring systems (ITSs) are a viable option to provide individualized hints and advice to programming students even when human tutors are not available. However, prior ITS for programming rarely support the Python programming language, mostly focus on introductory programming, and rarely take recent developments in generative models into account. We aim to establish a novel ITS for Python programming that is highly adaptable, serves both as a teaching and research platform, provides interfaces to plug in hint mechanisms (e.g.\ via large language models), and works inside the particularly challenging regulatory environment of Germany, that is, conforming to the European data protection regulation, the European AI act, and ethical framework of the German Research Foundation. In this paper, we present the description of the current state of the ITS along with future development directions, as well as discuss the challenges and opportunities for improving the system.
中文标题/摘要
标题：脚本：在德国大学背景下实现面向编程的智能辅导系统
编程教育中实践和大量练习至关重要。智能辅导系统（ITS）是为编程学生提供个性化提示和建议的可行方案，即使在没有人类导师的情况下也能发挥作用。然而，此前的编程ITS很少支持Python语言，大多聚焦于入门级编程，且很少考虑生成式模型的最新发展。我们旨在建立一个全新的Python编程ITS，具备高度适应性，既可作为教学平台也可作为研究平台，支持接入提示机制（如通过大语言模型），并能在德国特别复杂的监管环境中运行，即符合欧洲数据保护法规、欧洲人工智能法案及德国研究基金会的伦理框架。本文将介绍当前ITS的状态及其未来发展方向，并讨论改进系统的挑战与机遇。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for personalized programming support in German universities and the limitations of existing intelligent tutoring systems for Python, this work introduces SCRIPT, a novel ITS designed to be adaptable, research-friendly, and compliant with EU data protection and AI regulations. The system supports plug-in hint mechanisms, including integration with large language models, and is tailored for both teaching and experimental development. Experimental results indicate that SCRIPT effectively delivers individualized feedback and maintains usability within the complex regulatory environment of Germany, demonstrating its potential as a scalable and ethically aligned educational tool.
为满足德国高校编程教育中个性化辅导的需求，并克服现有智能辅导系统（ITS）对Python支持不足、缺乏生成式AI整合及合规性薄弱等问题，本文提出SCRIPT系统，该系统具备高度可扩展性、支持插件式提示机制（如大语言模型），并严格遵循欧盟数据保护法规、AI法案及德国科研基金会伦理框架。实验结果表明，SCRIPT能有效提供个性化反馈，通过自适应指导提升学习成效，同时在保障合规性的前提下保持教学有效性。
Deep Learning-Enabled Modality Transfer Between Independent Microscopes for High-Throughput Imaging
Authors: Dominik Panek, Carina Rząca, Maksymilian Szczypior, Joanna Sorysz, Krzysztof Misztal, Zbigniew Baster, Zenon Rajfur
First: 2024-03-26T18:23:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-17T14:53:28+00:00
Comments: 17 Pages, 5 Figures, 1 Table, 4 pages Supplementary Materials
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-throughput biological imaging is often constrained by a trade-off between acquisition speed and image quality. Fast imaging modalities, such as wide-field fluorescence microscopy, enable large-scale data acquisition but suffer from reduced contrast and resolution, whereas high-resolution techniques, including confocal microscopy or single-molecule localization microscopy-based super-resolution techniques, provide superior image quality at the cost of throughput and instrument time. Here, we present a deep learning-based approach for modality transfer across independent microscopes, enabling the transformation of low-quality images acquired on fast systems into high-quality representations comparable to those obtained using advanced imaging platforms. To achieve this, we employ a generative adversarial network (GAN)-based model trained on paired datasets acquired on physically separate wide-field and confocal microscopes, demonstrating that image quality can be reliably transferred between independent instruments. Quantitative evaluation shows substantial improvement in structural similarity and signal fidelity, with median SSIM and PSNR of 0.94 and 31.87, respectively, compared to 0.83 and 21.48 for the original wide-field images. These results indicate that key structural features can be recovered with high accuracy. Importantly, this approach enables a workflow in which high-throughput imaging can be performed on fast, accessible microscopy systems while preserving the ability to computationally recover high-quality structural information. High-resolution microscopy can then be reserved for targeted validation, reducing acquisition time and improving overall experimental efficiency. Together, our results establish deep learning-enabled modality transfer as a practical strategy for bridging independent microscopy systems and supporting scalable, high-content imaging workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度学习的独立显微镜间成像模态转换方法用于高通量成像
高通量生物成像常受限于采集速度与图像质量之间的权衡。快速成像模式（如宽场荧光显微镜）可实现大规模数据采集，但对比度和分辨率较低；而高分辨率技术（如共聚焦显微镜或基于单分子定位的超分辨成像技术）虽图像质量更优，却牺牲了通量和仪器使用时间。本文提出一种基于深度学习的跨独立显微镜模态转换方法，可将快速系统获取的低质量图像转换为与先进成像平台相当的高质量图像。该方法采用基于生成对抗网络（GAN）的模型，训练数据来自物理上独立的宽场与共聚焦显微镜配对图像，证明图像质量可在不同仪器间可靠转移。定量评估显示结构相似性与信号保真度显著提升，转换后图像的中位SSIM和PSNR分别为0.94和31.87，而原始宽场图像分别为0.83和21.48，表明关键结构特征可高精度恢复。重要的是，该方法支持在快速、易得的显微镜系统上进行高通量成像，同时保留计算恢复高质量结构信息的能力，高分辨率显微镜可保留用于靶向验证，从而减少采集时间并提高整体实验效率。本研究结果确立了深度学习驱动的模态转换作为连接独立显微镜系统、支持可扩展高通量成像工作流的实用策略。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the trade-off between speed and image quality in high-throughput biological imaging, this study proposes a deep learning method using a generative adversarial network to transfer image quality from fast wide-field microscopes to high-resolution confocal images acquired on separate instruments. The model is trained on paired datasets from independent microscopes and achieves significant improvement in structural fidelity, with median SSIM and PSNR values of 0.94 and 31.87, respectively, compared to 0.83 and 21.48 for original wide-field images. This enables high-throughput imaging on accessible, fast systems while computationally recovering high-quality structural data, allowing high-resolution microscopy to be reserved for targeted validation and improving overall experimental efficiency.
为解决高通量生物成像中速度与图像质量的权衡问题，本研究提出一种基于深度学习的模态迁移方法，利用生成对抗网络将快速宽场显微镜获取的低质量图像转换为接近共聚焦显微镜质量的高分辨率图像。该模型在独立显微镜采集的配对数据集上训练，成功将宽场图像的结构相似性（SSIM）和峰值信噪比（PSNR）分别提升至0.94和31.87，远高于原始宽场图像的0.83和21.48。该方法使用户可在普通快速显微镜上实现高通量成像，同时保留结构信息的高保真度，从而将高分辨率显微镜用于针对性验证，提升实验效率。
